COMMANDS EXECUTED:

  $ const fs = require('fs')
  $ const inputPath =
  $ 'SOURCEFASTCHANNELS.txt'
  $ const outputPath =
  $ 'SOURCEFASTCHANNELS2.txt'
  $ const input =
  $ fs.readFileSync(
  $ inputPath,
  $ 'utf8'
  $ )
  $ const lines =
  $ input.split(
  $ /\r?\n/
  $ )
  $ const uniqueChannels =
  $ new Set()
  $ let channelsRead =
  $ 0
  $ let channelsKept =
  $ 0
  $ let localChannelsRemoved =
  $ 0
  $ let duplicateChannelsRemoved =
  $ 0
  $ let invalidLinesSkipped =
  $ 0
  $ const localCallSignPattern =
  $ /\((?:W|K)[A-Z]{3}\)\.us$/i
  $ for (
  $ const line of lines
  $ ) {
  $ const trimmed =
  $ line.trim()
  $ if (
  $ !trimmed
  $ ) {
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ trimmed ===
  $ 'SOURCE FAST CHANNELS'
  $ ) {
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ trimmed ===
  $ 'SOURCE FAST CHANNELS CLEANED'
  $ ) {
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ trimmed.startsWith(
  $ 'Generated:'
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ trimmed.startsWith(
  $ 'Total channels:'
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ !trimmed.includes(
  $ ' | '
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ invalidLinesSkipped++
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ const parts =
  $ trimmed.split(
  $ ' | '
  $ )
  $ const displayName =
  $ parts[0]
  $ ? parts[0].trim()
  $ : ''
  $ const channelId =
  $ parts[1]
  $ ? parts[1].trim()
  $ : ''
  $ if (
  $ !displayName ||
  $ !channelId
  $ ) {
  $ invalidLinesSkipped++
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ channelsRead++
  $ if (
  $ !channelId.endsWith(
  $ '.us'
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ invalidLinesSkipped++
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ localCallSignPattern.test(
  $ channelId
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ localChannelsRemoved++
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ const outputLine =
  $ displayName +
  $ ' | ' +
  $ channelId
  $ if (
  $ uniqueChannels.has(
  $ outputLine
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ duplicateChannelsRemoved++
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ uniqueChannels.add(
  $ outputLine
  $ )
  $ channelsKept++
  $ }
  $ const sortedChannels =
  $ Array.from(
  $ uniqueChannels
  $ ).sort(
  $ (
  $ a,
  $ b
  $ ) =>
  $ a.localeCompare(
  $ b,
  $ undefined,
  $ {
  $ sensitivity: 'base'
  $ }
  $ )
  $ )
  $ const header = [
  $ 'SOURCE FAST CHANNELS CLEANED',
  $ 'Generated: ' +
  $ new Date().toISOString(),
  $ 'Total channels: ' +
  $ sortedChannels.length,
  $ ''
  $ ]
  $ fs.writeFileSync(
  $ outputPath,
  $ header
  $ .concat(
  $ sortedChannels
  $ )
  $ .join(
  $ '\n'
  $ ) +
  $ '\n',
  $ 'utf8'
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'SOURCEFASTCHANNELS cleanup complete'
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Channels read: ' +
  $ channelsRead
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Channels kept: ' +
  $ channelsKept
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Local channels removed: ' +
  $ localChannelsRemoved
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Exact duplicates removed: ' +
  $ duplicateChannelsRemoved
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Invalid lines skipped: ' +
  $ invalidLinesSkipped
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Output: ' +
  $ outputPath
  $ )
  $ EOF
  $ test -s SOURCEFASTCHANNELS2.txt
  $ CHANNEL_COUNT=$(grep -c ' | ' SOURCEFASTCHANNELS2.txt || true)

  $ echo "No cleaned channels were written"

  $ echo "No SOURCEFASTCHANNELS2 changes to commit"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ git commit -m "Update SOURCEFASTCHANNELS2"
  $ for attempt in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  $ echo "Push attempt $attempt"
  $ if git push origin HEAD:main; then
  $ echo "Push succeeded"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ echo "Push failed. Fetching latest remote changes..."
  $ git fetch origin main
  $ if git rebase origin/main; then
  $ echo "Rebase succeeded"
  $ else
  $ echo "Rebase failed. Aborting rebase."
  $ git rebase --abort || true
  $ fi
  $ echo "Waiting before retry..."
  $ sleep 10
  $ done
  $ echo "Push failed after all retries"
  $ exit 1

PRIME Cross Check
-----------------
Workflow: .github/workflows/CROSSCHECKFASTCHANNELS.yml

COMMANDS EXECUTED:
  $ node <<'EOF'
  $ const fs = require('fs')
  $ const providerPath = 'fastchannels.txt'
  $ const jessmanPath = 'SOURCEFASTCHANNELS2.txt'
  $ const outputPath = 'PRIMECROSSCHECK.txt'
  $ const providerText = fs.readFileSync(providerPath, 'utf8')
  $ const jessmanText = fs.readFileSync(jessmanPath, 'utf8')
  $ function normalize(value) {
  $ return String(value || '')
  $ .normalize('NFKD')
  $ .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
  $ .toUpperCase()
  $ .replace(/ᴿᴬᵂ/g, '')
  $ .replace(/\[FAST\]/gi, '')
  $ .replace(/\bFAST\b/gi, '')
  $ .replace(/\bPRIME\b/gi, '')
  $ .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g, '')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function cleanProviderName(name) {
  $ return String(name || '')
  $ .replace(/^PRIME\s*:\s*/i, '')
  $ .replace(/ᴿᴬᵂ/g, '')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function cleanJessmanName(name) {
  $ return String(name || '')
  $ .replace(/\s*\[FAST\]\s*/gi, ' ')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ /*
  $ * Read provider JSON objects.
  $ */
  $ const providerChannels = []
  $ const providerMatches = providerText.matchAll(
  $ /\{[\s\S]*?\}/g
  $ )
  $ for (const match of providerMatches) {
  $ try {
  $ const channel = JSON.parse(match[0])
  $ if (
  $ channel &&
  $ typeof channel.name === 'string' &&
  $ channel.name.trim().toUpperCase().startsWith('PRIME:')
  $ ) {
  $ const providerName = cleanProviderName(channel.name)
  $ const normalizedName = normalize(providerName)
  $ if (normalizedName) {
  $ providerChannels.push({
  $ num: channel.num || '',
  $ name: channel.name,
  $ stream_id: channel.stream_id || '',
  $ normalizedName
  $ })
  $ }
  $ }
  $ } catch {
  $ // Ignore malformed JSON objects
  $ }
  $ }
  $ /*
  $ * Read cleaned Jessman channels.
  $ *
  $ * Expected format:
  $ *
  $ * Channel Name | ChannelIdentifier.us
  $ */
  $ const jessmanChannels = []
  $ for (const rawLine of jessmanText.split(/\r?\n/)) {
  $ const line = rawLine.trim()
  $ if (!line) continue
  $ if (line.startsWith('SOURCE FAST CHANNELS')) continue
  $ if (line.startsWith('Generated:')) continue
  $ if (line.startsWith('Total channels:')) continue
  $ const parts = line.split('|').map(x => x.trim())
  $ if (parts.length < 2) continue
  $ const displayName = cleanJessmanName(parts[0])
  $ const channelId = parts[1]
  $ if (!displayName || !channelId) continue
  $ if (!channelId.endsWith('.us')) continue
  $ jessmanChannels.push({
  $ displayName,
  $ channelId,
  $ normalizedName: normalize(displayName)
  $ })
  $ }
  $ /*
  $ * Build lookup by normalized Jessman name.
  $ *
  $ * This intentionally allows multiple Jessman channels
  $ * with the same name.
  $ */
  $ const jessmanLookup = new Map()
  $ for (const channel of jessmanChannels) {
  $ if (!jessmanLookup.has(channel.normalizedName)) {
  $ jessmanLookup.set(channel.normalizedName, [])
  $ }
  $ jessmanLookup
  $ .get(channel.normalizedName)
  $ .push(channel)
  $ }
  $ const output = []
  $ output.push('PRIME CROSS CHECK')
  $ output.push(`Generated: ${new Date().toISOString()}`)
  $ output.push(`PRIME provider channels checked: ${providerChannels.length}`)
  $ output.push('')
  $ let matchCount = 0
  $ let unmatchedCount = 0
  $ for (const provider of providerChannels) {
  $ const matches =
  $ jessmanLookup.get(provider.normalizedName) || []
  $ if (matches.length === 0) {
  $ unmatchedCount++
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ for (const match of matches) {
  $ matchCount++
  $ output.push(
  $ [
  $ provider.num,
  $ provider.name,
  $ cleanProviderName(provider.name),
  $ match.displayName,
  $ match.channelId,
  $ `STREAM_ID: ${provider.stream_id}`,
  $ 'MATCH: EXACT'
  $ ].join(' | ')
  $ )
  $ }
  $ }
  $ output.splice(
  $ 3,
  $ 0,
  $ `Total matches written: ${matchCount}`,
  $ `Unmatched PRIME channels: ${unmatchedCount}`
  $ )
  $ fs.writeFileSync(
  $ outputPath,
  $ output.join('\n') + '\n',
  $ 'utf8'
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ `PRIME provider channels checked: ${providerChannels.length}`
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ `Total PRIME matches written: ${matchCount}`
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ `Unmatched PRIME channels: ${unmatchedCount}`
  $ )
  $ if (matchCount === 0) {
  $ console.error('No PRIME matches were found')
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ EOF
  $ test -s PRIMECROSSCHECK.txt
  $ MATCH_COUNT=$(grep -c 'MATCH: EXACT' PRIMECROSSCHECK.txt || true)
  $ echo "Total matches written: $MATCH_COUNT"
  $ if [ "$MATCH_COUNT" -lt 1 ]; then
  $ echo "No PRIME matches were found"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ echo "First 50 PRIME matches:"
  $ grep 'MATCH: EXACT' PRIMECROSSCHECK.txt | head -n 50
  $ echo ""
  $ echo "File size:"
  $ ls -lh PRIMECROSSCHECK.txt
  $ git config user.name "github-actions"
  $ git config user.email "github-actions@github.com"
  $ git fetch origin main
  $ git reset --hard origin/main
  $ git add PRIMECROSSCHECK.txt
  $ if git diff --cached --quiet; then
  $ echo "No PRIMECROSSCHECK changes to commit"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ git commit -m "Update PRIMECROSSCHECK"
  $ for attempt in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  $ echo "Push attempt $attempt"
  $ git fetch origin main
  $ git reset --hard origin/main
  $ git add PRIMECROSSCHECK.txt
  $ if git diff --cached --quiet; then
  $ echo "PRIMECROSSCHECK already up to date"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ git commit -m "Update PRIMECROSSCHECK" || true
  $ if git push origin HEAD:main; then
  $ echo "Push succeeded"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ echo "Push failed. Waiting before retry..."
  $ sleep 10
  $ done
  $ echo "Push failed after all retries"
  $ exit 1

COMMANDS EXECUTED:

  $ mkdir -p guides
  $ curl -L --fail --retry 5 --retry-delay 10 \
  $ https://epg.guru/7daygracenote/USFast.xml \
  $ -o fast/source.xml
  $ test -s fast/source.xml
  $ echo "FAST source downloaded successfully"
  $ ls -lh fast/source.xml
  $ cat > /tmp/process_fast.js <<'EOF'
  $ const fs = require('fs')
  $ const readline = require('readline')
  $ const inputPath = 'fast/source.xml'
  $ const outputPath = 'fast/processed.xml'
  $ const now = new Date()
  $ const cutoff = new Date(
  $ now.getTime() +
  $ 3 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
  $ )
  $ let programmesScanned = 0
  $ let programmesKept = 0
  $ let programmesSkipped = 0
  $ let descriptionsChanged = 0
  $ let actorsRemoved = 0
  $ let episodeNumbersConverted = 0
  $ let originalAirDatesUsed = 0
  $ function decodeXml(value) {
  $ return String(value || '')
  $ .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
  $ .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
  $ .replace(/&apos;/g, "'")
  $ .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
  $ .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
  $ }
  $ function escapeXml(value) {
  $ return String(value || '')
  $ .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
  $ .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
  $ .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
  $ .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
  $ .replace(/'/g, '&apos;')
  $ }
  $ function cleanSpaces(value) {
  $ return String(value || '')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function getTag(block, tag) {
  $ const regex = new RegExp(
  $ '<' +
  $ tag +
  $ '\\b[^>]*>([\\s\\S]*?)</' +
  $ tag +
  $ '>',
  $ 'i'
  $ )
  $ const match = block.match(regex)
  $ if (!match) {
  $ return ''
  $ }
  $ return decodeXml(
  $ match[1]
  $ .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '')
  $ .trim()
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function getTagBySystem(block, tag, system) {
  $ const regex = new RegExp(
  $ '<' +
  $ tag +
  $ '\\b[^>]*\\bsystem="' +
  $ system +
  $ '"[^>]*>([\\s\\S]*?)</' +
  $ tag +
  $ '>',
  $ 'i'
  $ )
  $ const match = block.match(regex)
  $ if (!match) {
  $ return ''
  $ }
  $ return decodeXml(
  $ match[1]
  $ .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '')
  $ .trim()
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function getStart(block) {
  $ const match = block.match(
  $ /<programme\b[^>]*\bstart="([^"]+)"/i
  $ )
  $ return match
  $ ? match[1]
  $ : ''
  $ }
  $ function parseXmltvDate(value) {
  $ const match = String(value || '').match(
  $ /^(\d{4})(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})/
  $ )
  $ if (!match) {
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ return new Date(
  $ Date.UTC(
  $ Number(match[1]),
  $ Number(match[2]) - 1,
  $ Number(match[3]),
  $ Number(match[4]),
  $ Number(match[5]),
  $ Number(match[6])
  $ )
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function parseOriginalAirDate(value) {
  $ const match = String(value || '').match(
  $ /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/
  $ )
  $ if (!match) {
  $ return ''
  $ }
  $ return (
  $ match[2] +
  $ '/' +
  $ match[3] +
  $ '/' +
  $ match[1]
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function replaceTag(block, tag, value) {
  $ const regex = new RegExp(
  $ '<' +
  $ tag +
  $ '\\b[^>]*>[\\s\\S]*?</' +
  $ tag +
  $ '>',
  $ 'i'
  $ )
  $ const replacement =
  $ '<' +
  $ tag +
  $ ' lang="en">' +
  $ escapeXml(value) +
  $ '</' +
  $ tag +
  $ '>'
  $ if (regex.test(block)) {
  $ return block.replace(
  $ regex,
  $ replacement
  $ )
  $ }
  $ return block.replace(
  $ /<\/programme>\s*$/i,
  $ '  ' +
  $ replacement +
  $ '\n</programme>'
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function removeTag(block, tag) {
  $ const regex = new RegExp(
  $ '\\s*<' +
  $ tag +
  $ '\\b[^>]*>[\\s\\S]*?</' +
  $ tag +
  $ '>',
  $ 'gi'
  $ )
  $ return block.replace(
  $ regex,
  $ ''
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function getEpisodeNumber(block) {
  $ let episodeNumber =
  $ getTagBySystem(
  $ block,
  $ 'episode-num',
  $ 'common'
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !episodeNumber
  $ ) {
  $ episodeNumber =
  $ getTagBySystem(
  $ block,
  $ 'episode-num',
  $ 'onscreen'
  $ )
  $ }
  $ const match =
  $ episodeNumber.match(
  $ /^S\s*(\d{1,3})\s*E\s*(\d{1,3})$/i
  $ )
  $ if (!match) {
  $ return ''
  $ }
  $ return (
  $ 'S' +
  $ Number(match[1]) +
  $ 'E' +
  $ Number(match[2])
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function processProgramme(block) {
  $ programmesScanned++
  $ const start =
  $ getStart(
  $ block
  $ )
  $ const startDate =
  $ parseXmltvDate(
  $ start
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !startDate ||
  $ startDate < now ||
  $ startDate >= cutoff
  $ ) {
  $ programmesSkipped++
  $ return ''
  $ }
  $ programmesKept++
  $ const originalTitle =
  $ cleanSpaces(
  $ getTag(
  $ block,
  $ 'title'
  $ )
  $ )
  $ const episodeTitle =
  $ cleanSpaces(
  $ getTag(
  $ block,
  $ 'sub-title'
  $ )
  $ )
  $ const originalDescription =
  $ cleanSpaces(
  $ getTag(
  $ block,
  $ 'desc'
  $ )
  $ )
  $ const episodeNumber =
  $ getEpisodeNumber(
  $ block
  $ )
  $ const originalAirDate =
  $ parseOriginalAirDate(
  $ getTagBySystem(
  $ block,
  $ 'episode-num',
  $ 'original-air-date'
  $ )
  $ )
  $ let newDescription =
  $ originalDescription
  $ if (
  $ episodeTitle &&
  $ episodeNumber
  $ ) {
  $ newDescription =
  $ episodeTitle +
  $ ' - ' +
  $ episodeNumber +
  $ (
  $ originalDescription
  $ ? '. ' +
  $ originalDescription
  $ : ''
  $ )
  $ episodeNumbersConverted++
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ originalAirDate
  $ ) {
  $ newDescription =
  $ newDescription
  $ ? newDescription +
  $ ' (' +
  $ originalAirDate +
  $ ')'
  $ : '(' +
  $ originalAirDate +
  $ ')'
  $ originalAirDatesUsed++
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ newDescription !==
  $ originalDescription
  $ ) {
  $ descriptionsChanged++
  $ }
  $ let output =
  $ block
  $ output =
  $ replaceTag(
  $ output,
  $ 'title',
  $ originalTitle
  $ )
  $ output =
  $ replaceTag(
  $ output,
  $ 'desc',
  $ newDescription
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ /<actor\b/i.test(
  $ output
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ actorsRemoved++
  $ }
  $ output =
  $ removeTag(
  $ output,
  $ 'actor'
  $ )
  $ output =
  $ removeTag(
  $ output,
  $ 'credits'
  $ )
  $ output =
  $ removeTag(
  $ output,
  $ 'sub-title'
  $ )
  $ output =
  $ removeTag(
  $ output,
  $ 'episode-num'
  $ )
  $ output =
  $ removeTag(
  $ output,
  $ 'date'
  $ )
  $ return output
  $ }
  $ async function main() {
  $ const input =
  $ fs.createReadStream(
  $ inputPath,
  $ {
  $ encoding: 'utf8'
  $ }
  $ )
  $ const output =
  $ fs.createWriteStream(
  $ outputPath,
  $ {
  $ encoding: 'utf8'
  $ }
  $ )
  $ const rl =
  $ readline.createInterface(
  $ {
  $ input,
  $ crlfDelay: Infinity
  $ }
  $ )
  $ let insideProgramme =
  $ false
  $ let programmeBuffer =
  $ ''
  $ function write(value) {
  $ return new Promise(
  $ (
  $ resolve,
  $ reject
  $ ) => {
  $ if (
  $ output.write(
  $ value
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ resolve()
  $ } else {
  $ output.once(
  $ 'drain',
  $ resolve
  $ )
  $ output.once(
  $ 'error',
  $ reject
  $ )
  $ }
  $ }
  $ )
  $ }
  $ for await (
  $ const line of rl
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ !insideProgramme
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '<programme '
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideProgramme =
  $ true
  $ programmeBuffer =
  $ line +
  $ '\n'
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '</programme>'
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideProgramme =
  $ false
  $ const processed =
  $ processProgramme(
  $ programmeBuffer
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ processed
  $ ) {
  $ await write(
  $ processed
  $ )
  $ }
  $ programmeBuffer =
  $ ''
  $ }
  $ } else {
  $ await write(
  $ line +
  $ '\n'
  $ )
  $ }
  $ } else {
  $ programmeBuffer +=
  $ line +
  $ '\n'
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '</programme>'
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideProgramme =
  $ false
  $ const processed =
  $ processProgramme(
  $ programmeBuffer
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ processed
  $ ) {
  $ await write(
  $ processed
  $ )
  $ }
  $ programmeBuffer =
  $ ''
  $ }
  $ }
  $ }
  $ await new Promise(
  $ (
  $ resolve,
  $ reject
  $ ) => {
  $ output.once(
  $ 'error',
  $ reject
  $ )
  $ output.end(
  $ resolve
  $ )
  $ }
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'FAST processing complete'
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Programmes scanned: ' +
  $ programmesScanned
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Programmes kept: ' +
  $ programmesKept
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Programmes skipped: ' +
  $ programmesSkipped
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Descriptions changed: ' +
  $ descriptionsChanged
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Actors removed: ' +
  $ actorsRemoved
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Episode numbers converted: ' +
  $ episodeNumbersConverted
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Original air dates used: ' +
  $ originalAirDatesUsed
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Output: ' +
  $ outputPath
  $ )
  $ }
  $ main().catch(
  $ error => {
  $ console.error(
  $ error
  $ )
  $ process.exit(
  $ 1
  $ )
  $ }
  $ )
  $ EOF
  $ node /tmp/process_fast.js
  $ CHANNELS=$(grep -c '<channel ' fast/processed.xml || true)
  $ PROGRAMMES=$(grep -c '<programme ' fast/processed.xml || true)
  $ echo "Processed FAST channels: $CHANNELS"
  $ echo "Processed FAST programmes: $PROGRAMMES"
  $ if [ "$CHANNELS" -lt 1 ]; then
  $ echo "Processed FAST guide contains no channels"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ if [ "$PROGRAMMES" -lt 1 ]; then
  $ echo "Processed FAST guide contains no programmes"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ test -s fast/processed.xml
  $ gzip -c fast/processed.xml > guides/FAST.xml.gz
  $ test -s guides/FAST.xml.gz
  $ ls -lh guides/FAST.xml.gz
  $ git config user.name "github-actions"
  $ git config user.email "github-actions@github.com"
  $ git add guides/FAST.xml.gz
  $ if git diff --cached --quiet; then
  $ echo "No FAST guide changes to commit"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ git commit -m "Update FAST guide"
  $ for attempt in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  $ echo "Push attempt $attempt"
  $ if git push origin HEAD:main; then
  $ echo "Push succeeded"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ echo "Push failed. Synchronizing with remote before retry..."
  $ git fetch origin main
  $ git rebase origin/main
  $ echo "Waiting before retry..."
  $ sleep 10
  $ done
  $ echo "Push failed after all retries"
  $ exit 1

SOURCEFASTCHANNELS
------------------
Workflow: .github/workflows/SOURCEFASTCHANNELS.yml

REMOTE SOURCES:
  - https://epg.guru/7dayiptv/USFast.xml

COMMANDS EXECUTED:
  $ mkdir -p sourcefastchannels
  $ curl -L --fail --retry 5 --retry-delay 10 \
  $ https://epg.guru/7dayiptv/USFast.xml \
  $ -o sourcefastchannels/USFast.xml
  $ test -s sourcefastchannels/USFast.xml
  $ echo "Jessman FAST source downloaded successfully"
  $ ls -lh sourcefastchannels/USFast.xml
  $ node <<'EOF'
  $ const fs = require('fs')
  $ const readline = require('readline')
  $ const inputPath =
  $ 'sourcefastchannels/USFast.xml'
  $ const outputPath =
  $ 'SOURCEFASTCHANNELS.txt'
  $ function decodeXml(value) {
  $ return String(value || '')
  $ .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
  $ .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
  $ .replace(/&apos;/g, "'")
  $ .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
  $ .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
  $ }
  $ function getTag(block, tag) {
  $ const regex =
  $ new RegExp(
  $ '<' +
  $ tag +
  $ '\\b[^>]*>([\\s\\S]*?)</' +
  $ tag +
  $ '>',
  $ 'i'
  $ )
  $ const match =
  $ block.match(
  $ regex
  $ )
  $ return match
  $ ? decodeXml(
  $ match[1]
  $ .replace(
  $ /<[^>]+>/g,
  $ ''
  $ )
  $ .trim()
  $ )
  $ : ''
  $ }
  $ function getAttribute(
  $ block,
  $ attribute
  $ ) {
  $ const regex =
  $ new RegExp(
  $ attribute +
  $ '\\s*=\\s*"([^"]*)"',
  $ 'i'
  $ )
  $ const match =
  $ block.match(
  $ regex
  $ )
  $ return match
  $ ? decodeXml(
  $ match[1]
  $ )
  $ : ''
  $ }
  $ async function main() {
  $ const input =
  $ fs.createReadStream(
  $ inputPath,
  $ {
  $ encoding: 'utf8'
  $ }
  $ )
  $ const rl =
  $ readline.createInterface(
  $ {
  $ input,
  $ crlfDelay: Infinity
  $ }
  $ )
  $ const channels = []
  $ let insideChannel =
  $ false
  $ let channelBuffer =
  $ ''
  $ for await (
  $ const line of rl
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ !insideChannel
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '<channel '
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideChannel =
  $ true
  $ channelBuffer =
  $ line +
  $ '\n'
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '</channel>'
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideChannel =
  $ false
  $ const block =
  $ channelBuffer
  $ const id =
  $ getAttribute(
  $ block,
  $ 'id'
  $ )
  $ const displayName =
  $ getTag(
  $ block,
  $ 'display-name'
  $ )
  $ const iconBlock =
  $ block.match(
  $ /<icon\b[^>]*>/i
  $ )
  $ const icon =
  $ iconBlock
  $ ? getAttribute(
  $ iconBlock[0],
  $ 'src'
  $ )
  $ : ''
  $ const url =
  $ getTag(
  $ block,
  $ 'url'
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ id ||
  $ displayName
  $ ) {
  $ channels.push(
  $ {
  $ id,
  $ displayName,
  $ icon,
  $ url
  $ }
  $ )
  $ }
  $ channelBuffer =
  $ ''
  $ }
  $ }
  $ } else {
  $ channelBuffer +=
  $ line +
  $ '\n'
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '</channel>'
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideChannel =
  $ false
  $ const block =
  $ channelBuffer
  $ const id =
  $ getAttribute(
  $ block,
  $ 'id'
  $ )
  $ const displayName =
  $ getTag(
  $ block,
  $ 'display-name'
  $ )
  $ const iconBlock =
  $ block.match(
  $ /<icon\b[^>]*>/i
  $ )
  $ const icon =
  $ iconBlock
  $ ? getAttribute(
  $ iconBlock[0],
  $ 'src'
  $ )
  $ : ''
  $ const url =
  $ getTag(
  $ block,
  $ 'url'
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ id ||
  $ displayName
  $ ) {
  $ channels.push(
  $ {
  $ id,
  $ displayName,
  $ icon,
  $ url
  $ }
  $ )
  $ }
  $ channelBuffer =
  $ ''
  $ }
  $ }
  $ }
  $ channels.sort(
  $ (
  $ a,
  $ b
  $ ) =>
  $ a.displayName.localeCompare(
  $ b.displayName,
  $ undefined,
  $ {
  $ sensitivity: 'base'
  $ }
  $ )
  $ )
  $ const output =
  $ fs.createWriteStream(
  $ outputPath,
  $ {
  $ encoding: 'utf8'
  $ }
  $ )
  $ function write(
  $ value
  $ ) {
  $ return new Promise(
  $ (
  $ resolve,
  $ reject
  $ ) => {
  $ if (
  $ output.write(
  $ value
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ resolve()
  $ } else {
  $ output.once(
  $ 'drain',
  $ resolve
  $ )
  $ output.once(
  $ 'error',
  $ reject
  $ )
  $ }
  $ }
  $ )
  $ }
  $ await write(
  $ 'SOURCE FAST CHANNELS\n'
  $ )
  $ await write(
  $ 'Generated: ' +
  $ new Date().toISOString() +
  $ '\n'
  $ )
  $ await write(
  $ 'Total channels: ' +
  $ channels.length +
  $ '\n'
  $ )
  $ await write(
  $ '\n'
  $ )
  $ for (
  $ const channel of channels
  $ ) {
  $ await write(
  $ [
  $ channel.displayName ||
  $ '(no display name)',
  $ channel.id ||
  $ '(no channel id)',
  $ channel.icon ||
  $ '(no icon)',
  $ channel.url ||
  $ '(no url)'
  $ ].join(
  $ ' | '
  $ ) +
  $ '\n'
  $ )
  $ }
  $ await new Promise(
  $ (
  $ resolve,
  $ reject
  $ ) => {
  $ output.once(
  $ 'error',
  $ reject
  $ )
  $ output.end(
  $ resolve
  $ )
  $ }
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'SOURCE FAST channels found: ' +
  $ channels.length
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Output: ' +
  $ outputPath
  $ )
  $ }
  $ main().catch(
  $ error => {
  $ console.error(
  $ error
  $ )
  $ process.exit(
  $ 1
  $ )
  $ }
  $ )
  $ EOF
  $ test -s SOURCEFASTCHANNELS.txt
  $ CHANNEL_COUNT=$(grep -c ' | ' SOURCEFASTCHANNELS.txt || true)
  $ echo "Channels written: $CHANNEL_COUNT"
  $ if [ "$CHANNEL_COUNT" -lt 1 ]; then
  $ echo "No channels were extracted"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ echo "First 25 channels:"
  $ head -n 30 SOURCEFASTCHANNELS.txt
  $ echo ""
  $ echo "File size:"
  $ ls -lh SOURCEFASTCHANNELS.txt
  $ git config user.name "github-actions"
  $ git config user.email "github-actions@github.com"
  $ git add SOURCEFASTCHANNELS.txt
  $ if git diff --cached --quiet; then
  $ echo "No SOURCEFASTCHANNELS changes to commit"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ git commit -m "Update SOURCEFASTCHANNELS"
  $ for attempt in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  $ echo "Push attempt $attempt"
  $ if git push origin HEAD:main; then
  $ echo "Push succeeded"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ echo "Push failed. Fetching latest remote changes..."
  $ git fetch origin main
  $ if git rebase origin/main; then
  $ echo "Rebase succeeded"
  $ else
  $ echo "Rebase failed. Aborting rebase."
  $ git rebase --abort || true
  $ fi
  $ echo "Waiting before retry..."
  $ sleep 10
  $ done
  $ echo "Push failed after all retries"
  $ exit 1

  $ ) {

  $ value ||

  $ )

  $ )

  $ function cleanSpaces(
  $ value
  $ ) {
  $ return String(
  $ value ||
  $ ''
  $ )
  $ .replace(
  $ /\s+/g,
  $ ' '
  $ )
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function removeEmbeddedCredits(
  $ value
  $ ) {
  $ let result =
  $ cleanSpaces(
  $ value
  $ )
  $ const protectedDates =
  $ []
  $ result =
  $ result.replace(
  $ /\(\d{2}\/\d{2}\/\d{4}\)/g,
  $ match => {
  $ const token =
  $ '__AIRDATE_' +
  $ protectedDates.length +
  $ '__'
  $ protectedDates.push(
  $ match
  $ )
  $ return token
  $ }
  $ )
  $ result =
  $ result.replace(
  $ /\([^()]*\)/g,
  $ ''
  $ )
  $ result =
  $ result.replace(
  $ /\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z.'-]*(?:\s*,\s*[A-Z][A-Za-z.'-]*)*(?:\s+(?:and|&)\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z.'-]*)\s+(?:star|stars|starring)\.?\s*$/i,
  $ ''
  $ )
  $ result =
  $ result.replace(
  $ /\s+(?:with|featuring|featuring performances by|with performances by)\s+[^.!?]*[.!?]?\s*$/i,
  $ ''
  $ )
  $ result =
  $ result.replace(
  $ /\s+\*+\s*$/g,
  $ ''
  $ )
  $ result =
  $ result.replace(
  $ /\s+\.\s*$/g,
  $ ''
  $ )
  $ result =
  $ result.replace(
  $ /\s{2,}/g,
  $ ' '
  $ )
  $ .trim()
  $ result =
  $ result.replace(
  $ /__AIRDATE_(\d+)__/g,
  $ (
  $ match,
  $ index
  $ ) =>
  $ protectedDates[
  $ Number(
  $ index
  $ )
  $ ] ||
  $ ''
  $ )
  $ return cleanSpaces(
  $ result
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function normalizeChannelName(

  $ )
  $ )

  $ function parseXmltvDate(
  $ value
  $ ) {

  $ value ||

  $ function normalizeEpisode(
  $ value
  $ ) {
  $ const match =
  $ String(
  $ value ||
  $ ''
  $ ).match(
  $ /^S\s*(\d{1,3})\s*E\s*(\d{1,3})$/i
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !match
  $ ) {
  $ return ''
  $ }
  $ return (
  $ 'S' +
  $ Number(
  $ match[1]
  $ ) +
  $ 'E' +
  $ Number(
  $ match[2]
  $ )
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function extractEpisodeNumber(
  $ block
  $ ) {
  $ let episodeNumber =
  $ getTagBySystem(
  $ block,
  $ 'episode-num',
  $ 'common'
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !episodeNumber
  $ ) {
  $ episodeNumber =
  $ getTagBySystem(
  $ block,
  $ 'episode-num',
  $ 'onscreen'
  $ )
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ !episodeNumber
  $ ) {
  $ episodeNumber =
  $ getTag(
  $ block,
  $ 'episode-num'
  $ )
  $ }
  $ return normalizeEpisode(
  $ episodeNumber
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function extractEpisodeTitle(
  $ block
  $ ) {
  $ const subtitle =
  $ cleanSpaces(
  $ getTag(
  $ block,
  $ 'sub-title'
  $ )
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ subtitle
  $ ) {
  $ return subtitle
  $ }
  $ const description =
  $ cleanSpaces(
  $ getTag(
  $ block,
  $ 'desc'
  $ )
  $ )
  $ const match =
  $ description.match(
  $ /^S\s*\d{1,3}\s*E\s*\d{1,3}\s*[—-]\s*(.+?)(?:\n|$)/i
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !match
  $ ) {
  $ return ''
  $ }
  $ return cleanSpaces(
  $ match[1]
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function extractAirDate(
  $ block
  $ ) {
  $ const structured =
  $ getTagBySystem(
  $ block,
  $ 'episode-num',
  $ 'original-air-date'
  $ )
  $ const fromStructured =
  $ parseOriginalAirDate(
  $ structured
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ fromStructured
  $ ) {
  $ return fromStructured
  $ }
  $ return parseCompactAirDate(
  $ getTag(
  $ block,
  $ 'date'
  $ )
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function buildDescription(
  $ episodeTitle,
  $ episodeNumber,
  $ description,
  $ airDate
  $ ) {
  $ let result =
  $ ''
  $ if (
  $ episodeTitle &&
  $ episodeNumber
  $ ) {
  $ result =
  $ episodeTitle +
  $ ' - ' +
  $ episodeNumber
  $ } else if (
  $ episodeTitle
  $ ) {
  $ result =
  $ episodeTitle
  $ } else if (
  $ episodeNumber
  $ ) {
  $ result =
  $ episodeNumber
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ description
  $ ) {
  $ result =
  $ result
  $ ? result +
  $ '. ' +
  $ description
  $ :
  $ description
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ airDate
  $ ) {
  $ result =
  $ result
  $ ? result +
  $ ' (' +
  $ airDate +
  $ ')'
  $ :
  $ '(' +
  $ airDate +
  $ ')'
  $ }
  $ return cleanSpaces(
  $ result
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function replaceTag(
  $ block,
  $ tag,
  $ value
  $ ) {
  $ const regex =
  $ new RegExp(
  $ '<' +
  $ tag +
  $ '\\b[^>]*>[\\s\\S]*?</' +
  $ tag +
  $ '>',
  $ 'i'
  $ )
  $ const replacement =
  $ '<' +
  $ tag +
  $ ' lang="en">' +
  $ escapeXml(
  $ value
  $ ) +
  $ '</' +
  $ tag +
  $ '>'
  $ if (
  $ regex.test(
  $ block
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ return block.replace(
  $ regex,
  $ replacement
  $ )
  $ }
  $ return block.replace(
  $ /<\/programme>\s*$/i,
  $ '  ' +
  $ replacement +
  $ '\n</programme>'
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function removeTag(
  $ block,
  $ tag
  $ ) {
  $ const regex =
  $ new RegExp(
  $ '\\s*<' +
  $ tag +
  $ '\\b[^>]*>[\\s\\S]*?</' +
  $ tag +
  $ '>',
  $ 'gi'
  $ )
  $ return block.replace(
  $ regex,
  $ ''
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function cleanProgrammeBlock(
  $ block,
  $ source
  $ ) {
  $ const originalDescription =
  $ cleanSpaces(
  $ getTag(
  $ block,
  $ 'desc'
  $ )
  $ )
  $ const episodeTitle =
  $ extractEpisodeTitle(
  $ block
  $ )
  $ const episodeNumber =
  $ extractEpisodeNumber(
  $ block
  $ )
  $ const airDate =
  $ extractAirDate(
  $ block
  $ )
  $ const cleanedOriginalDescription =
  $ removeEmbeddedCredits(
  $ originalDescription
  $ )
  $ const description =
  $ buildDescription(
  $ episodeTitle,
  $ episodeNumber,
  $ cleanedOriginalDescription,
  $ airDate
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ description !==
  $ originalDescription
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ source ===
  $ 'jessman'
  $ ) {
  $ jessmanDescriptionsChanged++
  $ } else {
  $ iptvOrgDescriptionsChanged++
  $ }
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ /<actor\b/i.test(
  $ block
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ source ===
  $ 'jessman'
  $ ) {
  $ jessmanActorsRemoved++
  $ } else {
  $ iptvOrgActorsRemoved++
  $ }
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ /<credits\b/i.test(
  $ block
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ source ===
  $ 'jessman'
  $ ) {
  $ jessmanCreditsRemoved++
  $ } else {
  $ iptvOrgCreditsRemoved++
  $ }
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ episodeNumber
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ source ===
  $ 'jessman'
  $ ) {
  $ jessmanEpisodeNumbersConverted++
  $ } else {
  $ iptvOrgEpisodeNumbersConverted++
  $ }
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ airDate
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ source ===
  $ 'jessman'
  $ ) {
  $ jessmanOriginalAirDatesUsed++
  $ } else {
  $ iptvOrgOriginalAirDatesUsed++
  $ }
  $ }
  $ let output =
  $ block
  $ output =
  $ replaceTag(
  $ output,
  $ 'title',
  $ cleanSpaces(
  $ getTag(
  $ block,
  $ 'title'
  $ )
  $ .replace(
  $ /\s+\(\d{4}\)\s*$/,
  $ ''
  $ )
  $ )
  $ )
  $ output =
  $ replaceTag(
  $ output,
  $ 'desc',
  $ description
  $ )
  $ output =
  $ removeTag(
  $ output,
  $ 'actor'
  $ )
  $ output =
  $ removeTag(
  $ output,
  $ 'credits'
  $ )
  $ return {
  $ block:
  $ output,
  $ title:
  $ cleanSpaces(
  $ getTag(
  $ block,
  $ 'title'
  $ )
  $ .replace(
  $ /\s+\(\d{4}\)\s*$/,
  $ ''
  $ )
  $ ),
  $ episodeTitle,
  $ episodeNumber,
  $ airDate,
  $ description,
  $ icon:
  $ getAttribute(
  $ block.match(
  $ /<icon\b[^>]*>/i
  $ )?.[0] ||
  $ '',
  $ 'src'
  $ )
  $ }
  $ }
  $ function processProgramme(
  $ block,
  $ source,
  $ canonicalChannel
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ source ===
  $ 'jessman'
  $ ) {
  $ jessmanProgrammesScanned++
  $ } else {
  $ iptvOrgProgrammesScanned++
  $ }
  $ const start =
  $ getAttribute(
  $ block,
  $ 'start'
  $ )
  $ const startDate =
  $ parseXmltvDate(
  $ start
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !startDate ||
  $ startDate < pastCutoff ||
  $ startDate >= cutoff
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ source ===
  $ 'jessman'
  $ ) {
  $ jessmanProgrammesSkipped++
  $ } else {
  $ iptvOrgProgrammesSkipped++
  $ }
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ source ===
  $ 'jessman'
  $ ) {
  $ jessmanProgrammesKept++
  $ } else {
  $ iptvOrgProgrammesKept++
  $ }
  $ const cleaned =
  $ cleanProgrammeBlock(
  $ block,
  $ source
  $ )
  $ const channel =
  $ getAttribute(
  $ block,
  $ 'channel'
  $ )
  $ const key =
  $ [
  $ canonicalChannel,
  $ start,
  $ normalizeTitle(
  $ cleaned.title
  $ )
  $ ].join(
  $ '|'
  $ )
  $ return {
  $ source,
  $ channel,
  $ canonicalChannel,
  $ start,
  $ stop:
  $ getAttribute(
  $ block,
  $ 'stop'
  $ ),
  $ title:
  $ cleaned.title,
  $ episodeTitle:
  $ cleaned.episodeTitle,
  $ episodeNumber:
  $ cleaned.episodeNumber,
  $ airDate:
  $ cleaned.airDate,
  $ description:
  $ cleaned.description,
  $ icon:
  $ cleaned.icon,
  $ block:
  $ cleaned.block,
  $ key
  $ }
  $ }
  $ function parseXmlStream(
  $ stream,
  $ callback
  $ ) {
  $ return new Promise(
  $ (
  $ resolve,
  $ reject
  $ ) => {
  $ const rl =
  $ readline.createInterface(
  $ {
  $ input:
  $ stream,
  $ crlfDelay:
  $ Infinity
  $ }
  $ )
  $ let insideChannel =
  $ false
  $ let channelBuffer =
  $ ''
  $ let insideProgramme =
  $ false
  $ let programmeBuffer =
  $ ''
  $ ;(async () => {
  $ try {
  $ for await (
  $ const line of rl
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ !insideChannel &&
  $ !insideProgramme &&
  $ line.includes(
  $ '<channel '
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideChannel =
  $ true
  $ channelBuffer =
  $ line +
  $ '\n'
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '</channel>'
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideChannel =
  $ false
  $ await callback(
  $ 'channel',
  $ channelBuffer
  $ )
  $ channelBuffer =
  $ ''
  $ }
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ insideChannel
  $ ) {
  $ channelBuffer +=
  $ line +
  $ '\n'
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '</channel>'
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideChannel =
  $ false
  $ await callback(
  $ 'channel',
  $ channelBuffer
  $ )
  $ channelBuffer =
  $ ''
  $ }
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '<programme '
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideProgramme =
  $ true
  $ programmeBuffer =
  $ line +
  $ '\n'
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '</programme>'
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideProgramme =
  $ false
  $ await callback(
  $ 'programme',
  $ programmeBuffer
  $ )
  $ programmeBuffer =
  $ ''
  $ }
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ insideProgramme
  $ ) {
  $ programmeBuffer +=
  $ line +
  $ '\n'
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '</programme>'
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideProgramme =
  $ false
  $ await callback(
  $ 'programme',
  $ programmeBuffer
  $ )
  $ programmeBuffer =
  $ ''
  $ }
  $ }
  $ }
  $ resolve()
  $ } catch (
  $ error
  $ ) {
  $ reject(
  $ error
  $ )
  $ }
  $ })()
  $ }
  $ )
  $ }
  $ async function loadJessman() {
  $ await parseXmlStream(
  $ fs.createReadStream(
  $ jessmanPath
  $ ),
  $ async (
  $ type,
  $ block
  $ ) => {
  $ if (
  $ type ===
  $ 'channel'
  $ ) {
  $ const id =
  $ getAttribute(
  $ block,
  $ 'id'
  $ )
  $ const displayNames =
  $ getAllTags(
  $ block,
  $ 'display-name'
  $ )
  $ const normalizedNames =
  $ displayNames.map(
  $ normalizeChannelName
  $ )
  $ let matchedName =
  $ ''
  $ for (
  $ const targetName of
  $ Object.keys(
  $ jessmanChannels
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ normalizedNames.includes(
  $ normalizeChannelName(
  $ targetName
  $ )
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ matchedName =
  $ targetName
  $ break
  $ }
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ !matchedName
  $ ) {
  $ return
  $ }
  $ jessmanChannelsFound++
  $ jessmanIdToName.set(
  $ id,
  $ matchedName
  $ )
  $ channelHeaders.set(
  $ 'jessman:' +
  $ matchedName,
  $ {
  $ source:
  $ 'jessman',
  $ id,
  $ canonicalChannel:
  $ matchedName,
  $ displayName:
  $ jessmanChannels[
  $ matchedName
  $ ].displayName,
  $ block
  $ }
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Jessman channel matched: ' +
  $ matchedName +
  $ ' -> ' +
  $ id
  $ )
  $ return
  $ }
  $ const channel =
  $ getAttribute(
  $ block,
  $ 'channel'
  $ )
  $ const canonicalChannel =
  $ jessmanIdToName.get(
  $ channel
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !canonicalChannel
  $ ) {
  $ return
  $ }
  $ const programme =
  $ processProgramme(
  $ block,
  $ 'jessman',
  $ canonicalChannel
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !programme
  $ ) {
  $ return
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ !jessman.has(
  $ canonicalChannel
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ jessman.set(
  $ canonicalChannel,
  $ new Map()
  $ )
  $ }
  $ jessman.get(
  $ canonicalChannel
  $ ).set(
  $ programme.key,
  $ programme
  $ )
  $ }
  $ )
  $ }
  $ async function loadIptvOrg() {
  $ const input =
  $ fs.createReadStream(
  $ iptvOrgPath
  $ )
  $ const gunzip =
  $ zlib.createGunzip()
  $ input.pipe(
  $ gunzip
  $ )
  $ await parseXmlStream(
  $ gunzip,
  $ async (
  $ type,
  $ block
  $ ) => {
  $ if (
  $ type ===
  $ 'channel'
  $ ) {
  $ const id =
  $ getAttribute(
  $ block,
  $ 'id'
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !iptvOrgChannels[id]
  $ ) {
  $ return
  $ }
  $ iptvOrgChannelsFound++
  $ const channel =
  $ iptvOrgChannels[id]
  $ channelHeaders.set(
  $ 'iptv:' +
  $ id,
  $ {
  $ source:
  $ 'iptvorg',
  $ id,
  $ canonicalChannel:
  $ channel.displayName,
  $ displayName:
  $ channel.displayName,
  $ block
  $ }
  $ )
  $ return
  $ }
  $ const channel =
  $ getAttribute(
  $ block,
  $ 'channel'
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !iptvOrgChannels[channel]
  $ ) {

  $ }

  $ const programme =
  $ processProgramme(
  $ block,

  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !programme
  $ ) {

  $ }

  $ !iptvOrg.has(

  $ ) {
  $ iptvOrg.set(

  $ )
  $ }
  $ iptvOrg.get(
  $ canonicalChannel
  $ ).set(
  $ programme.key,
  $ programme
  $ )
  $ }
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function fillMissingField(
  $ target,
  $ field,
  $ value,
  $ source
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ target[field]
  $ ) {
  $ return false
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ !value
  $ ) {
  $ return false
  $ }
  $ target[field] =
  $ value
  $ if (
  $ source ===
  $ 'iptvorg'
  $ ) {
  $ iptvFieldsUsed++
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ source ===
  $ 'tvmaze'
  $ ) {
  $ tvmazeFieldsUsed++
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ source ===
  $ 'tmdb'
  $ ) {
  $ tmdbFieldsUsed++
  $ }
  $ return true
  $ }
  $ function mergeMissingFields(
  $ target,
  $ source,
  $ sourceName
  $ ) {
  $ fillMissingField(
  $ target,
  $ 'episodeTitle',
  $ source.episodeTitle,
  $ sourceName
  $ )
  $ fillMissingField(
  $ target,
  $ 'episodeNumber',
  $ source.episodeNumber,
  $ sourceName
  $ )
  $ fillMissingField(
  $ target,
  $ 'description',
  $ source.description,
  $ sourceName
  $ )
  $ fillMissingField(
  $ target,
  $ 'airDate',
  $ source.airDate,
  $ sourceName
  $ )
  $ fillMissingField(
  $ target,
  $ 'icon',
  $ source.icon,
  $ sourceName
  $ )
  $ }
  $ const tvmazeCache =
  $ new Map()
  $ const tmdbCache =
  $ new Map()
  $ function sleep(
  $ milliseconds
  $ ) {
  $ return new Promise(
  $ resolve =>
  $ setTimeout(
  $ resolve,
  $ milliseconds
  $ )
  $ )
  $ }
  $ async function fetchJson(
  $ url,
  $ headers = {}
  $ ) {
  $ try {
  $ const response =
  $ await fetch(
  $ url,
  $ {
  $ headers
  $ }
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !response.ok
  $ ) {
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ return await response.json()
  $ } catch (
  $ error
  $ ) {
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ }
  $ async function getTvmazeShow(
  $ title
  $ ) {
  $ const key =
  $ normalizeTitle(
  $ title
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ tvmazeCache.has(
  $ key
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ return tvmazeCache.get(
  $ key
  $ )
  $ }
  $ const data =
  $ await fetchJson(
  $ 'https://api.tvmaze.com/search/shows?q=' +
  $ encodeURIComponent(
  $ title
  $ )
  $ )
  $ await sleep(
  $ 250
  $ )
  $ const result =
  $ data &&
  $ data.length
  $ ? data[0].show
  $ : null
  $ tvmazeCache.set(
  $ key,
  $ result
  $ )
  $ return result
  $ }
  $ async function enrichFromTvmaze(
  $ programme
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ !programme.episodeNumber
  $ ) {
  $ return
  $ }
  $ const match =
  $ programme.episodeNumber.match(
  $ /^S(\d+)E(\d+)$/i
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !match
  $ ) {
  $ return
  $ }
  $ const show =
  $ await getTvmazeShow(
  $ programme.title
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !show
  $ ) {
  $ return
  $ }
  $ const episodeData =
  $ await fetchJson(
  $ 'https://api.tvmaze.com/shows/' +
  $ show.id +
  $ '/episodebynumber?season=' +
  $ Number(
  $ match[1]
  $ ) +
  $ '&number=' +
  $ Number(
  $ match[2]
  $ )
  $ )
  $ await sleep(
  $ 250
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !episodeData
  $ ) {
  $ return
  $ }
  $ let used =
  $ false
  $ used =
  $ fillMissingField(
  $ programme,
  $ 'episodeTitle',
  $ cleanSpaces(
  $ episodeData.name
  $ ),
  $ 'tvmaze'
  $ ) ||
  $ used
  $ used =
  $ fillMissingField(
  $ programme,
  $ 'description',
  $ cleanSpaces(
  $ episodeData.summary
  $ ? episodeData.summary
  $ .replace(
  $ /<[^>]+>/g,
  $ ''
  $ )
  $ : ''
  $ ),
  $ 'tvmaze'
  $ ) ||
  $ used
  $ used =
  $ fillMissingField(
  $ programme,
  $ 'airDate',
  $ episodeData.airdate
  $ ? episodeData.airdate
  $ .split(
  $ '-'
  $ )
  $ .reverse()
  $ .join(
  $ '/'
  $ )
  $ : '',
  $ 'tvmaze'
  $ ) ||
  $ used
  $ used =
  $ fillMissingField(
  $ programme,
  $ 'icon',
  $ episodeData.image
  $ ? (
  $ episodeData.image.original ||
  $ episodeData.image.medium
  $ )
  $ : '',
  $ 'tvmaze'
  $ ) ||
  $ used
  $ if (
  $ used
  $ ) {
  $ tvmazeMatches++
  $ }
  $ }
  $ async function enrichFromTmdb(
  $ programme
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ !process.env.TMDB_API_TOKEN
  $ ) {
  $ return
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ !programme.episodeNumber
  $ ) {
  $ return
  $ }
  $ const match =
  $ programme.episodeNumber.match(
  $ /^S(\d+)E(\d+)$/i
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !match
  $ ) {
  $ return
  $ }
  $ const cacheKey =
  $ normalizeTitle(
  $ programme.title
  $ )
  $ let show =
  $ tmdbCache.get(
  $ cacheKey
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !show
  $ ) {
  $ const searchData =
  $ await fetchJson(
  $ 'https://api.themoviedb.org/3/search/tv?query=' +
  $ encodeURIComponent(
  $ programme.title
  $ ),
  $ {
  $ Authorization:
  $ 'Bearer ' +
  $ process.env.TMDB_API_TOKEN,
  $ accept:
  $ 'application/json'
  $ }
  $ )
  $ show =
  $ searchData &&
  $ searchData.results &&
  $ searchData.results.length
  $ ? searchData.results[0]
  $ : null
  $ tmdbCache.set(
  $ cacheKey,
  $ show
  $ )
  $ await sleep(
  $ 250
  $ )
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ !show
  $ ) {
  $ return
  $ }
  $ const episodeData =
  $ await fetchJson(
  $ 'https://api.themoviedb.org/3/tv/' +
  $ show.id +
  $ '/season/' +
  $ Number(
  $ match[1]
  $ ) +
  $ '/episode/' +
  $ Number(
  $ match[2]
  $ ),
  $ {
  $ Authorization:
  $ 'Bearer ' +
  $ process.env.TMDB_API_TOKEN,
  $ accept:
  $ 'application/json'
  $ }
  $ )
  $ await sleep(
  $ 250
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ !episodeData
  $ ) {
  $ return
  $ }
  $ let used =
  $ false
  $ used =
  $ fillMissingField(
  $ programme,
  $ 'episodeTitle',
  $ cleanSpaces(
  $ episodeData.name
  $ ),
  $ 'tmdb'
  $ ) ||
  $ used
  $ used =
  $ fillMissingField(
  $ programme,
  $ 'description',
  $ cleanSpaces(
  $ episodeData.overview
  $ ),
  $ 'tmdb'
  $ ) ||
  $ used
  $ used =
  $ fillMissingField(
  $ programme,
  $ 'airDate',
  $ episodeData.air_date
  $ ? episodeData.air_date
  $ .split(
  $ '-'
  $ )
  $ .reverse()
  $ .join(
  $ '/'
  $ )
  $ : '',
  $ 'tmdb'
  $ ) ||
  $ used
  $ if (
  $ used
  $ ) {
  $ tmdbMatches++
  $ }
  $ }
  $ async function buildFinalProgrammes() {
  $ const finalProgrammes =
  $ new Map()
  $ for (
  $ const programmes of
  $ jessman.values()
  $ ) {
  $ for (
  $ const programme of
  $ programmes.values()
  $ ) {
  $ finalProgrammes.set(
  $ programme.key,
  $ {
  $ ...programme
  $ }
  $ )
  $ }
  $ }
  $ for (
  $ const programmes of
  $ iptvOrg.values()
  $ ) {
  $ for (
  $ const programme of
  $ programmes.values()
  $ ) {
  $ const existing =
  $ finalProgrammes.get(
  $ programme.key
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ existing
  $ ) {
  $ jessmanIptvMatches++
  $ mergeMissingFields(
  $ existing,
  $ programme,
  $ 'iptvorg'
  $ )
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ finalProgrammes.set(
  $ programme.key,
  $ {
  $ ...programme
  $ }
  $ )
  $ iptvOnlyProgrammes++
  $ }
  $ }
  $ for (
  $ const programme of
  $ finalProgrammes.values()
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ programme.source ===
  $ 'jessman'
  $ ) {
  $ jessmanOnlyProgrammes++
  $ }
  $ }
  $ for (
  $ const programme of
  $ finalProgrammes.values()
  $ ) {
  $ await enrichFromTvmaze(
  $ programme
  $ )
  $ }
  $ for (
  $ const programme of
  $ finalProgrammes.values()
  $ ) {
  $ await enrichFromTmdb(
  $ programme
  $ )
  $ }
  $ return finalProgrammes
  $ }
  $ function buildChannel(
  $ channelHeader
  $ ) {
  $ let block =
  $ channelHeader.block
  $ const originalId =
  $ getAttribute(
  $ block,
  $ 'id'
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ originalId !==
  $ channelHeader.id
  $ ) {
  $ block =
  $ block.replace(
  $ /(<channel\b[^>]*\bid=")[^"]*(")/i,
  $ '$1' +
  $ escapeXml(
  $ channelHeader.id
  $ ) +
  $ '$2'
  $ )

  $ channelHeader.displayName
  $ if (
  $ getAllTags(
  $ block,
  $ 'display-name'
  $ ).length
  $ ) {
  $ block =
  $ block.replace(
  $ /(<display-name\b[^>]*>)[\s\S]*?(<\/display-name>)/i,
  $ '$1' +
  $ escapeXml(
  $ displayName
  $ ) +
  $ '$2'
  $ )
  $ }
  $ return block.trimEnd() +
  $ '\n'
  $ }
  $ function buildProgramme(
  $ programme
  $ ) {
  $ let block =
  $ programme.block
  $ const channel =
  $ programme.channel
  $ block =
  $ block.replace(
  $ /(\bchannel=")[^"]*(")/i,
  $ '$1' +
  $ escapeXml(
  $ channel
  $ ) +
  $ '$2'
  $ )
  $ return block.trimEnd() +
  $ '\n'
  $ }
  $ async function main() {
  $ await loadJessman()
  $ await loadIptvOrg()

  $ node <<'EOF'
  $ const fs = require('fs')
  $ const inputPath = 'guides/OTALocalsguide.raw.xml'
  $ const outputPath = 'epg/sites/tvguide.com/festy-ota.channels.xml'
  $ const OTA_PROVIDER_ID = '9100000664'
  $ const wanted = {
  $ 'WCBB-DT': 'PBS',
  $ 'WCSH': 'NBC PORTLAND',
  $ 'WGME': 'CBS PORTLAND',
  $ 'WGME-DT2': 'FOX PORTLAND',
  $ 'WPME': 'ION',
  $ 'WIPL-DT2': 'COURT TV',
  $ 'WIPL-DT4': 'GRIT',
  $ 'WIPL-DT5': 'ION PLUS',
  $ 'WIPL-DT7': 'GAME SHOW NETWORK',
  $ 'WMTW': 'ABC PORTLAND',
  $ 'WMTW-DT2': 'METV',
  $ 'WMTW-DT3': 'LAFF',
  $ 'WPFO-DT2': 'CHARGE',
  $ 'WPFO-DT3': 'COMET',
  $ 'WPXT-DT2': 'HEROES & ICONS',
  $ 'WPXT-DT3': 'ION MYSTERY'
  $ }
  $ function decodeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
  $ .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
  $ .replace(/&apos;/g, "'")
  $ .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
  $ .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
  $ }
  $ function escapeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
  $ .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
  $ .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
  $ .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
  $ }
  $ function xmltvId(name) {
  $ return String(name)
  $ .toUpperCase()
  $ .replace(/&/g, 'AND')
  $ .replace(/\+/g, 'PLUS')
  $ .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g, '.')
  $ .replace(/^\.+|\.+$/g, '') + '.us'
  $ }
  $ if (!fs.existsSync(inputPath)) {
  $ console.error(`Missing ${inputPath}`)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ const xml = fs.readFileSync(inputPath, 'utf8')
  $ const channelBlocks = [...xml.matchAll(/<channel\b([^>]*)>([\s\S]*?)<\/channel>/gi)]
  $ const outputChannels = []
  $ const found = new Set()
  $ for (const match of channelBlocks) {
  $ const attrs = match[1]
  $ const body = match[2]
  $ const idMatch = attrs.match(/id="([^"]+)"/i)
  $ if (!idMatch) continue
  $ const siteIdRaw = decodeXml(idMatch[1]).trim()
  $ const names = [...body.matchAll(/<display-name[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/display-name>/gi)]
  $ .map(m => decodeXml(m[1].replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim()))
  $ .filter(Boolean)
  $ const shortName = names[0]
  $ if (!shortName) continue
  $ if (!wanted[shortName]) continue
  $ const sourceId = siteIdRaw.split('#').pop()
  $ const siteId = `${OTA_PROVIDER_ID}#${sourceId}`
  $ const finalName = wanted[shortName]
  $ outputChannels.push(
  $ `  <channel site="tvguide.com" lang="en" xmltv_id="${escapeXml(xmltvId(finalName))}" site_id="${escapeXml(siteId)}">${escapeXml(finalName)}</channel>`
  $ )
  $ found.add(shortName)
  $ console.log(`[ADD] ${finalName} <= ${shortName} | ${siteId}`)
  $ }
  $ const missing = Object.keys(wanted).filter(name => !found.has(name))
  $ if (missing.length) {
  $ console.log('\nMissing selected OTA channels:')
  $ missing.forEach(name => console.log(`- ${name}`))
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ if (outputChannels.length !== 16) {
  $ console.error(`Expected exactly 16 OTA channels, built ${outputChannels.length}`)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ fs.writeFileSync(
  $ outputPath,
  $ `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n<channels>\n${outputChannels.join('\n')}\n</channels>\n`
  $ )
  $ console.log(`Built ${outputChannels.length} OTA channels`)

  $ async parser({ content, channel, date }) {
  $ const data = JSON.parse(content)
  $ const items = data?.data?.items || []
  $ const sourceId = String(channel.site_id).split('#').pop()
  $ const match = items.find(item =>
  $ String(item.sourceId || item.channelSourceId || item.id || item.channel?.sourceId || '') === sourceId
  $ )

  $ console.log(`[PULL] ${channel.name} | ${date.format('YYYY-MM-DD')} | programs=${schedules.length}`)
  $ const programs = []
  $ const batchSize = 10
  $ for (let i = 0; i < schedules.length; i += batchSize) {
  $ const batch = schedules.slice(i, i + batchSize)
  $ const results = await Promise.all(
  $ batch.map(async item => {
  $ const details = await loadProgramDetails(item)
  $ const title = cleanTitle(item.title || details.title || '')
  $ const episodeTitle = cleanSpaces(details.episodeTitle || details.subtitle || details.subTitle || '')
  $ const description = cleanSpaces(details.description || item.description || '')

  $ const episodeNumber = details.episodeNumber || details.episode || ''

  $ const airDate =
  $ formatTVGuideDate(details.originalAirDate || details.airDate || details.episodeAirDate) ||
  $ formatUnixDate(details.originalAirTime || details.firstAired)
  $ const programType = classifyProgramType({
  $ item,
  $ details,
  $ episodeTitle,

  $ episodeNumber,
  $ airDate,
  $ releaseYear

  $ airDate

  $ episodeCode,
  $ description,
  $ airDate,
  $ releaseYear,
  $ suppressDisplayDate
  $ })

  $ title,
  $ description: finalDescription,
  $ start: dayjs.unix(item.startTime),
  $ stop: dayjs.unix(item.endTime),
  $ category: buildCategories(programType)

  $ // Do not emit separate sub-title, episode-num, or date tags,
  $ // because the target guide display reads title + desc.
  $ return program
  $ })
  $ )
  $ programs.push(...results)
  $ }
  $ return programs
  $ }
  $ }
  $ function sleep(ms) {
  $ return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms))
  $ }
  $ function isRetryableDetailError(err) {
  $ const status = err.response?.status
  $ const code = err.code
  $ const message = String(err.message || '')
  $ if ([408, 429, 500, 502, 503, 504].includes(status)) return true
  $ if (['ECONNABORTED', 'ETIMEDOUT', 'ECONNRESET', 'ENOTFOUND', 'EAI_AGAIN'].includes(code)) return true
  $ if (/timeout|aborted|network|socket|reset/i.test(message)) return true
  $ return false
  $ }
  $ async function fetchProgramDetailsOnce(url, title, timeout, label, attempt, attempts) {
  $ try {
  $ const response = await axios.get(url, {
  $ timeout,
  $ headers: {
  $ Referer: 'https://www.tvguide.com/',
  $ 'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 Chrome/131.0.0.0 Safari/537.36'
  $ }
  $ })
  $ if (attempt > 1 || label.includes('MICRO')) {
  $ console.log(`[${label} OK] ${title} | attempt=${attempt}/${attempts}`)
  $ }
  $ return {
  $ ok: true,
  $ value: response.data?.data?.item || {}
  $ }
  $ } catch (err) {
  $ const status = err.response?.status || err.code || err.message || 'unknown'
  $ if (err.response?.status === 404) {
  $ console.log(`[${label} MISSING] ${title} | 404`)
  $ return {
  $ ok: true,
  $ value: {}
  $ }
  $ }
  $ return {
  $ ok: false,
  $ retryable: isRetryableDetailError(err),
  $ status
  $ }
  $ }
  $ }
  $ async function tryProgramDetails(url, title, options) {
  $ const { label, attempts, timeout, baseDelay, maxDelay } = options
  $ for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= attempts; attempt++) {
  $ const result = await fetchProgramDetailsOnce(url, title, timeout, label, attempt, attempts)
  $ if (result.ok) return result.value
  $ if (!result.retryable) {
  $ console.log(`[${label} FAIL] ${title} | ${result.status}`)
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ if (attempt < attempts) {
  $ const delay = Math.min(maxDelay, baseDelay * Math.pow(2, attempt - 1)) + Math.floor(Math.random() * 500)
  $ console.log(`[${label} RETRY] ${title} | ${result.status} | attempt=${attempt}/${attempts} | wait=${delay}ms`)
  $ await sleep(delay)
  $ } else {
  $ console.log(`[${label} EXHAUSTED] ${title} | ${result.status} | attempts=${attempts}`)
  $ }
  $ }
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ async function loadProgramDetails(item) {
  $ if (!item.programDetails) return {}
  $ const url = item.programDetails.includes('?')
  $ ? `${item.programDetails}&apiKey=DI9elXhZ3bU6ujsA2gXEKOANyncXGUGc`
  $ : `${item.programDetails}?apiKey=DI9elXhZ3bU6ujsA2gXEKOANyncXGUGc`
  $ const title = item.title || 'Unknown title'
  $ const fastResult = await tryProgramDetails(url, title, {
  $ label: 'DETAIL FAST',
  $ attempts: 1,
  $ timeout: 8000,
  $ baseDelay: 0,
  $ maxDelay: 0
  $ })
  $ if (fastResult) return fastResult
  $ const retryResult = await tryProgramDetails(url, title, {
  $ label: 'DETAIL BACKUP',
  $ attempts: 5,
  $ timeout: 8000,
  $ baseDelay: 750,
  $ maxDelay: 30000
  $ })
  $ if (retryResult) return retryResult
  $ console.log(`[DETAIL MICRO PASS] ${title} | starting slow cleanup pass`)
  $ const microResult = await tryProgramDetails(url, title, {
  $ label: 'DETAIL MICRO',
  $ attempts: 4,
  $ timeout: 20000,
  $ baseDelay: 5000,
  $ maxDelay: 60000
  $ })
  $ if (microResult) return microResult
  $ console.log(`[DETAIL FINAL MISS] ${title} | no details after fast + backup + micro`)
  $ return {}
  $ }
  $ function classifyProgramType({ item, details, episodeTitle, seasonNumber, episodeNumber, airDate, releaseYear }) {
  $ const haystack = [
  $ item.type,
  $ item.programType,
  $ item.entityType,
  $ item.category,
  $ item.subcategory,
  $ item.genres,
  $ details.type,
  $ details.programType,
  $ details.entityType,
  $ details.category,
  $ details.subcategory,
  $ details.genres,
  $ details.seriesId,
  $ details.showId,
  $ details.episodeId
  $ ]
  $ .flat()
  $ .filter(Boolean)
  $ .join(' ')
  $ .toLowerCase()
  $ const hasEpisodeSignals = Boolean(
  $ episodeTitle ||
  $ seasonNumber ||
  $ episodeNumber ||
  $ details.episodeNumber ||
  $ details.seasonNumber ||
  $ details.episodeTitle ||
  $ details.subtitle ||
  $ details.subTitle ||
  $ details.originalAirDate ||
  $ details.episodeAirDate ||
  $ details.seriesId ||
  $ details.showId ||
  $ details.episodeId
  $ )
  $ if (/mini[\s-]?series|limited series|limited-series/.test(haystack)) return 'miniseries'
  $ if (/series|episode|tv show|television|scripted|reality|documentary series/.test(haystack)) return 'series'
  $ if (hasEpisodeSignals) return 'series'
  $ if (/movie|film|feature film|cinema/.test(haystack)) return 'movie'
  $ const durationMinutes = getDurationMinutes(item)
  $ if (durationMinutes >= 75 && releaseYear && !airDate && !hasEpisodeSignals) return 'movie'
  $ return 'program'
  $ }
  $ function getDurationMinutes(item) {
  $ const start = Number(item.startTime || 0)
  $ const end = Number(item.endTime || 0)
  $ if (!start || !end || end <= start) return 0
  $ return Math.round((end - start) / 60)
  $ }
  $ function buildCategories(programType) {
  $ if (programType === 'movie') return ['Movie']
  $ if (programType === 'miniseries') return ['Miniseries', 'Series']
  $ if (programType === 'series') return ['Series']
  $ return ['Program']
  $ }
  $ function shouldSuppressDisplayDate({ title, description, programType, item, details, airDate }) {
  $ if (!airDate) return false
  $ const haystack = [
  $ title,
  $ description,
  $ item?.title,
  $ item?.type,
  $ item?.programType,
  $ item?.entityType,
  $ item?.category,
  $ item?.subcategory,
  $ item?.genres,
  $ details?.type,
  $ details?.programType,
  $ details?.entityType,
  $ details?.category,
  $ details?.subcategory,
  $ details?.genres
  $ ]
  $ .flat()
  $ .filter(Boolean)
  $ .join(' ')
  $ .toLowerCase()
  $ const liveOrCurrent = /\b(live|news|newscast|weather|sports|game|match|tournament|football|basketball|baseball|hockey|soccer|golf|tennis|racing|wwe|ufc|mma|boxing|pregame|postgame|post-game|pre-game)\b/.test(haystack)
  $ if (!liveOrCurrent) return false
  $ const scheduleDate = item?.startTime ? dayjs.unix(Number(item.startTime)) : null
  $ const parsedAirDate = parseDisplayDate(airDate)
  $ if (!scheduleDate || !scheduleDate.isValid() || !parsedAirDate || !parsedAirDate.isValid()) return true
  $ // For live/current news and sports, the original air date is usually just the broadcast date.
  $ // Suppress it when it is close to the scheduled airing. Keep it when it is genuinely old.
  $ return Math.abs(scheduleDate.diff(parsedAirDate, 'day')) <= 45
  $ }
  $ function parseDisplayDate(value) {
  $ const raw = String(value || '').trim()
  $ const m = raw.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})$/)
  $ if (!m) return dayjs(raw)
  $ return dayjs(`${m[3]}-${m[1].padStart(2, '0')}-${m[2].padStart(2, '0')}`)
  $ }
  $ function buildPackedDescription({ programType, episodeTitle, episodeCode, description, airDate, releaseYear, suppressDisplayDate }) {
  $ const parts = []
  $ let prefix = ''
  $ if (programType === 'series' || programType === 'miniseries') {
  $ if (episodeTitle && episodeCode) prefix = `${episodeTitle} - ${episodeCode}`
  $ else if (episodeTitle) prefix = episodeTitle
  $ else if (episodeCode) prefix = episodeCode
  $ }
  $ if (prefix) parts.push(prefix)
  $ if (description) parts.push(description)
  $ let finalDescription = cleanPackedDescription(parts.join('. '))
  $ if (programType === 'movie') {
  $ if (releaseYear && !descriptionIncludesDisplayDate(finalDescription, releaseYear)) {
  $ finalDescription = finalDescription ? `${finalDescription} (${releaseYear})` : `(${releaseYear})`
  $ }
  $ return finalDescription
  $ }
  $ if (programType === 'series' || programType === 'miniseries') {
  $ if (airDate && !suppressDisplayDate && !descriptionIncludesDisplayDate(finalDescription, airDate)) {
  $ finalDescription = finalDescription ? `${finalDescription} (${airDate})` : `(${airDate})`
  $ }
  $ return finalDescription
  $ }
  $ const dateOrYear = suppressDisplayDate ? releaseYear : (airDate || releaseYear)
  $ if (dateOrYear && !descriptionIncludesDisplayDate(finalDescription, dateOrYear)) {
  $ finalDescription = finalDescription
  $ ? `${finalDescription} (${dateOrYear})`
  $ : `(${dateOrYear})`
  $ }
  $ return finalDescription
  $ }
  $ function cleanPackedDescription(value) {
  $ return cleanSpaces(value)
  $ .replace(/\s+\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*$/g, ' ($2)')
  $ .replace(/\s+Aired\s+(\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4})\.??\s*$/i, ' ($1)')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function descriptionIncludesDisplayDate(description, dateOrYear) {
  $ if (!description || !dateOrYear) return false
  $ const escaped = String(dateOrYear).replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&')
  $ return new RegExp(`\\(${escaped}\\)\\s*$`).test(description) || description.includes(String(dateOrYear))
  $ }
  $ function buildEpisodeCode(seasonNumber, episodeNumber) {
  $ const s = String(seasonNumber || '').trim()
  $ const e = String(episodeNumber || '').trim()
  $ if (!s && !e) return ''
  $ if (s && e) return `S${s}E${e}`
  $ if (e) return `E${e}`
  $ return `S${s}`
  $ }
  $ function cleanTitle(value) {
  $ return String(value || '')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .replace(/^\s*(New|Live|Repeat)\s*[:\-]\s*/i, '')
  $ .replace(/\s*\((New|Live|Repeat)\)\s*$/i, '')
  $ .replace(/\s*[-–—]\s*(New|Live|Repeat)\s*$/i, '')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function cleanSpaces(value) {
  $ return String(value || '')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .replace(/\s+([.,!?;:])/g, '$1')
  $ .replace(/([.,!?;:])([A-Za-z0-9])/g, '$1 $2')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function formatTVGuideDate(value) {
  $ const raw = String(value || '').trim()
  $ if (!raw) return ''
  $ const tvMatch = raw.match(/\/Date\((\d+)\)\//)
  $ if (tvMatch) {
  $ return dayjs(Number(tvMatch[1])).format('MM/DD/YYYY')
  $ }
  $ const parsed = dayjs(raw)
  $ if (parsed.isValid()) return parsed.format('MM/DD/YYYY')
  $ return ''
  $ }
  $ function formatUnixDate(value) {
  $ if (!value) return ''
  $ const parsed = dayjs.unix(Number(value))
  $ return parsed.isValid() ? parsed.format('MM/DD/YYYY') : ''
  $ }
  $ function normalizeXmltvDate(value) {
  $ const parsed = dayjs(value)
  $ if (!parsed.isValid()) return ''
  $ return parsed.format('YYYYMMDD')
  $ }

  $ function sleep(ms) {

  $ function escapeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
  $ .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
  $ .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
  $ .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
