  $ function decodeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
  $ .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
  $ .replace(/&apos;/g, "'")
  $ .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
  $ .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
  $ }
  $ function stripTags(s) {
  $ return decodeXml(String(s || '').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim())
  $ }
  $ function getTag(block, tag) {
  $ const m = block.match(new RegExp(`<${tag}\\b[^>]*>([\\s\\S]*?)<\\/${tag}>`, 'i'))
  $ return m ? stripTags(m[1]) : ''
  $ }
  $ function getTags(block, tag) {
  $ return [...block.matchAll(new RegExp(`<${tag}\\b[^>]*>([\\s\\S]*?)<\\/${tag}>`, 'gi'))]
  $ .map(m => stripTags(m[1]))
  $ .filter(Boolean)
  $ }
  $ function hasTag(block, tag) {
  $ return new RegExp(`<${tag}\\b`, 'i').test(block)
  $ }
  $ function hasCategory(block, category) {
  $ return getTags(block, 'category').some(c => c.toLowerCase() === category.toLowerCase())
  $ }
  $ function hasAnyCategory(block) {
  $ return hasTag(block, 'category')
  $ }
  $ function getStartDate(block) {
  $ const m = block.match(/start="(\d{8})/i)
  $ if (!m) return ''
  $ return `${m[1].slice(0, 4)}-${m[1].slice(4, 6)}-${m[1].slice(6, 8)}`
  $ }
  $ function cleanTitleForSearch(title) {
  $ return String(title || '')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .replace(/^\s*(New|Live|Repeat)\s*[:\-]\s*/i, '')
  $ .replace(/\s*\((New|Live|Repeat)\)\s*$/i, '')
  $ .replace(/\s*[-–—]\s*(New|Live|Repeat)\s*$/i, '')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function isMovie(block) {
  $ if (hasCategory(block, 'Movie')) return true
  $ if (hasCategory(block, 'Film')) return true
  $ return false
  $ }
  $ function needsTvmaze(block) {
  $ if (!hasAnyCategory(block)) return true
  $ const desc = getTag(block, 'desc')
  $ if (!desc) return true
  $ // Strict rule: TVMaze may only fill fields TVGuide did not find.
  $ // It must not rebuild descriptions because an episode title/code is missing.
  $ // The only packed-desc edit allowed after TVGuide is appending a missing airdate.
  $ const hasPackedDisplayDate = /\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*$/.test(desc)
  $ if (!hasPackedDisplayDate) return true
  $ return false
  $ }
  $ function tvmazeShowIsMovieLike(show) {
  $ const type = String(show?.type || '').toLowerCase()
  $ return type === 'movie' || type === 'film'
  $ }
  $ function tvmazeShowIsMiniseries(show) {
  $ const type = String(show?.type || '').toLowerCase()
  $ const name = String(show?.name || '').toLowerCase()
  $ const summary = String(show?.summary || '').toLowerCase()
  $ return (
  $ type.includes('mini') ||
  $ type.includes('limited') ||
  $ name.includes('miniseries') ||
  $ summary.includes('miniseries') ||
  $ summary.includes('limited series')
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function normalize(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .toLowerCase()
  $ .replace(/&/g, 'and')
  $ .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, ' ')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function scoreShow(title, show) {
  $ const a = normalize(title)
  $ const b = normalize(show?.name || '')
  $ if (!a || !b) return 0
  $ if (a === b) return 100
  $ if (a.includes(b) || b.includes(a)) return 80
  $ return 0
  $ }
  $ async function tvmazeSearchShow(title) {
  $ const key = `show:${normalize(title)}`
  $ if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(cache, key)) return cache[key]
  $ const url = `https://api.tvmaze.com/search/shows?q=${encodeURIComponent(title)}`
  $ const result = await fetchJsonWithRetries(url, `search ${title}`)
  $ const candidates = Array.isArray(result) ? result.map(x => x.show).filter(Boolean) : []
  $ let best = null
  $ let bestScore = 0
  $ for (const show of candidates) {
  $ if (tvmazeShowIsMovieLike(show)) continue
  $ const score = scoreShow(title, show)
  $ if (score > bestScore) {
  $ best = show
  $ bestScore = score
  $ }
  $ }
  $ if (bestScore < 80) best = null
  $ cache[key] = best
  $ await sleep(250)
  $ return best
  $ }
  $ async function tvmazeEpisodes(showId) {
  $ const key = `episodes:${showId}`
  $ if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(cache, key)) return cache[key]
  $ const url = `https://api.tvmaze.com/shows/${showId}/episodes`
  $ const episodes = await fetchJsonWithRetries(url, `episodes ${showId}`)
  $ cache[key] = Array.isArray(episodes) ? episodes : []
  $ await sleep(250)
  $ return cache[key]
  $ }
  $ async function fetchJsonWithRetries(url, label) {
  $ const attempts = 4
  $ for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= attempts; attempt++) {
  $ try {
  $ const response = await fetch(url, {
  $ headers: {
  $ 'User-Agent': 'festy-epg-tvmaze-fill-missing-only/1.0'
  $ }
  $ })
  $ if (response.status === 404) return null
  $ if (response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) {
  $ throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`)
  $ }
  $ if (!response.ok) return null
  $ return await response.json()
  $ } catch (err) {
  $ if (attempt === attempts) {
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE FAIL] ${label} | ${err.message}`)
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ const delay = 1000 * attempt + Math.floor(Math.random() * 500)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE RETRY] ${label} | ${err.message} | attempt=${attempt}/${attempts}`)
  $ await sleep(delay)
  $ }
  $ }
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ function findEpisodeForProgramme(episodes, block) {
  $ const scheduleDate = getStartDate(block)
  $ const subtitle = getTag(block, 'sub-title')
  $ const desc = getTag(block, 'desc')
  $ const codeMatch = desc.match(/\bS(\d+)E(\d+)\b/i)
  $ if (codeMatch) {
  $ const season = Number(codeMatch[1])
  $ const number = Number(codeMatch[2])
  $ const byCode = episodes.find(ep => Number(ep.season) === season && Number(ep.number) === number)
  $ if (byCode) return byCode
  $ }
  $ if (subtitle) {
  $ const subNorm = normalize(subtitle)
  $ const byName = episodes.find(ep => normalize(ep.name) === subNorm)
  $ if (byName) return byName
  $ }
  $ const packedTitle = getPackedEpisodeTitleFromDesc(desc)
  $ if (packedTitle) {
  $ const packedNorm = normalize(packedTitle)
  $ const byPackedTitle = episodes.find(ep => normalize(ep.name) === packedNorm)
  $ if (byPackedTitle) return byPackedTitle
  $ }
  $ // Last fallback only. The schedule date is the current rerun date, so it is usually
  $ // not the original TVMaze airdate for syndicated/retro channels.
  $ if (scheduleDate) {
  $ const exact = episodes.find(ep => ep.airdate === scheduleDate)
  $ if (exact) return exact
  $ }
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ function getPackedEpisodeTitleFromDesc(desc) {
  $ const cleaned = cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(desc)
  $ .replace(/\s*\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*$/, '')
  $ .trim()
  $ if (!cleaned) return ''
  $ const firstSentence = cleaned.match(/^(.+?)(?:\.\s+|$)/)
  $ const first = firstSentence ? firstSentence[1].trim() : ''
  $ if (!first) return ''
  $ const prefixMatch = first.match(/^(.+?)\s+-\s+(S\d+E\d+|S\d+|E\d+)$/i)
  $ if (prefixMatch) return prefixMatch[1].trim()
  $ if (/^S\d+E\d+$/i.test(first)) return ''
  $ if (first.length > 80) return ''
  $ return first
  $ }
  $ function xmltvNs(season, episode) {
  $ const s = Number(season)
  $ const e = Number(episode)
  $ if (!Number.isFinite(s) || !Number.isFinite(e) || s < 1 || e < 1) return ''
  $ return `${s - 1}.${e - 1}.`
  $ }
  $ function formatDateXmltv(date) {
  $ if (!date || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date)) return ''
  $ return date.replace(/-/g, '')
  $ }
  $ function cleanTvmazeSummary(value) {
  $ return decodeXml(String(value || '')
  $ .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .trim())
  $ }
  $ function formatDateDisplay(date) {
  $ if (!date || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date)) return ''
  $ return `${date.slice(5, 7)}/${date.slice(8, 10)}/${date.slice(0, 4)}`
  $ }
  $ function buildTvmazeEpisodeCode(season, episode) {
  $ const s = Number(season)
  $ const e = Number(episode)
  $ if (!Number.isFinite(s) || !Number.isFinite(e) || s < 1 || e < 1) return ''
  $ return `S${s}E${e}`
  $ }
  $ function descHasEpisodeName(desc, episodeName) {
  $ if (!desc || !episodeName) return false
  $ const descNorm = normalize(desc)
  $ const nameNorm = normalize(episodeName)
  $ return Boolean(nameNorm && descNorm.includes(nameNorm))
  $ }
  $ function descHasEpisodeCode(desc, season, episode) {
  $ const code = buildTvmazeEpisodeCode(season, episode)
  $ if (!desc || !code) return false
  $ return new RegExp(`\\b${code}\\b`, 'i').test(desc)
  $ }
  $ function descHasDisplayDate(desc, airdate) {
  $ const displayDate = formatDateDisplay(airdate)
  $ if (!desc || !displayDate) return false
  $ return desc.includes(`(${displayDate})`) || desc.includes(displayDate)
  $ }
  $ function shouldSuppressTvmazeDisplayDate(block, airdate) {
  $ if (!airdate) return false
  $ const title = getTag(block, 'title')
  $ const desc = getTag(block, 'desc')
  $ const categories = getTags(block, 'category')
  $ const haystack = [title, desc, categories]
  $ .flat()
  $ .filter(Boolean)
  $ .join(' ')
  $ .toLowerCase()
  $ const liveOrCurrent = /\b(live|news|newscast|weather|sports|sporting|game|match|tournament|football|basketball|baseball|hockey|soccer|golf|tennis|racing|wwe|ufc|mma|boxing|pregame|postgame|post-game|pre-game|paid programming|infomercial)\b/.test(haystack)
  $ if (!liveOrCurrent) return false
  $ const scheduleDateText = getStartDate(block)
  $ if (!scheduleDateText || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(scheduleDateText) || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(airdate)) return true
  $ const scheduleDate = new Date(`${scheduleDateText}T00:00:00Z`)
  $ const parsedAirDate = new Date(`${airdate}T00:00:00Z`)
  $ if (Number.isNaN(scheduleDate.getTime()) || Number.isNaN(parsedAirDate.getTime())) return true
  $ // Match the original guide pass: for live/current news, sports, paid programming,
  $ // and similar listings, suppress dates when TVMaze only found the current airing date.
  $ return Math.abs(scheduleDate.getTime() - parsedAirDate.getTime()) / 86400000 <= 45
  $ }
  $ function cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(value) {
  $ return String(value || '')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .replace(/\s+([.,!?;:])/g, '$1')
  $ .replace(/([.,!?;:])([A-Za-z0-9])/g, '$1 $2')
  $ .replace(/\s+Aired\s+(\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4})\.?\s*$/i, ' ($1)')
  $ .replace(/\s+\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*$/g, ' ($2)')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function splitTrailingDisplayDate(desc) {
  $ const cleaned = cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(desc)
  $ const match = cleaned.match(/\s*\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*$/)
  $ if (!match) return { body: cleaned, dateText: '' }
  $ return {
  $ body: cleaned.slice(0, match.index).trim(),
  $ dateText: match[1]
  $ }
  $ }
  $ function parsePackedDescription(desc) {
  $ const split = splitTrailingDisplayDate(desc)
  $ const withoutDate = split.body
  $ const firstSentence = withoutDate.match(/^(.+?)(?:\.\s+|$)/)
  $ const first = firstSentence ? firstSentence[1].trim() : ''
  $ const rest = firstSentence && firstSentence[0].length < withoutDate.length
  $ ? withoutDate.slice(firstSentence[0].length).trim()
  $ : ''
  $ const prefixMatch = first.match(/^(.+?)\s+-\s+(S\d+E\d+|S\d+|E\d+)$/i)
  $ if (prefixMatch) {
  $ return {
  $ episodeTitle: prefixMatch[1].trim(),
  $ episodeCode: prefixMatch[2].toUpperCase(),
  $ body: rest,
  $ dateText: split.dateText,
  $ hadPrefix: true
  $ }
  $ }
  $ const codeOnlyMatch = first.match(/^(S\d+E\d+|S\d+|E\d+)$/i)
  $ if (codeOnlyMatch) {
  $ return {
  $ episodeTitle: '',
  $ episodeCode: codeOnlyMatch[1].toUpperCase(),
  $ body: rest,
  $ dateText: split.dateText,
  $ hadPrefix: true
  $ }
  $ }
  $ // A first sentence that exactly matches the TVMaze episode name is an existing title-only prefix.
  $ return {
  $ episodeTitle: '',
  $ episodeCode: '',
  $ body: withoutDate,
  $ dateText: split.dateText,
  $ hadPrefix: false
  $ }
  $ }
  $ function buildTvmazePackedDescription(existingDesc, show, episode, block) {
  $ const existing = cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(existingDesc)
  $ const parsed = parsePackedDescription(existing)
  $ const episodeName = cleanTvmazeSummary(episode?.name || '')
  $ const episodeCode = buildTvmazeEpisodeCode(episode?.season, episode?.number)
  $ const episodeSummary = cleanTvmazeSummary(episode?.summary || '')
  $ const showSummary = cleanTvmazeSummary(show?.summary || '')
  $ const displayDate = formatDateDisplay(episode?.airdate)
  $ let titlePart = parsed.episodeTitle
  $ let codePart = parsed.episodeCode
  $ let bodyPart = parsed.body
  $ let datePart = parsed.dateText
  $ // If TVGuide packed the episode title as the first sentence, e.g.
  $ // "Thanksgiving. The Taylor family...", recognize it as the episode title
  $ // instead of treating it as synopsis text.
  $ const packedTitle = getPackedEpisodeTitleFromDesc(existing)
  $ if (!titlePart && episodeName && packedTitle && normalize(packedTitle) === normalize(episodeName)) {
  $ titlePart = packedTitle
  $ const firstSentence = bodyPart.match(/^(.+?)(?:\.\s+|$)/)
  $ if (firstSentence) bodyPart = bodyPart.slice(firstSentence[0].length).trim()
  $ }
  $ // If TVGuide already put the episode title in the desc, leave it.
  $ // If it is missing and TVMaze has it, add only that missing title.
  $ if (!titlePart && episodeName && !descHasEpisodeName(existing, episodeName)) {
  $ titlePart = episodeName
  $ }
  $ // If TVGuide already put SxxExx in the desc, leave it.
  $ // If it is missing and TVMaze has it, add only that missing code.
  $ if (!codePart && episodeCode && !descHasEpisodeCode(existing, episode?.season, episode?.number)) {
  $ codePart = episodeCode
  $ }
  $ // If TVGuide already gave a real description body, leave it untouched.
  $ // If the body is missing or the whole desc was only metadata, add only the missing synopsis.
  $ if (!bodyPart || bodyPart === titlePart || bodyPart === codePart || bodyPart === `${titlePart} - ${codePart}`) {
  $ bodyPart = episodeSummary || showSummary || ''
  $ }
  $ // If TVGuide already put the date/year at the end, leave it.
  $ // If it is missing and TVMaze has an airdate, add only that missing date.
  $ if (!datePart && displayDate && !shouldSuppressTvmazeDisplayDate(block, episode?.airdate) && !descHasDisplayDate(existing, episode?.airdate)) {
  $ datePart = displayDate
  $ }
  $ const parts = []
  $ if (titlePart && codePart) parts.push(`${titlePart} - ${codePart}`)
  $ else if (titlePart) parts.push(titlePart)
  $ else if (codePart) parts.push(codePart)
  $ if (bodyPart) {
  $ const bodyNorm = normalize(bodyPart)
  $ const prefixNorm = normalize(parts[0] || '')
  $ if (!prefixNorm || bodyNorm !== prefixNorm) parts.push(bodyPart)
  $ }
  $ let desc = cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(parts.join('. '))
  $ if (datePart) desc = desc ? `${desc} (${datePart})` : `(${datePart})`
  $ return cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(desc)
  $ }
  $ function replaceTag(block, tag, value) {
  $ const escaped = escapeXml(value)
  $ const tagXml = `  <${tag} lang="en">${escaped}</${tag}>`
  $ if (hasTag(block, tag)) {
  $ return block.replace(new RegExp(`<${tag}\\b[^>]*>[\\s\\S]*?<\\/${tag}>`, 'i'), tagXml.trim())
  $ }
  $ return insertBeforeProgrammeEnd(block, tagXml)
  $ }
  $ function insertBeforeProgrammeEnd(block, tagXml) {
  $ return block.replace(/<\/programme>\s*$/i, `${tagXml}\n</programme>`)
  $ }
  $ function enrichBlockMissingOnly(block, show, episode) {
  $ let updated = block
  $ let changed = false
  $ if (!hasAnyCategory(updated)) {
  $ const category = tvmazeShowIsMiniseries(show) ? 'Miniseries' : 'Series'
  $ updated = insertBeforeProgrammeEnd(updated, `  <category lang="en">${category}</category>`)
  $ changed = true
  $ stats.addedCategories++
  $ }
  $ // Field-level fill only:
  $ // Keep every TVGuide-provided desc component that already exists.
  $ // Add only missing episode title, missing SxxExx, missing synopsis, or missing airdate.
  $ // Never replace TVGuide's existing synopsis just because one metadata piece is missing.
  $ const existingDesc = getTag(updated, 'desc')
  $ const packedDesc = buildTvmazePackedDescription(existingDesc, show, episode, updated)
  $ if (packedDesc && packedDesc !== existingDesc) {
  $ updated = replaceTag(updated, 'desc', packedDesc)
  $ changed = true
  $ stats.addedDescriptions++
  $ }
  $ if (changed) stats.enriched++
  $ return updated
  $ }
  $ async function main() {
  $ const parts = xml.split(/(<programme\b[\s\S]*?<\/programme>)/gi)
  $ const out = []
  $ for (const part of parts) {
  $ if (!/^<programme\b/i.test(part)) {
  $ out.push(part)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ stats.programmes++
  $ let block = part
  $ const title = cleanTitleForSearch(getTag(block, 'title'))
  $ if (!title) {
  $ out.push(block)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (isMovie(block)) {
  $ stats.skippedMovies++
  $ out.push(block)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (!needsTvmaze(block)) {
  $ out.push(block)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ stats.searchedShows++
  $ const show = await tvmazeSearchShow(title)
  $ if (!show) {
  $ out.push(block)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (tvmazeShowIsMovieLike(show)) {
  $ stats.skippedMovies++
  $ out.push(block)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ stats.matchedShows++
  $ const episodes = await tvmazeEpisodes(show.id)
  $ const episode = findEpisodeForProgramme(episodes, block)
  $ block = enrichBlockMissingOnly(block, show, episode)
  $ out.push(block)
  $ }
  $ fs.writeFileSync(guidePath, out.join(''))
  $ fs.writeFileSync(cachePath, JSON.stringify(cache, null, 2))
  $ console.log('[TVMAZE] missing-only enrichment complete')
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] programmes scanned: ${stats.programmes}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] movies skipped: ${stats.skippedMovies}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] shows searched: ${stats.searchedShows}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] shows matched: ${stats.matchedShows}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] programmes enriched: ${stats.enriched}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] categories added: ${stats.addedCategories}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] subtitles added: ${stats.addedSubtitles}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] episode nums added: ${stats.addedEpisodeNums}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] dates added: ${stats.addedDates}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] descriptions added: ${stats.addedDescriptions}`)
  $ }
  $ main().catch(err => {
  $ console.error(err)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ })
  $ EOF
  $ CH=$(grep -c '<channel ' guides/OTALocalsguide.xml || true)
  $ PR=$(grep -c '<programme ' guides/OTALocalsguide.xml || true)
  $ DESC=$(grep -c '<desc ' guides/OTALocalsguide.xml || true)
  $ echo "OTA channels: $CH"
  $ echo "OTA programmes: $PR"
  $ echo "OTA descriptions: $DESC"
  $ if [ "$CH" -ne 16 ]; then
  $ echo "Invalid OTA output — expected exactly 16 channels"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ if [ "$PR" -lt 1 ]; then
  $ echo "Invalid OTA output — no programmes"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ if [ "$DESC" -lt 1 ]; then
  $ echo "Invalid OTA output — no descriptions"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ node <<'EOF'
  $ const fs = require('fs')
  $ const rawPath = 'guides/OTALocalsguide.raw.xml'
  $ const guidePath = 'guides/OTALocalsguide.xml'
  $ if (!fs.existsSync(rawPath)) {
  $ console.error(`Missing RAW OTA source: ${rawPath}`)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ if (!fs.existsSync(guidePath)) {
  $ console.error(`Missing generated OTA guide: ${guidePath}`)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ const wanted = {
  $ 'WCBB-DT': 'PBS',
  $ 'WCSH': 'NBC PORTLAND',
  $ 'WGME': 'CBS PORTLAND',
  $ 'WGME-DT2': 'FOX PORTLAND',
  $ 'WPME': 'ION',
  $ 'WIPL-DT2': 'COURT TV',
  $ 'WIPL-DT4': 'GRIT',
  $ 'WIPL-DT5': 'ION PLUS',
  $ 'WIPL-DT7': 'GAME SHOW NETWORK',
  $ 'WMTW': 'ABC PORTLAND',
  $ 'WMTW-DT2': 'METV',
  $ 'WMTW-DT3': 'LAFF',
  $ 'WPFO-DT2': 'CHARGE',
  $ 'WPFO-DT3': 'COMET',
  $ 'WPXT-DT2': 'HEROES & ICONS',
  $ 'WPXT-DT3': 'ION MYSTERY'
  $ }
  $ function decodeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
  $ .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
  $ .replace(/&apos;/g, "'")
  $ .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
  $ .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
  $ }
  $ function escapeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
  $ .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
  $ .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
  $ .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
  $ }
  $ function normalize(s) {
  $ return decodeXml(s)
  $ .toUpperCase()
  $ .replace(/&/g, 'AND')
  $ .replace(/\+/g, 'PLUS')
  $ .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g, ' ')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function channelNames(block) {
  $ return [...block.matchAll(/<display-name[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/display-name>/gi)]
  $ .map(m => decodeXml(m[1].replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim()))
  $ .filter(Boolean)
  $ }
  $ function channelId(block) {
  $ const m = block.match(/<channel\b[^>]*\bid="([^"]+)"/i)
  $ return m ? decodeXml(m[1]).trim() : ''
  $ }
  $ function iconSrc(block) {
  $ const m = block.match(/<icon\b[^>]*src="([^"]+)"/i)
  $ return m ? decodeXml(m[1]).trim() : ''
  $ }
  $ const rawXml = fs.readFileSync(rawPath, 'utf8')
  $ let guideXml = fs.readFileSync(guidePath, 'utf8')
  $ const logosByFinalName = new Map()
  $ const logosById = new Map()
  $ for (const match of rawXml.matchAll(/<channel\b[\s\S]*?<\/channel>/gi)) {
  $ const block = match[0]
  $ const id = channelId(block)
  $ const names = channelNames(block)
  $ const shortName = names[0]
  $ const logo = iconSrc(block)
  $ if (!logo) continue
  $ if (id) logosById.set(normalize(id), logo)
  $ if (shortName && wanted[shortName]) {
  $ logosByFinalName.set(normalize(wanted[shortName]), logo)
  $ }
  $ for (const name of names) {
  $ logosByFinalName.set(normalize(name), logo)
  $ }
  $ }
  $ let scanned = 0
  $ let added = 0
  $ let updated = 0
  $ let missing = 0
  $ guideXml = guideXml.replace(/<channel\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/channel>/gi, block => {
  $ scanned++
  $ const id = channelId(block)
  $ const names = channelNames(block)
  $ const displayName = names[0] || ''
  $ const logo =
  $ logosByFinalName.get(normalize(displayName)) ||
  $ logosById.get(normalize(id))
  $ if (!logo) {
  $ missing++
  $ return block
  $ }
  $ if (/<icon\b[^>]*src="/i.test(block)) {
  $ const next = block.replace(/<icon\b[^>]*src="[^"]*"[^/]*\/>/i, `<icon src="${escapeXml(logo)}"/>`)
  $ if (next !== block) updated++
  $ return next
  $ }
  $ added++
  $ return block.replace(/(<display-name\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/display-name>)/i, `$1<icon src="${escapeXml(logo)}"/>`)
  $ })
  $ fs.writeFileSync(guidePath, guideXml)
  $ console.log(`OTA channels scanned: ${scanned}`)
  $ console.log(`RAW OTA logos available: ${logosByFinalName.size}`)
  $ console.log(`OTA icons added: ${added}`)
  $ console.log(`OTA icons updated: ${updated}`)
  $ console.log(`OTA channels still missing logo: ${missing}`)
  $ if (scanned !== 16) {
  $ console.error(`Expected 16 OTA channels, scanned ${scanned}`)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ if (added + updated < 10) {
  $ console.error('Refusing to continue: expected at least 10 OTA logos to be injected')
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ EOF
  $ gzip -c guides/OTALocalsguide.xml > guides/OTALocalsguide.xml.gz
  $ git config user.name "github-actions"
  $ git config user.email "github-actions@github.com"
  $ git fetch origin main
  $ # Preserve compressed guides created by the other workflows.
  $ # This workflow should only update its own XML/GZ output.
  $ git checkout origin/main -- guides/TVPassport.xml.gz guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml.gz 2>/dev/null || true
  $ git add guides/OTALocalsguide.xml guides/OTALocalsguide.xml.gz guides/.tvmaze-ota-cache.json
  $ git add guides/TVPassport.xml.gz guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml.gz 2>/dev/null || true
  $ if git diff --cached --quiet; then
  $ echo "No OTA guide changes to commit"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ git commit -m "Update OTA guide"
  $ for attempt in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  $ echo "Push attempt $attempt"
  $ if git push origin HEAD:main; then
  $ echo "Push succeeded"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ echo "Push failed, retrying after fresh fetch/rebase..."
  $ git fetch origin main
  $ git rebase origin/main
  $ git checkout origin/main -- guides/TVPassport.xml.gz guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml.gz 2>/dev/null || true
  $ git add guides/OTALocalsguide.xml guides/OTALocalsguide.xml.gz guides/.tvmaze-ota-cache.json
  $ git add guides/TVPassport.xml.gz guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml.gz 2>/dev/null || true
  $ if git diff --cached --quiet; then
  $ echo "No new commit needed after rebase, retrying push..."
  $ else
  $ git commit -m "Update OTA guide"
  $ fi
  $ sleep 5
  $ done
  $ echo "Push failed after retries"
  $ exit 1

TVguide-Tivimate
----------------
Workflow: .github/workflows/festy-tvguide.yml

REMOTE SOURCES:
  - https://api.tvmaze.com/search/shows?q=${encodeURIComponent(title)}`
  - https://api.tvmaze.com/shows/${showId}/episodes`
  - https://backend.tvguide.com/tvschedules/tvguide/${providerId}/web?start=${date.startOf(
  - https://github.com/iptv-org/epg.git
  - https://www.tvguide.com/

FILES REFERENCED BY WORKFLOW:
  - ../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${BEST_PASS}.xml
      Type: DATA / GUIDE
      Status: NOT FOUND IN REPOSITORY
  - ../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS}.descriptions.count

  $ function decodeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
  $ .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
  $ .replace(/&apos;/g, "'")
  $ .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
  $ .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
  $ }
  $ function escapeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
  $ .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
  $ .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
  $ .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
  $ }
  $ function attr(attrs, name) {
  $ const m = attrs.match(new RegExp(`${name}="([^"]*)"`, 'i'))
  $ return m ? decodeXml(m[1]).trim() : ''
  $ }
  $ function cleanBody(body) {
  $ const display = body.match(/<display-name[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/display-name>/i)
  $ if (display) return decodeXml(display[1].replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim())
  $ return decodeXml(body.replace(/<url>[\s\S]*?<\/url>/gi, '').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim())
  $ }
  $ function norm(s) {
  $ return decodeXml(s)
  $ .replace(/\.us$/i, '')
  $ .replace(/&/g, 'AND')
  $ .replace(/\+/g, 'PLUS')
  $ .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/gi, '.')
  $ .replace(/^\.+|\.+$/g, '')
  $ .toUpperCase()
  $ }
  $ function parseChannels(xml) {
  $ return [...xml.matchAll(/<channel\b([^>]*)>([\s\S]*?)<\/channel>/gi)].map(m => {
  $ const attrs = m[1]
  $ const body = m[2]
  $ return {
  $ id: attr(attrs, 'id') || attr(attrs, 'xmltv_id'),
  $ xmltv_id: attr(attrs, 'xmltv_id') || attr(attrs, 'id'),
  $ site_id: attr(attrs, 'site_id') || attr(attrs, 'site-id'),
  $ name: cleanBody(body)
  $ }
  $ })
  $ }
  $ if (!fs.existsSync(sourcePath)) {
  $ console.error(`Missing ${sourcePath}`)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ if (!fs.existsSync(rawPath)) {
  $ console.error(`Missing ${rawPath}`)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ const sourceChannels = parseChannels(fs.readFileSync(sourcePath, 'utf8'))
  $ const rawChannels = parseChannels(fs.readFileSync(rawPath, 'utf8'))
  $ const rawById = new Map()
  $ const rawByName = new Map()
  $ for (const ch of rawChannels) {
  $ if (ch.xmltv_id && ch.site_id) rawById.set(norm(ch.xmltv_id), ch)
  $ if (ch.name && ch.site_id) rawByName.set(norm(ch.name), ch)
  $ }
  $ const out = []
  $ const missing = []
  $ const seen = new Set()

  $ continue
  $ }
  $ const key = `${wantedId}|${siteId}`
  $ if (seen.has(key)) continue
  $ seen.add(key)
  $ out.push(
  $ `  <channel site="tvguide.com" lang="en" xmltv_id="${escapeXml(wantedId)}" site_id="${escapeXml(siteId)}">${escapeXml(wantedName)}</channel>`

  $ module.exports = {

  $ url({ date, channel }) {

  $ }
  $ }
  $ },
  $ async parser({ content, channel, date }) {
  $ const data = JSON.parse(content)
  $ const items = data?.data?.items || []
  $ const sourceId = String(channel.site_id).split('#').pop()
  $ const match = items.find(item =>
  $ String(item.sourceId || item.channelSourceId || item.id || item.channel?.sourceId || '') === sourceId
  $ )
  $ const schedules = match?.programSchedules || []
  $ console.log(`[PULL] ${channel.name} | ${date.format('YYYY-MM-DD')} | programs=${schedules.length}`)
  $ const programs = []
  $ const batchSize = 10
  $ for (let i = 0; i < schedules.length; i += batchSize) {
  $ const batch = schedules.slice(i, i + batchSize)
  $ const results = await Promise.all(
  $ batch.map(async item => {
  $ const details = await loadProgramDetails(item, channel)
  $ const title = cleanTitle(item.title || details.title || '')
  $ const episodeTitle = cleanSpaces(details.episodeTitle || details.subtitle || details.subTitle || '')
  $ const description = cleanSpaces(details.description || item.description || '')

  $ const episodeNumber = details.episodeNumber || details.episode || ''

  $ formatTVGuideDate(details.originalAirDate || details.airDate || details.episodeAirDate) ||
  $ formatUnixDate(details.originalAirTime || details.firstAired)

  $ details,
  $ episodeTitle,
  $ seasonNumber,
  $ episodeNumber,
  $ airDate,
  $ releaseYear
  $ })

  $ details,
  $ airDate

  $ episodeCode,

  $ airDate,
  $ releaseYear,
  $ suppressDisplayDate

  $ title,
  $ description: finalDescription,

  $ stop: dayjs.unix(item.endTime),

  $ // Do not emit separate sub-title, episode-num, or date fields here, because TiviMate
  $ // may use them to rebuild/duplicate display text outside the packed description.
  $ return program
  $ })
  $ )
  $ programs.push(...results)
  $ }
  $ return programs
  $ }
  $ }
  $ function sleep(ms) {
  $ return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms))
  $ }
  $ function isRetryableDetailError(err) {
  $ const status = err.response?.status
  $ const code = err.code
  $ const message = String(err.message || '')
  $ if ([408, 429, 500, 502, 503, 504].includes(status)) return true
  $ if (['ECONNABORTED', 'ETIMEDOUT', 'ECONNRESET', 'ENOTFOUND', 'EAI_AGAIN'].includes(code)) return true
  $ if (/timeout|aborted|network|socket|reset/i.test(message)) return true
  $ return false
  $ }
  $ async function fetchProgramDetailsOnce(url, channelName, title, timeout, label, attempt, attempts) {
  $ try {
  $ const response = await axios.get(url, {
  $ timeout,
  $ headers: {
  $ Referer: 'https://www.tvguide.com/',
  $ 'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 Chrome/131.0.0.0 Safari/537.36'
  $ }
  $ })
  $ if (attempt > 1 || label.includes('MICRO')) {
  $ console.log(`[${label} OK] ${channelName} | ${title} | attempt=${attempt}/${attempts}`)
  $ }
  $ return {
  $ ok: true,
  $ value: response.data?.data?.item || {}
  $ }
  $ } catch (err) {
  $ const status = err.response?.status || err.code || err.message || 'unknown'
  $ if (err.response?.status === 404) {
  $ console.log(`[${label} MISSING] ${channelName} | ${title} | 404`)
  $ return {
  $ ok: true,
  $ value: {}
  $ }
  $ }
  $ return {
  $ ok: false,
  $ retryable: isRetryableDetailError(err),
  $ status
  $ }
  $ }
  $ }
  $ async function tryProgramDetails(url, channelName, title, options) {
  $ const { label, attempts, timeout, baseDelay, maxDelay } = options
  $ for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= attempts; attempt++) {
  $ const result = await fetchProgramDetailsOnce(url, channelName, title, timeout, label, attempt, attempts)
  $ if (result.ok) return result.value
  $ if (!result.retryable) {
  $ console.log(`[${label} FAIL] ${channelName} | ${title} | ${result.status}`)
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ if (attempt < attempts) {
  $ const delay = Math.min(maxDelay, baseDelay * Math.pow(2, attempt - 1)) + Math.floor(Math.random() * 500)
  $ console.log(`[${label} RETRY] ${channelName} | ${title} | ${result.status} | attempt=${attempt}/${attempts} | wait=${delay}ms`)
  $ await sleep(delay)
  $ } else {
  $ console.log(`[${label} EXHAUSTED] ${channelName} | ${title} | ${result.status} | attempts=${attempts}`)
  $ }
  $ }
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ async function loadProgramDetails(item, channel) {
  $ if (!item.programDetails) return {}
  $ const url = item.programDetails.includes('?')
  $ ? `${item.programDetails}&apiKey=DI9elXhZ3bU6ujsA2gXEKOANyncXGUGc`
  $ : `${item.programDetails}?apiKey=DI9elXhZ3bU6ujsA2gXEKOANyncXGUGc`
  $ const channelName = channel?.name || 'Unknown channel'
  $ const title = item.title || 'Unknown title'
  $ const fastResult = await tryProgramDetails(url, channelName, title, {
  $ label: 'DETAIL FAST',
  $ attempts: 1,
  $ timeout: 8000,
  $ baseDelay: 0,
  $ maxDelay: 0
  $ })
  $ if (fastResult) return fastResult
  $ const backupResult = await tryProgramDetails(url, channelName, title, {
  $ label: 'DETAIL BACKUP',
  $ attempts: 5,
  $ timeout: 8000,
  $ baseDelay: 750,
  $ maxDelay: 30000
  $ })
  $ if (backupResult) return backupResult
  $ console.log(`[DETAIL MICRO PASS] ${channelName} | ${title} | starting slow cleanup pass`)
  $ const microResult = await tryProgramDetails(url, channelName, title, {
  $ label: 'DETAIL MICRO',
  $ attempts: 4,
  $ timeout: 20000,
  $ baseDelay: 5000,
  $ maxDelay: 60000
  $ })
  $ if (microResult) return microResult
  $ console.log(`[DETAIL FINAL MISS] ${channelName} | ${title} | no details after fast + backup + micro`)
  $ return {}
  $ }
  $ function classifyProgramType({ item, details, episodeTitle, seasonNumber, episodeNumber, airDate, releaseYear }) {
  $ const haystack = [
  $ item.type,
  $ item.programType,
  $ item.entityType,
  $ item.category,
  $ item.subcategory,
  $ item.genres,
  $ details.type,
  $ details.programType,
  $ details.entityType,
  $ details.category,
  $ details.subcategory,
  $ details.genres,
  $ details.seriesId,
  $ details.showId,
  $ details.episodeId
  $ ]
  $ .flat()
  $ .filter(Boolean)
  $ .join(' ')
  $ .toLowerCase()
  $ const hasEpisodeSignals = Boolean(
  $ episodeTitle ||
  $ seasonNumber ||
  $ episodeNumber ||
  $ details.episodeNumber ||
  $ details.seasonNumber ||
  $ details.episodeTitle ||
  $ details.subtitle ||
  $ details.subTitle ||
  $ details.originalAirDate ||
  $ details.episodeAirDate ||
  $ details.seriesId ||
  $ details.showId ||
  $ details.episodeId
  $ )
  $ if (/mini[\s-]?series|limited series|limited-series/.test(haystack)) return 'miniseries'
  $ if (/series|episode|tv show|television|scripted|reality|documentary series/.test(haystack)) return 'series'
  $ if (hasEpisodeSignals) return 'series'
  $ if (/movie|film|feature film|cinema/.test(haystack)) return 'movie'
  $ const durationMinutes = getDurationMinutes(item)
  $ if (durationMinutes >= 75 && releaseYear && !airDate && !hasEpisodeSignals) return 'movie'
  $ return 'program'
  $ }
  $ function getDurationMinutes(item) {
  $ const start = Number(item.startTime || 0)
  $ const end = Number(item.endTime || 0)
  $ if (!start || !end || end <= start) return 0
  $ return Math.round((end - start) / 60)
  $ }
  $ function buildCategories(programType) {
  $ if (programType === 'movie') return ['Movie']
  $ if (programType === 'miniseries') return ['Miniseries', 'Series']
  $ if (programType === 'series') return ['Series']
  $ return ['Program']
  $ }
  $ function shouldSuppressDisplayDate({ title, description, programType, item, details, airDate }) {
  $ if (!airDate) return false
  $ const haystack = [
  $ title,
  $ description,
  $ item?.title,
  $ item?.type,
  $ item?.programType,
  $ item?.entityType,
  $ item?.category,
  $ item?.subcategory,
  $ item?.genres,
  $ details?.type,
  $ details?.programType,
  $ details?.entityType,
  $ details?.category,
  $ details?.subcategory,
  $ details?.genres
  $ ]
  $ .flat()
  $ .filter(Boolean)
  $ .join(' ')
  $ .toLowerCase()
  $ const liveOrCurrent = /\b(live|news|newscast|weather|sports|game|match|tournament|football|basketball|baseball|hockey|soccer|golf|tennis|racing|wwe|ufc|mma|boxing|pregame|postgame|post-game|pre-game)\b/.test(haystack)
  $ if (!liveOrCurrent) return false
  $ const scheduleDate = item?.startTime ? dayjs.unix(Number(item.startTime)) : null
  $ const parsedAirDate = parseDisplayDate(airDate)
  $ if (!scheduleDate || !scheduleDate.isValid() || !parsedAirDate || !parsedAirDate.isValid()) return true
  $ // For live/current news and sports, the original air date is usually just the broadcast date.
  $ // Suppress it when it is close to the scheduled airing. Keep it when it is genuinely old.
  $ return Math.abs(scheduleDate.diff(parsedAirDate, 'day')) <= 45
  $ }
  $ function parseDisplayDate(value) {
  $ const raw = String(value || '').trim()
  $ const m = raw.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})$/)
  $ if (!m) return dayjs(raw)
  $ return dayjs(`${m[3]}-${m[1].padStart(2, '0')}-${m[2].padStart(2, '0')}`)
  $ }
  $ function buildPackedDescription({ programType, episodeTitle, episodeCode, description, airDate, releaseYear, suppressDisplayDate }) {
  $ const parts = []
  $ let prefix = ''
  $ if (programType === 'series' || programType === 'miniseries') {
  $ if (episodeTitle && episodeCode) prefix = `${episodeTitle} - ${episodeCode}`
  $ else if (episodeTitle) prefix = episodeTitle
  $ else if (episodeCode) prefix = episodeCode
  $ }
  $ if (prefix) parts.push(prefix)
  $ if (description) parts.push(description)
  $ let finalDescription = cleanPackedDescription(parts.join('. '))
  $ if (programType === 'movie') {
  $ if (releaseYear && !descriptionIncludesDisplayDate(finalDescription, releaseYear)) {
  $ finalDescription = finalDescription ? `${finalDescription} (${releaseYear})` : `(${releaseYear})`
  $ }
  $ return finalDescription
  $ }
  $ if (programType === 'series' || programType === 'miniseries') {
  $ if (airDate && !suppressDisplayDate && !descriptionIncludesDisplayDate(finalDescription, airDate)) {
  $ finalDescription = finalDescription ? `${finalDescription} (${airDate})` : `(${airDate})`
  $ }
  $ return finalDescription
  $ }
  $ const dateOrYear = suppressDisplayDate ? releaseYear : (airDate || releaseYear)
  $ if (dateOrYear && !descriptionIncludesDisplayDate(finalDescription, dateOrYear)) {
  $ finalDescription = finalDescription
  $ ? `${finalDescription} (${dateOrYear})`
  $ : `(${dateOrYear})`
  $ }
  $ return finalDescription
  $ }
  $ function cleanPackedDescription(value) {
  $ return cleanSpaces(value)
  $ .replace(/\s+Aired\s+(\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4})\.?\s*$/i, ' ($1)')
  $ .replace(/\s+\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*$/g, ' ($2)')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function descriptionIncludesDisplayDate(description, dateOrYear) {
  $ if (!description || !dateOrYear) return false
  $ const escaped = String(dateOrYear).replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&')
  $ return new RegExp(`\\(${escaped}\\)\\s*$`).test(description) || description.includes(String(dateOrYear))
  $ }
  $ function buildEpisodeCode(seasonNumber, episodeNumber) {
  $ const s = String(seasonNumber || '').trim()
  $ const e = String(episodeNumber || '').trim()
  $ if (!s && !e) return ''
  $ if (s && e) return `S${s}E${e}`
  $ if (e) return `E${e}`
  $ return `S${s}`
  $ }
  $ function cleanTitle(value) {
  $ return String(value || '')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .replace(/^\s*(New|Live|Repeat)\s*[:\-]\s*/i, '')
  $ .replace(/\s*\((New|Live|Repeat)\)\s*$/i, '')
  $ .replace(/\s*[-–—]\s*(New|Live|Repeat)\s*$/i, '')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function cleanSpaces(value) {
  $ return String(value || '')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .replace(/\s+([.,!?;:])/g, '$1')
  $ .replace(/([.,!?;:])([A-Za-z0-9])/g, '$1 $2')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function formatTVGuideDate(value) {
  $ const raw = String(value || '').trim()
  $ if (!raw) return ''
  $ const tvMatch = raw.match(/\/Date\((\d+)\)\//)
  $ if (tvMatch) {
  $ return dayjs(Number(tvMatch[1])).format('MM/DD/YYYY')
  $ }
  $ const parsed = dayjs(raw)
  $ if (parsed.isValid()) return parsed.format('MM/DD/YYYY')
  $ return ''
  $ }
  $ function formatUnixDate(value) {
  $ if (!value) return ''
  $ const parsed = dayjs.unix(Number(value))
  $ return parsed.isValid() ? parsed.format('MM/DD/YYYY') : ''
  $ }
  $ function normalizeXmltvDate(value) {
  $ const parsed = dayjs(value)
  $ if (!parsed.isValid()) return ''
  $ return parsed.format('YYYYMMDD')
  $ }

  $ PASS_NAME="$1"
  $ MAX_CONNECTIONS="$2"

  $ echo ""

  $ --channels=sites/tvguide.com/festy-tivimate.channels.xml \
  $ --output="../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS_NAME}.xml" \
  $ --days=14 \
  $ --maxConnections="$MAX_CONNECTIONS" \
  $ 2>&1 | tee "../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS_NAME}.log"
  $ STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}
  $ set -e
  $ if [ "$STATUS" -ne 0 ]; then
  $ echo "$PASS_NAME exited with status $STATUS"
  $ fi
  $ if [ ! -f "../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS_NAME}.xml" ]; then
  $ echo "$PASS_NAME did not create an XML file"
  $ echo "0" > "../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS_NAME}.programmes.count"
  $ echo "0" > "../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS_NAME}.descriptions.count"
  $ echo "999999" > "../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS_NAME}.issues.count"
  $ return 0
  $ fi
  $ PR=$(grep -c '<programme ' "../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS_NAME}.xml" || true)
  $ DESC=$(grep -c '<desc ' "../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS_NAME}.xml" || true)
  $ ISSUE_COUNT=$(grep -Eic 'ERR:|timeout|programs=0|ECONNABORTED|ETIMEDOUT|ECONNRESET|ENOTFOUND|EAI_AGAIN| 408 | 429 | 500 | 502 | 503 | 504 ' "../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS_NAME}.log" || true)

  $ echo "$DESC" > "../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS_NAME}.descriptions.count"
  $ echo "$ISSUE_COUNT" > "../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS_NAME}.issues.count"

  $ echo "$PASS_NAME descriptions: $DESC"
  $ echo "$PASS_NAME possible schedule/detail issues: $ISSUE_COUNT"
  $ }

  $ PASS1_ISSUES=$(cat ../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.pass1-fast.issues.count)
  $ if [ "$PASS1_ISSUES" -gt 0 ]; then
  $ echo ""
  $ echo "Fast pass had possible issues, running backup schedule pass..."
  $ run_pass "pass2-backup" 5 15000
  $ else
  $ echo ""
  $ echo "Fast pass had no obvious schedule issues. Skipping backup pass."
  $ fi
  $ BEST_PASS="pass1-fast"
  $ BEST_PR=$(cat ../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.pass1-fast.programmes.count)
  $ BEST_DESC=$(cat ../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.pass1-fast.descriptions.count)
  $ BEST_ISSUES=$(cat ../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.pass1-fast.issues.count)

  $ PASS2_ISSUES=$(cat ../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.pass2-backup.issues.count)
  $ if [ "$PASS2_PR" -gt "$BEST_PR" ]; then
  $ BEST_PASS="pass2-backup"
  $ BEST_PR="$PASS2_PR"
  $ BEST_DESC="$PASS2_DESC"
  $ BEST_ISSUES="$PASS2_ISSUES"
  $ elif [ "$PASS2_PR" -eq "$BEST_PR" ] && [ "$PASS2_DESC" -gt "$BEST_DESC" ]; then
  $ BEST_PASS="pass2-backup"
  $ BEST_PR="$PASS2_PR"
  $ BEST_DESC="$PASS2_DESC"
  $ BEST_ISSUES="$PASS2_ISSUES"
  $ elif [ "$PASS2_PR" -eq "$BEST_PR" ] && [ "$PASS2_DESC" -eq "$BEST_DESC" ] && [ "$PASS2_ISSUES" -lt "$BEST_ISSUES" ]; then
  $ BEST_PASS="pass2-backup"
  $ BEST_PR="$PASS2_PR"
  $ BEST_DESC="$PASS2_DESC"
  $ BEST_ISSUES="$PASS2_ISSUES"
  $ fi
  $ fi

  $ run_pass "pass3-micro" 2 30000
  $ else
  $ echo ""
  $ echo "Best pass has no obvious schedule issues. Skipping micro schedule pass."
  $ fi
  $ BEST_PASS="pass1-fast"
  $ BEST_PR=$(cat ../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.pass1-fast.programmes.count)
  $ BEST_DESC=$(cat ../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.pass1-fast.descriptions.count)
  $ BEST_ISSUES=$(cat ../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.pass1-fast.issues.count)
  $ for PASS in pass2-backup pass3-micro; do
  $ if [ -f "../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS}.programmes.count" ]; then

  $ BEST_PASS="$PASS"
  $ BEST_PR="$CANDIDATE_PR"
  $ BEST_DESC="$CANDIDATE_DESC"
  $ BEST_ISSUES="$CANDIDATE_ISSUES"
  $ elif [ "$CANDIDATE_PR" -eq "$BEST_PR" ] && [ "$CANDIDATE_DESC" -gt "$BEST_DESC" ]; then

  $ BEST_PR="$CANDIDATE_PR"

  $ BEST_PR="$CANDIDATE_PR"
  $ BEST_DESC="$CANDIDATE_DESC"
  $ BEST_ISSUES="$CANDIDATE_ISSUES"

  $ fi
  $ done
  $ echo ""
  $ echo "============================================================"
  $ echo "Selected best schedule pass: $BEST_PASS"
  $ echo "Best programmes: $BEST_PR"
  $ echo "Best descriptions: $BEST_DESC"
  $ echo "Best possible issues: $BEST_ISSUES"
  $ echo "============================================================"
  $ cp "../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${BEST_PASS}.xml" "../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml"

  $ if [ -f "../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS}.programmes.count" ]; then

  $ echo "  programmes:   $(cat ../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS}.programmes.count)"
  $ echo "  descriptions: $(cat ../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS}.descriptions.count)"
  $ echo "  issues:       $(cat ../guides/tvguidetivimateguide.${PASS}.issues.count)"
  $ fi
  $ done
  $ cd ..

  $ const fs = require('fs')
  $ const guidePath = 'guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml'
  $ const cachePath = 'guides/.tvmaze-tivimate-cache.json'
  $ if (!fs.existsSync(guidePath)) {
  $ console.error(`Missing ${guidePath}`)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ const xml = fs.readFileSync(guidePath, 'utf8')
  $ let cache = {}
  $ if (fs.existsSync(cachePath)) {
  $ try {
  $ cache = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(cachePath, 'utf8'))
  $ } catch {
  $ cache = {}
  $ }
  $ }
  $ const stats = {
  $ programmes: 0,
  $ skippedMovies: 0,
  $ searchedShows: 0,
  $ matchedShows: 0,
  $ enriched: 0,
  $ addedCategories: 0,
  $ addedSubtitles: 0,
  $ addedEpisodeNums: 0,
  $ addedDates: 0,
  $ addedDescriptions: 0
  $ }
  $ function sleep(ms) {
  $ return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms))
  $ }
  $ function escapeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
  $ .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
  $ .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
  $ .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
  $ }
  $ function decodeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
  $ .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
  $ .replace(/&apos;/g, "'")
  $ .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
  $ .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
  $ }
  $ function stripTags(s) {
  $ return decodeXml(String(s || '').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim())
  $ }
  $ function getTag(block, tag) {
  $ const m = block.match(new RegExp(`<${tag}\\b[^>]*>([\\s\\S]*?)<\\/${tag}>`, 'i'))
  $ return m ? stripTags(m[1]) : ''
  $ }
  $ function getTags(block, tag) {
  $ return [...block.matchAll(new RegExp(`<${tag}\\b[^>]*>([\\s\\S]*?)<\\/${tag}>`, 'gi'))]
  $ .map(m => stripTags(m[1]))
  $ .filter(Boolean)
  $ }
  $ function hasTag(block, tag) {
  $ return new RegExp(`<${tag}\\b`, 'i').test(block)
  $ }
  $ function hasCategory(block, category) {
  $ return getTags(block, 'category').some(c => c.toLowerCase() === category.toLowerCase())
  $ }
  $ function hasAnyCategory(block) {
  $ return hasTag(block, 'category')
  $ }
  $ function getStartDate(block) {
  $ const m = block.match(/start="(\d{8})/i)
  $ if (!m) return ''
  $ return `${m[1].slice(0, 4)}-${m[1].slice(4, 6)}-${m[1].slice(6, 8)}`
  $ }
  $ function cleanTitleForSearch(title) {
  $ return String(title || '')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .replace(/^\s*(New|Live|Repeat)\s*[:\-]\s*/i, '')
  $ .replace(/\s*\((New|Live|Repeat)\)\s*$/i, '')
  $ .replace(/\s*[-–—]\s*(New|Live|Repeat)\s*$/i, '')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function isMovie(block) {
  $ if (hasCategory(block, 'Movie')) return true
  $ if (hasCategory(block, 'Film')) return true
  $ return false
  $ }
  $ function needsTvmaze(block) {
  $ if (!hasAnyCategory(block)) return true
  $ const desc = getTag(block, 'desc')
  $ if (!desc) return true
  $ // Strict rule: TVMaze may only fill fields TVGuide did not find.
  $ // It must not rebuild descriptions because an episode title/code is missing.
  $ // The only packed-desc edit allowed after TVGuide is appending a missing airdate.
  $ const hasPackedDisplayDate = /\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*$/.test(desc)
  $ if (!hasPackedDisplayDate) return true
  $ return false
  $ }
  $ function tvmazeShowIsMovieLike(show) {
  $ const type = String(show?.type || '').toLowerCase()
  $ return type === 'movie' || type === 'film'
  $ }
  $ function tvmazeShowIsMiniseries(show) {
  $ const type = String(show?.type || '').toLowerCase()
  $ const name = String(show?.name || '').toLowerCase()
  $ const summary = String(show?.summary || '').toLowerCase()
  $ return (
  $ type.includes('mini') ||
  $ type.includes('limited') ||
  $ name.includes('miniseries') ||
  $ summary.includes('miniseries') ||
  $ summary.includes('limited series')
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function normalize(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .toLowerCase()
  $ .replace(/&/g, 'and')
  $ .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, ' ')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function scoreShow(title, show) {
  $ const a = normalize(title)
  $ const b = normalize(show?.name || '')
  $ if (!a || !b) return 0
  $ if (a === b) return 100
  $ if (a.includes(b) || b.includes(a)) return 80
  $ return 0
  $ }
  $ async function tvmazeSearchShow(title) {
  $ const key = `show:${normalize(title)}`
  $ if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(cache, key)) return cache[key]
  $ const url = `https://api.tvmaze.com/search/shows?q=${encodeURIComponent(title)}`
  $ const result = await fetchJsonWithRetries(url, `search ${title}`)
  $ const candidates = Array.isArray(result) ? result.map(x => x.show).filter(Boolean) : []
  $ let best = null
  $ let bestScore = 0
  $ for (const show of candidates) {
  $ if (tvmazeShowIsMovieLike(show)) continue
  $ const score = scoreShow(title, show)
  $ if (score > bestScore) {
  $ best = show
  $ bestScore = score
  $ }
  $ }
  $ if (bestScore < 80) best = null
  $ cache[key] = best
  $ await sleep(250)
  $ return best
  $ }
  $ async function tvmazeEpisodes(showId) {
  $ const key = `episodes:${showId}`
  $ if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(cache, key)) return cache[key]
  $ const url = `https://api.tvmaze.com/shows/${showId}/episodes`
  $ const episodes = await fetchJsonWithRetries(url, `episodes ${showId}`)
  $ cache[key] = Array.isArray(episodes) ? episodes : []
  $ await sleep(250)
  $ return cache[key]
  $ }
  $ async function fetchJsonWithRetries(url, label) {
  $ const attempts = 4
  $ for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= attempts; attempt++) {
  $ try {
  $ const response = await fetch(url, {
  $ headers: {
  $ 'User-Agent': 'festy-epg-tvmaze-tivimate-fill-missing-only/1.0'
  $ }
  $ })
  $ if (response.status === 404) return null
  $ if (response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) {
  $ throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`)
  $ }
  $ if (!response.ok) return null
  $ return await response.json()
  $ } catch (err) {
  $ if (attempt === attempts) {
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE FAIL] ${label} | ${err.message}`)
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ const delay = 1000 * attempt + Math.floor(Math.random() * 500)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE RETRY] ${label} | ${err.message} | attempt=${attempt}/${attempts}`)
  $ await sleep(delay)
  $ }
  $ }
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ function findEpisodeForProgramme(episodes, block) {
  $ const scheduleDate = getStartDate(block)
  $ const subtitle = getTag(block, 'sub-title')
  $ const desc = getTag(block, 'desc')
  $ const codeMatch = desc.match(/\bS(\d+)E(\d+)\b/i)
  $ if (codeMatch) {
  $ const season = Number(codeMatch[1])
  $ const number = Number(codeMatch[2])
  $ const byCode = episodes.find(ep => Number(ep.season) === season && Number(ep.number) === number)
  $ if (byCode) return byCode
  $ }
  $ if (subtitle) {
  $ const subNorm = normalize(subtitle)
  $ const byName = episodes.find(ep => normalize(ep.name) === subNorm)
  $ if (byName) return byName
  $ }
  $ const packedTitle = getPackedEpisodeTitleFromDesc(desc)
  $ if (packedTitle) {
  $ const packedNorm = normalize(packedTitle)
  $ const byPackedTitle = episodes.find(ep => normalize(ep.name) === packedNorm)
  $ if (byPackedTitle) return byPackedTitle
  $ }
  $ // Last fallback only. The schedule date is the current rerun date, so it is usually
  $ // not the original TVMaze airdate for syndicated/retro channels.
  $ if (scheduleDate) {
  $ const exact = episodes.find(ep => ep.airdate === scheduleDate)
  $ if (exact) return exact
  $ }
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ function getPackedEpisodeTitleFromDesc(desc) {
  $ const cleaned = cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(desc)
  $ .replace(/\s*\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*$/, '')
  $ .trim()
  $ if (!cleaned) return ''
  $ const firstSentence = cleaned.match(/^(.+?)(?:\.\s+|$)/)
  $ const first = firstSentence ? firstSentence[1].trim() : ''
  $ if (!first) return ''
  $ const prefixMatch = first.match(/^(.+?)\s+-\s+(S\d+E\d+|S\d+|E\d+)$/i)
  $ if (prefixMatch) return prefixMatch[1].trim()
  $ if (/^S\d+E\d+$/i.test(first)) return ''
  $ if (first.length > 80) return ''
  $ return first
  $ }
  $ function xmltvNs(season, episode) {
  $ const s = Number(season)
  $ const e = Number(episode)
  $ if (!Number.isFinite(s) || !Number.isFinite(e) || s < 1 || e < 1) return ''
  $ return `${s - 1}.${e - 1}.`
  $ }
  $ function formatDateXmltv(date) {
  $ if (!date || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date)) return ''
  $ return date.replace(/-/g, '')
  $ }
  $ function cleanTvmazeSummary(value) {
  $ return decodeXml(String(value || '')
  $ .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .trim())
  $ }
  $ function formatDateDisplay(date) {
  $ if (!date || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date)) return ''
  $ return `${date.slice(5, 7)}/${date.slice(8, 10)}/${date.slice(0, 4)}`
  $ }
  $ function buildTvmazeEpisodeCode(season, episode) {
  $ const s = Number(season)
  $ const e = Number(episode)
  $ if (!Number.isFinite(s) || !Number.isFinite(e) || s < 1 || e < 1) return ''
  $ return `S${s}E${e}`
  $ }
  $ function descHasEpisodeName(desc, episodeName) {
  $ if (!desc || !episodeName) return false
  $ const descNorm = normalize(desc)
  $ const nameNorm = normalize(episodeName)
  $ return Boolean(nameNorm && descNorm.includes(nameNorm))
  $ }
  $ function descHasEpisodeCode(desc, season, episode) {
  $ const code = buildTvmazeEpisodeCode(season, episode)
  $ if (!desc || !code) return false
  $ return new RegExp(`\\b${code}\\b`, 'i').test(desc)
  $ }
  $ function descHasDisplayDate(desc, airdate) {
  $ const displayDate = formatDateDisplay(airdate)
  $ if (!desc || !displayDate) return false
  $ return desc.includes(`(${displayDate})`) || desc.includes(displayDate)
  $ }
  $ function shouldSuppressTvmazeDisplayDate(block, airdate) {
  $ if (!airdate) return false
  $ const title = getTag(block, 'title')
  $ const desc = getTag(block, 'desc')
  $ const categories = getTags(block, 'category')
  $ const haystack = [title, desc, categories]
  $ .flat()
  $ .filter(Boolean)
  $ .join(' ')
  $ .toLowerCase()
  $ const liveOrCurrent = /\b(live|news|newscast|weather|sports|sporting|game|match|tournament|football|basketball|baseball|hockey|soccer|golf|tennis|racing|wwe|ufc|mma|boxing|pregame|postgame|post-game|pre-game|paid programming|infomercial)\b/.test(haystack)
  $ if (!liveOrCurrent) return false
  $ const scheduleDateText = getStartDate(block)
  $ if (!scheduleDateText || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(scheduleDateText) || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(airdate)) return true
  $ const scheduleDate = new Date(`${scheduleDateText}T00:00:00Z`)
  $ const parsedAirDate = new Date(`${airdate}T00:00:00Z`)
  $ if (Number.isNaN(scheduleDate.getTime()) || Number.isNaN(parsedAirDate.getTime())) return true
  $ // Match the original guide pass: for live/current news, sports, paid programming,
  $ // and similar listings, suppress dates when TVMaze only found the current airing date.
  $ return Math.abs(scheduleDate.getTime() - parsedAirDate.getTime()) / 86400000 <= 45
  $ }
  $ function cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(value) {
  $ return String(value || '')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .replace(/\s+([.,!?;:])/g, '$1')
  $ .replace(/([.,!?;:])([A-Za-z0-9])/g, '$1 $2')
  $ .replace(/\s+Aired\s+(\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4})\.?\s*$/i, ' ($1)')
  $ .replace(/\s+\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*$/g, ' ($2)')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function splitTrailingDisplayDate(desc) {
  $ const cleaned = cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(desc)
  $ const match = cleaned.match(/\s*\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*$/)
  $ if (!match) return { body: cleaned, dateText: '' }
  $ return {
  $ body: cleaned.slice(0, match.index).trim(),
  $ dateText: match[1]
  $ }
  $ }
  $ function parsePackedDescription(desc) {
  $ const split = splitTrailingDisplayDate(desc)
  $ const withoutDate = split.body
  $ const firstSentence = withoutDate.match(/^(.+?)(?:\.\s+|$)/)
  $ const first = firstSentence ? firstSentence[1].trim() : ''
  $ const rest = firstSentence && firstSentence[0].length < withoutDate.length
  $ ? withoutDate.slice(firstSentence[0].length).trim()
  $ : ''
  $ const prefixMatch = first.match(/^(.+?)\s+-\s+(S\d+E\d+|S\d+|E\d+)$/i)
  $ if (prefixMatch) {
  $ return {
  $ episodeTitle: prefixMatch[1].trim(),
  $ episodeCode: prefixMatch[2].toUpperCase(),
  $ body: rest,
  $ dateText: split.dateText,
  $ hadPrefix: true
  $ }
  $ }
  $ const codeOnlyMatch = first.match(/^(S\d+E\d+|S\d+|E\d+)$/i)
  $ if (codeOnlyMatch) {
  $ return {
  $ episodeTitle: '',
  $ episodeCode: codeOnlyMatch[1].toUpperCase(),
  $ body: rest,
  $ dateText: split.dateText,
  $ hadPrefix: true
  $ }
  $ }
  $ // A first sentence that exactly matches the TVMaze episode name is an existing title-only prefix.
  $ return {
  $ episodeTitle: '',
  $ episodeCode: '',
  $ body: withoutDate,
  $ dateText: split.dateText,
  $ hadPrefix: false
  $ }
  $ }
  $ function buildTvmazePackedDescription(existingDesc, show, episode, block) {
  $ const existing = cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(existingDesc)
  $ const parsed = parsePackedDescription(existing)
  $ const episodeName = cleanTvmazeSummary(episode?.name || '')
  $ const episodeCode = buildTvmazeEpisodeCode(episode?.season, episode?.number)
  $ const episodeSummary = cleanTvmazeSummary(episode?.summary || '')
  $ const showSummary = cleanTvmazeSummary(show?.summary || '')
  $ const displayDate = formatDateDisplay(episode?.airdate)
  $ let titlePart = parsed.episodeTitle
  $ let codePart = parsed.episodeCode
  $ let bodyPart = parsed.body
  $ let datePart = parsed.dateText
  $ // If TVGuide packed the episode title as the first sentence, e.g.
  $ // "Thanksgiving. The Taylor family...", recognize it as the episode title
  $ // instead of treating it as synopsis text.
  $ const packedTitle = getPackedEpisodeTitleFromDesc(existing)
  $ if (!titlePart && episodeName && packedTitle && normalize(packedTitle) === normalize(episodeName)) {
  $ titlePart = packedTitle
  $ const firstSentence = bodyPart.match(/^(.+?)(?:\.\s+|$)/)
  $ if (firstSentence) bodyPart = bodyPart.slice(firstSentence[0].length).trim()
  $ }
  $ // If TVGuide already put the episode title in the desc, leave it.
  $ // If it is missing and TVMaze has it, add only that missing title.
  $ if (!titlePart && episodeName && !descHasEpisodeName(existing, episodeName)) {
  $ titlePart = episodeName
  $ }
  $ // If TVGuide already put SxxExx in the desc, leave it.
  $ // If it is missing and TVMaze has it, add only that missing code.
  $ if (!codePart && episodeCode && !descHasEpisodeCode(existing, episode?.season, episode?.number)) {
  $ codePart = episodeCode
  $ }
  $ // If TVGuide already gave a real description body, leave it untouched.
  $ // If the body is missing or the whole desc was only metadata, add only the missing synopsis.
  $ if (!bodyPart || bodyPart === titlePart || bodyPart === codePart || bodyPart === `${titlePart} - ${codePart}`) {
  $ bodyPart = episodeSummary || showSummary || ''
  $ }
  $ // If TVGuide already put the date/year at the end, leave it.
  $ // If it is missing and TVMaze has an airdate, add only that missing date.
  $ if (!datePart && displayDate && !shouldSuppressTvmazeDisplayDate(block, episode?.airdate) && !descHasDisplayDate(existing, episode?.airdate)) {
  $ datePart = displayDate
  $ }
  $ const parts = []
  $ if (titlePart && codePart) parts.push(`${titlePart} - ${codePart}`)
  $ else if (titlePart) parts.push(titlePart)
  $ else if (codePart) parts.push(codePart)
  $ if (bodyPart) {
  $ const bodyNorm = normalize(bodyPart)
  $ const prefixNorm = normalize(parts[0] || '')
  $ if (!prefixNorm || bodyNorm !== prefixNorm) parts.push(bodyPart)
  $ }
  $ let desc = cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(parts.join('. '))
  $ if (datePart) desc = desc ? `${desc} (${datePart})` : `(${datePart})`
  $ return cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(desc)
  $ }
  $ function replaceTag(block, tag, value) {
  $ const escaped = escapeXml(value)
  $ const tagXml = `  <${tag} lang="en">${escaped}</${tag}>`
  $ if (hasTag(block, tag)) {
  $ return block.replace(new RegExp(`<${tag}\\b[^>]*>[\\s\\S]*?<\\/${tag}>`, 'i'), tagXml.trim())
  $ }
  $ return insertBeforeProgrammeEnd(block, tagXml)
  $ }
  $ function insertBeforeProgrammeEnd(block, tagXml) {
  $ return block.replace(/<\/programme>\s*$/i, `${tagXml}\n</programme>`)
  $ }
  $ function enrichBlockMissingOnly(block, show, episode) {
  $ let updated = block
  $ let changed = false
  $ if (!hasAnyCategory(updated)) {
  $ const category = tvmazeShowIsMiniseries(show) ? 'Miniseries' : 'Series'
  $ updated = insertBeforeProgrammeEnd(updated, `  <category lang="en">${category}</category>`)
  $ changed = true
  $ stats.addedCategories++
  $ }
  $ // Field-level fill only:
  $ // Keep every TVGuide-provided desc component that already exists.
  $ // Add only missing episode title, missing SxxExx, missing synopsis, or missing airdate.
  $ // Never replace TVGuide's existing synopsis just because one metadata piece is missing.
  $ const existingDesc = getTag(updated, 'desc')
  $ const packedDesc = buildTvmazePackedDescription(existingDesc, show, episode, updated)
  $ if (packedDesc && packedDesc !== existingDesc) {
  $ updated = replaceTag(updated, 'desc', packedDesc)
  $ changed = true
  $ stats.addedDescriptions++
  $ }
  $ if (changed) stats.enriched++
  $ return updated
  $ }
  $ async function main() {
  $ const parts = xml.split(/(<programme\b[\s\S]*?<\/programme>)/gi)
  $ const out = []
  $ for (const part of parts) {
  $ if (!/^<programme\b/i.test(part)) {
  $ out.push(part)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ stats.programmes++
  $ let block = part
  $ const title = cleanTitleForSearch(getTag(block, 'title'))
  $ if (!title) {
  $ out.push(block)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (isMovie(block)) {
  $ stats.skippedMovies++
  $ out.push(block)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (!needsTvmaze(block)) {
  $ out.push(block)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ stats.searchedShows++
  $ const show = await tvmazeSearchShow(title)
  $ if (!show) {
  $ out.push(block)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (tvmazeShowIsMovieLike(show)) {
  $ stats.skippedMovies++
  $ out.push(block)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ stats.matchedShows++
  $ const episodes = await tvmazeEpisodes(show.id)
  $ const episode = findEpisodeForProgramme(episodes, block)
  $ block = enrichBlockMissingOnly(block, show, episode)
  $ out.push(block)
  $ }
  $ fs.writeFileSync(guidePath, out.join(''))
  $ fs.writeFileSync(cachePath, JSON.stringify(cache, null, 2))
  $ console.log('[TVMAZE] missing-only enrichment complete')
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] programmes scanned: ${stats.programmes}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] movies skipped: ${stats.skippedMovies}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] shows searched: ${stats.searchedShows}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] shows matched: ${stats.matchedShows}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] programmes enriched: ${stats.enriched}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] categories added: ${stats.addedCategories}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] subtitles added: ${stats.addedSubtitles}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] episode nums added: ${stats.addedEpisodeNums}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] dates added: ${stats.addedDates}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] descriptions added: ${stats.addedDescriptions}`)
  $ }
  $ main().catch(err => {
  $ console.error(err)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ })
  $ EOF
  $ CH=$(grep -c '<channel ' guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml || true)
  $ PR=$(grep -c '<programme ' guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml || true)
  $ DESC=$(grep -c '<desc ' guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml || true)
  $ echo "TiviMate channels: $CH"
  $ echo "TiviMate programmes: $PR"
  $ echo "TiviMate descriptions: $DESC"
  $ if [ "$CH" -lt 123 ]; then
  $ echo "Invalid TiviMate output - expected at least 123 channels"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ if [ "$PR" -lt 1 ]; then
  $ echo "Invalid TiviMate output - no programmes"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ if [ "$DESC" -lt 1 ]; then
  $ echo "Invalid TiviMate output - no descriptions"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ node <<'EOF'
  $ const fs = require('fs')
  $ const curatedPath = 'guides/tvguidetivimate.xml'
  $ const guidePath = 'guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml'
  $ if (!fs.existsSync(curatedPath)) {
  $ console.error(`Missing curated channel list: ${curatedPath}`)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ if (!fs.existsSync(guidePath)) {
  $ console.error(`Missing generated guide: ${guidePath}`)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ function decodeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
  $ .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
  $ .replace(/&apos;/g, "'")
  $ .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
  $ .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
  $ }
  $ function escapeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
  $ .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
  $ .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
  $ .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
  $ }
  $ function attr(attrs, name) {
  $ const m = attrs.match(new RegExp(`${name}="([^"]*)"`, 'i'))
  $ return m ? decodeXml(m[1]).trim() : ''
  $ }
  $ function normalize(s) {
  $ return decodeXml(s)
  $ .toLowerCase()
  $ .replace(/&/g, 'and')
  $ .replace(/\+/g, 'plus')
  $ .replace(/\.us$/i, '')
  $ .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, ' ')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function channelName(block) {
  $ const m = block.match(/<display-name\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/display-name>/i)
  $ return m ? decodeXml(m[1].replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim()) : ''
  $ }
  $ const curatedXml = fs.readFileSync(curatedPath, 'utf8')
  $ let guideXml = fs.readFileSync(guidePath, 'utf8')
  $ const logoById = new Map()
  $ const logoByName = new Map()
  $ for (const match of curatedXml.matchAll(/<channel\b([^>]*)>([\s\S]*?)<\/channel>/gi)) {
  $ const attrs = match[1]
  $ const body = match[2]
  $ const id = attr(attrs, 'id') || attr(attrs, 'xmltv_id')
  $ const name = channelName(match[0])
  $ const iconMatch = match[0].match(/<icon\b[^>]*src="([^"]+)"/i)
  $ if (!iconMatch) continue
  $ const logo = decodeXml(iconMatch[1]).trim()
  $ if (!logo) continue
  $ if (id) logoById.set(id, logo)
  $ if (id) logoById.set(decodeXml(id).replace(/&amp;/g, '&'), logo)
  $ if (name) logoByName.set(normalize(name), logo)
  $ }
  $ let scanned = 0
  $ let added = 0
  $ let updated = 0
  $ let missing = 0
  $ guideXml = guideXml.replace(/<channel\b([^>]*)>([\s\S]*?)<\/channel>/gi, (block, attrs) => {
  $ scanned++
  $ const id = attr(attrs, 'id') || attr(attrs, 'xmltv_id')
  $ const name = channelName(block)
  $ const logo =
  $ logoById.get(id) ||
  $ logoById.get(decodeXml(id).replace(/&amp;/g, '&')) ||
  $ logoByName.get(normalize(name))
  $ if (!logo) {
  $ missing++
  $ return block
  $ }
  $ if (/<icon\b[^>]*src="/i.test(block)) {
  $ const next = block.replace(/<icon\b[^>]*src="[^"]*"[^/]*\/>/i, `<icon src="${escapeXml(logo)}"/>`)
  $ if (next !== block) updated++
  $ return next
  $ }
  $ added++
  $ return block.replace(/(<display-name\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/display-name>)/i, `$1<icon src="${escapeXml(logo)}"/>`)
  $ })
  $ fs.writeFileSync(guidePath, guideXml)
  $ console.log(`Generated guide channels scanned: ${scanned}`)
  $ console.log(`Curated logos available: ${logoById.size}`)
  $ console.log(`Icons added to generated guide: ${added}`)
  $ console.log(`Icons updated in generated guide: ${updated}`)
  $ console.log(`Generated guide channels still without curated logo match: ${missing}`)
  $ if (added + updated < 100) {
  $ console.error('Refusing to continue: expected at least 100 curated logos to be injected into generated guide')
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ EOF
  $ perl -0pi -e 's/Mayday: Air Disaster/Air Disasters/g' guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml
  $ gzip -c guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml > guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml.gz
  $ git config user.name "github-actions"
  $ git config user.email "github-actions@github.com"
  $ git fetch origin main
  $ # Preserve compressed guides created by the other workflows.
  $ # This workflow should only update its own XML/GZ output.
  $ git checkout origin/main -- guides/TVPassport.xml.gz guides/OTALocalsguide.xml.gz 2>/dev/null || true
  $ git add guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml.gz
  $ git add guides/TVPassport.xml.gz guides/OTALocalsguide.xml.gz 2>/dev/null || true
  $ if git diff --cached --quiet; then
  $ echo "No TiviMate guide changes to commit"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ git commit -m "Update TiviMate guide"
  $ for attempt in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  $ echo "Push attempt $attempt"
  $ if git push origin HEAD:main; then
  $ echo "Push succeeded"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ echo "Push failed, retrying after fresh fetch/rebase..."
  $ git fetch origin main
  $ git rebase origin/main
  $ git checkout origin/main -- guides/TVPassport.xml.gz guides/OTALocalsguide.xml.gz 2>/dev/null || true
  $ git add guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml guides/tvguidetivimateguide.xml.gz
  $ git add guides/TVPassport.xml.gz guides/OTALocalsguide.xml.gz 2>/dev/null || true
  $ if git diff --cached --quiet; then
  $ echo "No new commit needed after rebase, retrying push..."
  $ else
  $ git commit -m "Update TiviMate guide"
  $ fi
  $ sleep 5
  $ done
  $ echo "Push failed after retries"
  $ exit 1
