Get Matched Channels
--------------------
Workflow: .github/workflows/get-matched-channels.yml

COMMANDS EXECUTED:
  $ echo "Provider credentials loaded"
  $ if [ -z "$XTREAM_URL" ]; then
  $ echo "XTREAM_URL missing"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ if [ -z "$XTREAM_USERNAME" ]; then
  $ echo "XTREAM_USERNAME missing"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ if [ -z "$XTREAM_PASSWORD" ]; then
  $ echo "XTREAM_PASSWORD missing"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ echo "XTREAM_URL exists"
  $ echo "XTREAM_USERNAME exists"
  $ echo "XTREAM_PASSWORD exists"
  $ python -m pip install requests
  $ python - <<'PY'
  $ import os
  $ import re
  $ import time
  $ import requests
  $ FILE = "matcheverychannel.txt"
  $ XTREAM_URL = os.environ["XTREAM_URL"].rstrip("/")
  $ USERNAME = os.environ["XTREAM_USERNAME"]
  $ PASSWORD = os.environ["XTREAM_PASSWORD"]
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ # Normalize Xtream URL
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ if XTREAM_URL.startswith("https://"):
  $ XTREAM_URL = XTREAM_URL.replace(
  $ "https://",
  $ "http://"
  $ )
  $ if ":80" not in XTREAM_URL and ":443" not in XTREAM_URL:
  $ XTREAM_URL += ":80"
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ # Read selected groups
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ selected_category_ids = set()
  $ with open(
  $ FILE,
  $ "r",
  $ encoding="utf-8"
  $ ) as file:
  $ for line in file:
  $ line = line.strip()
  $ if not line:
  $ continue
  $ # Existing group lines look like:
  $ #
  $ # [188] 24/7 HD
  $ #
  $ # or:
  $ #
  $ # 188 | 188 |  | ###### 24/7 HD ######
  $ match = re.match(
  $ r"^\[(\d+)\]",
  $ line
  $ )
  $ if match:
  $ selected_category_ids.add(
  $ match.group(1)
  $ )
  $ continue
  $ match = re.match(
  $ r"^(\d+)\s*\|\s*\1\s*\|\s*\|\s*######",
  $ line
  $ )
  $ if match:
  $ selected_category_ids.add(
  $ match.group(1)
  $ )
  $ if not selected_category_ids:
  $ raise SystemExit(
  $ "No selected category IDs found in "
  $ "matcheverychannel.txt"
  $ )
  $ print("")
  $ print(
  $ "Selected category IDs:"
  $ )
  $ for category_id in sorted(
  $ selected_category_ids,
  $ key=lambda x: int(x)
  $ ):
  $ print(category_id)
  $ print("")
  $ print(
  $ f"Selected groups: "
  $ f"{len(selected_category_ids)}"
  $ )
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ # Create session
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ session = requests.Session()
  $ session.headers.update(
  $ {
  $ "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"
  $ }
  $ )
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ # Download categories
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ categories_url = (
  $ f"{XTREAM_URL}/player_api.php"
  $ f"?username={USERNAME}"
  $ f"&password={PASSWORD}"
  $ f"&action=get_live_categories"
  $ )
  $ categories = None
  $ for attempt in range(1, 6):
  $ try:
  $ print(
  $ f"Downloading provider groups "
  $ f"(attempt {attempt}/5)..."
  $ )
  $ response = session.get(
  $ categories_url,
  $ timeout=(30, 300)
  $ )
  $ response.raise_for_status()
  $ categories = response.json()
  $ break
  $ except Exception as e:
  $ print(e)
  $ time.sleep(10)
  $ if categories is None:
  $ raise SystemExit(
  $ "Provider groups download failed"
  $ )
  $ category_lookup = {}
  $ for category in categories:
  $ category_id = str(
  $ category.get(
  $ "category_id",
  $ ""
  $ )
  $ ).strip()
  $ if category_id in selected_category_ids:
  $ category_lookup[category_id] = category
  $ print("")
  $ print(
  $ f"Selected groups found: "
  $ f"{len(category_lookup)}"
  $ )
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ # Download all provider channels
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ streams_url = (
  $ f"{XTREAM_URL}/player_api.php"
  $ f"?username={USERNAME}"
  $ f"&password={PASSWORD}"
  $ f"&action=get_live_streams"
  $ )
  $ streams = None
  $ for attempt in range(1, 6):
  $ try:
  $ print(
  $ f"Downloading provider channels "
  $ f"(attempt {attempt}/5)..."
  $ )
  $ response = session.get(
  $ streams_url,
  $ timeout=(30, 300)
  $ )
  $ response.raise_for_status()
  $ streams = response.json()
  $ break
  $ except Exception as e:
  $ print(e)
  $ time.sleep(10)
  $ if streams is None:
  $ raise SystemExit(
  $ "Provider channels download failed"
  $ )
  $ if not isinstance(streams, list):
  $ print(
  $ "Unexpected provider response:"
  $ )
  $ print(streams)
  $ raise SystemExit(1)
  $ print("")
  $ print(
  $ f"Provider channels downloaded: "
  $ f"{len(streams)}"
  $ )
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ # Match channels to selected groups
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ matched = {}
  $ for stream in streams:
  $ category_id = str(
  $ stream.get(
  $ "category_id",
  $ ""
  $ )
  $ ).strip()
  $ if category_id not in selected_category_ids:
  $ continue
  $ if category_id not in matched:
  $ matched[category_id] = []
  $ matched[category_id].append(
  $ stream
  $ )
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ # Write same file in pipe format
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ total_channels = 0
  $ with open(
  $ FILE,
  $ "w",
  $ encoding="utf-8"
  $ ) as file:
  $ for category_id in sorted(
  $ selected_category_ids,
  $ key=lambda x: (
  $ category_lookup.get(
  $ x,
  $ {}
  $ ).get(
  $ "category_name",
  $ ""
  $ ).upper(),
  $ x
  $ )
  $ ):
  $ category = category_lookup.get(
  $ category_id,
  $ {}
  $ )
  $ category_name = category.get(
  $ "category_name",
  $ "UNKNOWN GROUP"
  $ )
  $ channels = matched.get(
  $ category_id,
  $ []
  $ )
  $ channels.sort(
  $ key=lambda stream: (
  $ str(
  $ stream.get(
  $ "name",
  $ ""
  $ )
  $ ).upper(),
  $ str(
  $ stream.get(
  $ "stream_id",
  $ ""
  $ )
  $ )
  $ )
  $ )
  $ # Group header
  $ file.write(
  $ f"{category_id} | "
  $ f"{category_id} |  | "
  $ f"###### "
  $ f"{category_name}"
  $ f" ######\n"
  $ )
  $ # Channels
  $ for stream in channels:
  $ stream_id = str(
  $ stream.get(
  $ "stream_id",
  $ ""
  $ )
  $ ).strip()
  $ channel_name = str(
  $ stream.get(
  $ "name",
  $ ""
  $ )
  $ ).strip()
  $ if not stream_id:
  $ continue
  $ file.write(
  $ f"{stream_id} | "
  $ f"{category_id} |  | "
  $ f"{channel_name}\n"
  $ )
  $ total_channels += 1
  $ file.write("\n")
  $ print("")
  $ print(
  $ f"Matched channels: "
  $ f"{total_channels}"
  $ )
  $ print("")
  $ print(
  $ "Updated: "
  $ f"{FILE}"
  $ )
  $ PY
  $ git config --global user.name "github-actions"
  $ git config --global user.email "github-actions@github.com"
  $ git add matcheverychannel.txt
  $ git commit -m "Update matched provider channels" || echo "No changes"
  $ git push

IPTV Org
--------
Workflow: .github/workflows/iptvorg-discover.yml

REMOTE SOURCES:
  - https://iptv-epg.org/files/epg-us.xml.gz

FILES REFERENCED BY WORKFLOW:
  - /tmp/process_iptvorg.js
      Type: TEMPORARY
      Status: TEMPORARY / CREATED DURING RUN

COMMANDS EXECUTED:
  $ mkdir -p iptvorg
  $ mkdir -p guides
  $ curl -L --fail --retry 5 --retry-delay 10 \
  $ https://iptv-epg.org/files/epg-us.xml.gz \
  $ -o iptvorg/source.xml.gz
  $ test -s iptvorg/source.xml.gz
  $ echo "IPTV-org source downloaded successfully"
  $ ls -lh iptvorg/source.xml.gz
  $ cat > /tmp/process_iptvorg.js <<'EOF'
  $ const fs = require('fs')
  $ const readline = require('readline')
  $ const zlib = require('zlib')
  $ const inputPath = 'iptvorg/source.xml.gz'
  $ const outputPath = 'iptvorg/processed.xml'
  $ const now = new Date()
  $ const cutoff = new Date(
  $ now.getTime() +
  $ 3 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
  $ )
  $ let channelsScanned = 0
  $ let programmesScanned = 0
  $ let programmesKept = 0
  $ let programmesSkipped = 0
  $ function getStart(block) {
  $ const match = block.match(
  $ /<programme\b[^>]*\bstart="([^"]+)"/i
  $ )
  $ return match
  $ ? match[1]
  $ : ''
  $ }
  $ function parseXmltvDate(value) {
  $ const match = String(value || '').match(
  $ /^(\d{4})(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})/
  $ )
  $ if (!match) {
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ return new Date(
  $ Date.UTC(
  $ Number(match[1]),
  $ Number(match[2]) - 1,
  $ Number(match[3]),
  $ Number(match[4]),
  $ Number(match[5]),
  $ Number(match[6])
  $ )
  $ )
  $ }
  $ async function main() {
  $ const input = fs.createReadStream(
  $ inputPath
  $ )
  $ const gunzip = zlib.createGunzip()
  $ input.pipe(gunzip)
  $ const rl = readline.createInterface({
  $ input: gunzip,
  $ crlfDelay: Infinity
  $ })
  $ const output = fs.createWriteStream(
  $ outputPath,
  $ {
  $ encoding: 'utf8'
  $ }
  $ )
  $ let insideChannel = false
  $ let channelBuffer = ''
  $ let insideProgramme = false
  $ let programmeBuffer = ''
  $ function write(value) {
  $ return new Promise(
  $ (
  $ resolve,
  $ reject
  $ ) => {
  $ if (
  $ output.write(
  $ value
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ resolve()
  $ } else {
  $ output.once(
  $ 'drain',
  $ resolve
  $ )
  $ output.once(
  $ 'error',
  $ reject
  $ )
  $ }
  $ }
  $ )
  $ }
  $ for await (
  $ const line of rl
  $ ) {
  $ if (
  $ !insideChannel &&
  $ !insideProgramme &&
  $ line.includes(
  $ '<channel '
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideChannel = true
  $ channelBuffer =
  $ line +
  $ '\n'
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '</channel>'
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideChannel = false
  $ channelsScanned++
  $ await write(
  $ channelBuffer
  $ )
  $ channelBuffer = ''
  $ }
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ insideChannel
  $ ) {
  $ channelBuffer +=
  $ line +
  $ '\n'
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '</channel>'
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideChannel = false
  $ channelsScanned++
  $ await write(
  $ channelBuffer
  $ )
  $ channelBuffer = ''
  $ }
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '<programme '
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideProgramme = true
  $ programmeBuffer =
  $ line +
  $ '\n'
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '</programme>'
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideProgramme = false
  $ programmesScanned++
  $ const start =
  $ getStart(
  $ programmeBuffer
  $ )
  $ const startDate =
  $ parseXmltvDate(
  $ start
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ startDate &&
  $ startDate >= now &&
  $ startDate < cutoff
  $ ) {
  $ programmesKept++
  $ await write(
  $ programmeBuffer
  $ )
  $ } else {
  $ programmesSkipped++
  $ }
  $ programmeBuffer = ''
  $ }
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (
  $ insideProgramme
  $ ) {
  $ programmeBuffer +=
  $ line +
  $ '\n'
  $ if (
  $ line.includes(
  $ '</programme>'
  $ )
  $ ) {
  $ insideProgramme = false
  $ programmesScanned++
  $ const start =
  $ getStart(
  $ programmeBuffer
  $ )
  $ const startDate =
  $ parseXmltvDate(
  $ start
  $ )
  $ if (
  $ startDate &&
  $ startDate >= now &&
  $ startDate < cutoff
  $ ) {
  $ programmesKept++
  $ await write(
  $ programmeBuffer
  $ )
  $ } else {
  $ programmesSkipped++
  $ }
  $ programmeBuffer = ''
  $ }
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ await write(
  $ line +
  $ '\n'
  $ )
  $ }
  $ await new Promise(
  $ (
  $ resolve,
  $ reject
  $ ) => {
  $ output.once(
  $ 'error',
  $ reject
  $ )
  $ output.end(
  $ resolve
  $ )
  $ }
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'IPTV-org processing complete'
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Channels scanned: ' +
  $ channelsScanned
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Programmes scanned: ' +
  $ programmesScanned
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Programmes kept: ' +
  $ programmesKept
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Programmes skipped: ' +
  $ programmesSkipped
  $ )
  $ console.log(
  $ 'Output: ' +
  $ outputPath
  $ )
  $ }
  $ main().catch(
  $ error => {
  $ console.error(
  $ error
  $ )
  $ process.exit(
  $ 1
  $ )
  $ }
  $ )
  $ EOF
  $ node /tmp/process_iptvorg.js
  $ CHANNELS=$(grep -c \
  $ '<channel ' \
  $ iptvorg/processed.xml || true)
  $ PROGRAMMES=$(grep -c \
  $ '<programme ' \
  $ iptvorg/processed.xml || true)
  $ echo "Processed IPTV-org channels: $CHANNELS"
  $ echo "Processed IPTV-org programmes: $PROGRAMMES"
  $ if [ "$CHANNELS" -lt 1 ]; then
  $ echo "Processed IPTV-org guide contains no channels"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ if [ "$PROGRAMMES" -lt 1 ]; then
  $ echo "Processed IPTV-org guide contains no programmes"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ test -s iptvorg/processed.xml
  $ gzip -c \
  $ iptvorg/processed.xml \
  $ > guides/iptvorg.xml.gz
  $ test -s guides/iptvorg.xml.gz
  $ echo "Compressed IPTV-org guide:"
  $ ls -lh guides/iptvorg.xml.gz
  $ git config user.name "github-actions"
  $ git config user.email "github-actions@github.com"
  $ git add guides/iptvorg.xml.gz
  $ if git diff --cached --quiet; then
  $ echo "No IPTV-org guide changes to commit"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ git commit \
  $ -m "Update IPTV-org guide"
  $ for attempt in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  $ echo "Push attempt $attempt"
  $ if git push origin HEAD:main; then
  $ echo "Push succeeded"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ echo "Push failed. Synchronizing with remote before retry..."
  $ git fetch origin main
  $ git rebase origin/main
  $ echo "Waiting before retry..."
  $ sleep 10
  $ done
  $ echo "Push failed after all retries"
  $ exit 1

IPTV Org Guide Inspection
-------------------------
Workflow: .github/workflows/iptvorg-inspect.yml

REMOTE SOURCES:
  - https://iptv-epg.org/files/epg-us.xml.gz

COMMANDS EXECUTED:
  $ mkdir -p /tmp/iptvorg
  $ curl -L --fail --retry 5 --retry-delay 10 \
  $ "https://iptv-epg.org/files/epg-us.xml.gz" \
  $ -o "/tmp/iptvorg/source.xml.gz"
  $ test -s "/tmp/iptvorg/source.xml.gz"
  $ echo "IPTV-org EPG source downloaded successfully"
  $ ls -lh "/tmp/iptvorg/source.xml.gz"
  $ mkdir -p guides
  $ gzip -dc \
  $ "/tmp/iptvorg/source.xml.gz" \
  $ > "guides/iptvorg.xml"
  $ test -s "guides/iptvorg.xml"
  $ echo "IPTV-org EPG decompressed successfully"
  $ ls -lh "guides/iptvorg.xml"
  $ CHANNELS=$(grep -c '<channel ' "guides/iptvorg.xml" || true)
  $ PROGRAMMES=$(grep -c '<programme ' "guides/iptvorg.xml" || true)
  $ echo "IPTV-org channels: $CHANNELS"
  $ echo "IPTV-org programmes: $PROGRAMMES"
  $ if [ "$CHANNELS" -lt 1 ]; then
  $ echo "IPTV-org guide contains no channels"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ if [ "$PROGRAMMES" -lt 1 ]; then
  $ echo "IPTV-org guide contains no programmes"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ git config user.name "github-actions"
  $ git config user.email "github-actions@github.com"
  $ git add "guides/iptvorg.xml"
  $ if git diff --cached --quiet; then
  $ echo "No IPTV-org guide changes to commit"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ git commit -m "Add IPTV-org guide for inspection"
  $ for attempt in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  $ echo "Push attempt $attempt"
  $ if git push origin HEAD:main; then
  $ echo "Push succeeded"
  $ exit 0
  $ fi
  $ echo "Push failed. Synchronizing with remote before retry..."
  $ git fetch origin main
  $ git rebase origin/main
  $ echo "Waiting before retry..."
  $ sleep 10
  $ done
  $ echo "Push failed after all retries"
  $ exit 1

Match Every Channel
-------------------
Workflow: .github/workflows/match-every-channel.yml

COMMANDS EXECUTED:
  $ echo "Provider credentials loaded"
  $ if [ -z "$XTREAM_URL" ]; then
  $ echo "XTREAM_URL missing"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ if [ -z "$XTREAM_USERNAME" ]; then
  $ echo "XTREAM_USERNAME missing"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ if [ -z "$XTREAM_PASSWORD" ]; then
  $ echo "XTREAM_PASSWORD missing"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ echo "XTREAM_URL exists"
  $ echo "XTREAM_USERNAME exists"
  $ echo "XTREAM_PASSWORD exists"
  $ python -m pip install requests
  $ python - <<'PY'
  $ import os
  $ import re
  $ import time
  $ import requests
  $ XTREAM_URL = os.environ["XTREAM_URL"].rstrip("/")
  $ USERNAME = os.environ["XTREAM_USERNAME"]
  $ PASSWORD = os.environ["XTREAM_PASSWORD"]
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ # Normalize Xtream URL
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ if XTREAM_URL.startswith("https://"):
  $ XTREAM_URL = XTREAM_URL.replace(
  $ "https://",
  $ "http://"
  $ )
  $ if ":80" not in XTREAM_URL and ":443" not in XTREAM_URL:
  $ XTREAM_URL += ":80"
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ # Session
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ session = requests.Session()
  $ session.headers.update(
  $ {
  $ "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"
  $ }
  $ )
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ # Download provider categories
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ categories_url = (
  $ f"{XTREAM_URL}/player_api.php"
  $ f"?username={USERNAME}"
  $ f"&password={PASSWORD}"
  $ f"&action=get_live_categories"
  $ )
  $ categories = None
  $ for attempt in range(1, 6):
  $ try:
  $ print(
  $ f"Downloading provider groups "
  $ f"(attempt {attempt}/5)..."
  $ )
  $ response = session.get(
  $ categories_url,
  $ timeout=(30, 300)
  $ )
  $ response.raise_for_status()
  $ categories = response.json()
  $ break
  $ except Exception as e:
  $ print(e)
  $ time.sleep(10)
  $ if categories is None:
  $ raise SystemExit(
  $ "Provider group download failed"
  $ )
  $ if not isinstance(categories, list):
  $ print(
  $ "Unexpected provider response:"
  $ )
  $ print(categories)
  $ raise SystemExit(1)
  $ print("")
  $ print(
  $ f"Provider groups: "
  $ f"{len(categories)}"
  $ )
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ # Match CA / UK / US groups
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ matched_categories = []
  $ for category in categories:
  $ category_id = str(
  $ category.get(
  $ "category_id",
  $ ""
  $ )
  $ ).strip()
  $ category_name = str(
  $ category.get(
  $ "category_name",
  $ ""
  $ )
  $ ).strip()
  $ if not category_id or not category_name:
  $ continue
  $ name_upper = category_name.upper()
  $ # Match country prefixes or names
  $ if (
  $ re.search(
  $ r"(^|[^A-Z])CA([^A-Z]|$)",
  $ name_upper
  $ )
  $ or re.search(
  $ r"(^|[^A-Z])UK([^A-Z]|$)",
  $ name_upper
  $ )
  $ or re.search(
  $ r"(^|[^A-Z])US([^A-Z]|$)",
  $ name_upper
  $ )
  $ or "CANADA" in name_upper
  $ or "UNITED KINGDOM" in name_upper
  $ or "UNITED STATES" in name_upper
  $ ):
  $ matched_categories.append(
  $ {
  $ "category_id":
  $ category_id,
  $ "category_name":
  $ category_name
  $ }
  $ )
  $ matched_categories.sort(
  $ key=lambda item:
  $ (
  $ item["category_name"].upper(),
  $ item["category_id"]
  $ )
  $ )
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ # Write output
  $ # ---------------------------------
  $ with open(
  $ "matcheverychannel.txt",
  $ "w",
  $ encoding="utf-8"
  $ ) as file:
  $ file.write(
  $ "MATCHED PROVIDER CHANNEL GROUPS\n"
  $ )
  $ file.write(
  $ "================================\n\n"
  $ )
  $ file.write(
  $ "CA / UK / US GROUPS\n"
  $ )
  $ file.write(
  $ "==================\n\n"
  $ )
  $ for category in matched_categories:
  $ file.write(
  $ f'[{category["category_id"]}] '

  $ f'[{category["category_id"]}] '
  $ f'{category["category_name"]}'
  $ )

  $ mkdir -p epg/sites/tvpassport.com

  $ OUTPUT="epg/sites/tvpassport.com/festy-tvpassport.channels.xml"

  $ const axios = require('axios')
  $ const dayjs = require('dayjs')
  $ const cheerio = require('cheerio')
  $ const utc = require('dayjs/plugin/utc')
  $ const timezone = require('dayjs/plugin/timezone')
  $ const customParseFormat = require('dayjs/plugin/customParseFormat')
  $ dayjs.extend(utc)
  $ dayjs.extend(timezone)
  $ dayjs.extend(customParseFormat)
  $ module.exports = {
  $ site: 'tvpassport.com',
  $ days: 14,
  $ url({ channel, date }) {
  $ return `https://www.tvpassport.com/tv-listings/stations/${channel.site_id}/${date.format('YYYY-MM-DD')}`
  $ },
  $ async request() {
  $ return {
  $ timeout: Number(process.env.TVPASSPORT_SCHEDULE_TIMEOUT || 30000),
  $ headers: {
  $ Cookie: await getCookie(),
  $ 'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 Chrome/131.0.0.0 Safari/537.36'

  $ parser({ content, channel, date }) {
  $ const programs = []
  $ const currentTimezone = parseCurrentTimezone(content)
  $ const items = parseItems(content)
  $ console.log(`[PULL] ${channel.name} | ${date.format('YYYY-MM-DD')} | programs=${items.length}`)
  $ for (const item of items) {
  $ const $item = cheerio.load(item)
  $ const start = parseStart($item, currentTimezone)
  $ const duration = parseDuration($item)
  $ const stop = start.add(duration, 'm')
  $ let title = cleanTitle(parseTitle($item))
  $ let episodeTitle = cleanSpaces(parseSubTitle($item))
  $ const description = cleanPassportDescription(cleanSpaces(parseDescription($item)))
  $ const image = parseImage($item)
  $ const rating = parseRating($item)
  $ const year = parseYear($item)
  $ const categories = parseCategory($item)
  $ const actors = parseActors($item)
  $ const guest = parseGuest($item)
  $ const director = parseDirector($item)
  $ if (title === 'Movie' || title === 'Cinéma') {
  $ title = episodeTitle
  $ episodeTitle = ''
  $ }
  $ const programType = classifyProgramType({
  $ title,
  $ episodeTitle,
  $ description,
  $ year,
  $ categories,
  $ duration
  $ })
  $ const finalDescription = buildPackedDescription({
  $ programType,
  $ episodeTitle,
  $ episodeCode: '',
  $ description,
  $ airDate: '',
  $ releaseYear: year,
  $ suppressDisplayDate: false
  $ })
  $ const program = {
  $ title,
  $ description: finalDescription,
  $ start,
  $ stop,
  $ category: buildCategories(programType, categories)
  $ }
  $ if (image) program.image = image
  $ if (rating) program.rating = rating
  $ if (actors.length) program.actors = actors
  $ if (guest.length) program.guest = guest
  $ if (director.length) program.director = director
  $ // Keep TiviMate display clean:
  $ // - <title> only for row title
  $ // - episode/year/date text packed into <desc>
  $ // - no separate sub-title, episode-num, or date fields
  $ programs.push(program)
  $ }
  $ return programs
  $ }
  $ }
  $ async function getCookie() {
  $ const res = await axios.get('https://www.tvpassport.com/tv-listings')
  $ const setCookie = res.headers['set-cookie']
  $ if (!setCookie || setCookie.length === 0) return ''
  $ const cookies = setCookie.map(cookie => cookie.split(';')[0])
  $ return cookies.join('; ')
  $ }
  $ function parseDescription($item) {
  $ return $item('*').data('description') || ''
  $ }
  $ function parseImage($item) {
  $ const showpicture = $item('*').data('showpicture')
  $ if (!showpicture) return null
  $ try {
  $ return new URL(showpicture, 'https://cdn.tvpassport.com/image/show/960x540/').href
  $ } catch {
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ }
  $ function parseTitle($item) {
  $ return $item('*').data('showname')?.toString() || ''
  $ }
  $ function parseSubTitle($item) {
  $ return $item('*').data('episodetitle')?.toString() || ''
  $ }
  $ function parseYear($item) {
  $ return $item('*').data('year')?.toString() || ''
  $ }
  $ function parseCategory($item) {
  $ const showtype = $item('*').data('showtype')
  $ return showtype ? String(showtype).split(', ').filter(Boolean) : []
  $ }
  $ function parseActors($item) {
  $ const cast = $item('*').data('cast')
  $ return cast ? String(cast).split(', ').filter(Boolean) : []
  $ }
  $ function parseDirector($item) {
  $ const director = $item('*').data('director')
  $ return director ? String(director).split(', ').filter(Boolean) : []
  $ }
  $ function parseGuest($item) {
  $ const guest = $item('*').data('guest')
  $ return guest ? String(guest).split(', ').filter(Boolean) : []
  $ }
  $ function parseRating($item) {
  $ const rating = $item('*').data('rating')
  $ return rating
  $ ? {
  $ system: 'MPA',
  $ value: String(rating).replace(/^TV/, 'TV-')
  $ }
  $ : null
  $ }
  $ function parseStart($item, currentTimezone) {
  $ const time = $item('*').data('st')
  $ return dayjs.tz(time, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss', currentTimezone)
  $ }
  $ function parseDuration($item) {
  $ const duration = $item('*').data('duration')
  $ return parseInt(duration || 0, 10)
  $ }
  $ function parseItems(content) {
  $ if (!content) return []
  $ const $ = cheerio.load(content)
  $ return $('.station-listings .list-group-item').toArray()
  $ }
  $ function parseCurrentTimezone(content) {
  $ if (!content) return 'America/New_York'
  $ const $ = cheerio.load(content)
  $ return $('#timezone_selector').val() || 'America/New_York'
  $ }
  $ function classifyProgramType({ title, episodeTitle, description, year, categories, duration }) {
  $ const haystack = [title, description, categories].flat().filter(Boolean).join(' ').toLowerCase()
  $ const hasEpisodeSignals = Boolean(episodeTitle)
  $ if (/mini[\s-]?series|limited series|limited-series/.test(haystack)) return 'miniseries'
  $ if (/series|episode|tv show|television|scripted|reality|documentary series/.test(haystack)) return 'series'
  $ if (hasEpisodeSignals) return 'series'
  $ if (/movie|film|feature film|cinema/.test(haystack)) return 'movie'
  $ if (duration >= 75 && year && !hasEpisodeSignals) return 'movie'
  $ return 'program'
  $ }
  $ function buildCategories(programType, originalCategories) {
  $ const categories = Array.isArray(originalCategories) ? originalCategories.filter(Boolean) : []
  $ if (programType === 'movie') return unique(['Movie', ...categories])
  $ if (programType === 'miniseries') return unique(['Miniseries', 'Series', ...categories])
  $ if (programType === 'series') return unique(['Series', ...categories])
  $ return unique(categories.length ? categories : ['Program'])
  $ }
  $ function buildPackedDescription({ programType, episodeTitle, episodeCode, description, airDate, releaseYear, suppressDisplayDate }) {
  $ const parts = []
  $ let prefix = ''
  $ if (programType === 'series' || programType === 'miniseries') {
  $ if (episodeTitle && episodeCode) prefix = `${episodeTitle} - ${episodeCode}`
  $ else if (episodeTitle) prefix = episodeTitle
  $ else if (episodeCode) prefix = episodeCode
  $ }
  $ if (prefix) parts.push(prefix)
  $ if (description) parts.push(description)
  $ let finalDescription = cleanPackedDescription(parts.join('. '))
  $ if (programType === 'movie') {
  $ if (releaseYear && !descriptionIncludesDisplayDate(finalDescription, releaseYear)) {
  $ finalDescription = finalDescription ? `${finalDescription} (${releaseYear})` : `(${releaseYear})`
  $ }
  $ return finalDescription
  $ }
  $ if (programType === 'series' || programType === 'miniseries') {
  $ if (airDate && !suppressDisplayDate && !descriptionIncludesDisplayDate(finalDescription, airDate)) {
  $ finalDescription = finalDescription ? `${finalDescription} (${airDate})` : `(${airDate})`
  $ }
  $ return finalDescription
  $ }
  $ const dateOrYear = suppressDisplayDate ? releaseYear : airDate || releaseYear
  $ if (dateOrYear && !descriptionIncludesDisplayDate(finalDescription, dateOrYear)) {
  $ finalDescription = finalDescription ? `${finalDescription} (${dateOrYear})` : `(${dateOrYear})`
  $ }
  $ return finalDescription
  $ }
  $ function cleanPassportDescription(value) {
  $ return cleanSpaces(value)
  $ // Remove actor-name parentheticals from Passport descriptions.
  $ // Example:
  $ // "James Bond (Daniel Craig) must..." -> "James Bond must..."
  $ // Keeps years/dates like (2012), ratings, and normal description text.
  $ .replace(/\s*\(([A-Z][a-z]+(?:\s+[A-Z][a-z]+){1,3})(?:,\s*[A-Z][a-z]+(?:\s+[A-Z][a-z]+){1,3})*\)/g, '')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function cleanPackedDescription(value) {
  $ return cleanSpaces(value)
  $ .replace(/\s+Aired\s+(\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4})\.?\s*$/i, ' ($1)')
  $ .replace(/\s+\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*$/g, ' ($2)')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function descriptionIncludesDisplayDate(description, dateOrYear) {
  $ if (!description || !dateOrYear) return false
  $ const escaped = String(dateOrYear).replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&')
  $ return new RegExp(`\\(${escaped}\\)\\s*$`).test(description) || description.includes(String(dateOrYear))
  $ }
  $ function cleanTitle(value) {
  $ return cleanSpaces(value)
  $ .replace(/^\s*(New|Live|Repeat)\s*[:\-]\s*/i, '')
  $ .replace(/\s*\((New|Live|Repeat)\)\s*$/i, '')
  $ .replace(/\s*[-–—]\s*(New|Live|Repeat)\s*$/i, '')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function cleanSpaces(value) {
  $ return String(value || '')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .replace(/\s+([.,!?;:])/g, '$1')
  $ .replace(/([.,!?;:])([A-Za-z0-9])/g, '$1 $2')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function unique(values) {
  $ return [...new Set(values.filter(Boolean))]
  $ }
  $ EOFJS

  $ const fs = require('fs')

  $ } catch {
  $ cache = {}

  $ const stats = {
  $ programmes: 0,

  $ searchedShows: 0,

  $ enriched: 0,
  $ addedCategories: 0,
  $ addedDescriptions: 0
  $ }
  $ function sleep(ms) {
  $ return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms))
  $ }
  $ function escapeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
  $ .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
  $ .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
  $ .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
  $ }
  $ function decodeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
  $ .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
  $ .replace(/&apos;/g, "'")
  $ .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
  $ .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
  $ }
  $ function stripTags(s) {
  $ return decodeXml(String(s || '').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim())
  $ }
  $ function getTag(block, tag) {
  $ const m = block.match(new RegExp(`<${tag}\\b[^>]*>([\\s\\S]*?)<\\/${tag}>`, 'i'))
  $ return m ? stripTags(m[1]) : ''
  $ }
  $ function getTags(block, tag) {
  $ return [...block.matchAll(new RegExp(`<${tag}\\b[^>]*>([\\s\\S]*?)<\\/${tag}>`, 'gi'))]
  $ .map(m => stripTags(m[1]))
  $ .filter(Boolean)
  $ }
  $ function hasTag(block, tag) {
  $ return new RegExp(`<${tag}\\b`, 'i').test(block)
  $ }
  $ function hasCategory(block, category) {
  $ return getTags(block, 'category').some(c => c.toLowerCase() === category.toLowerCase())
  $ }
  $ function hasAnyCategory(block) {
  $ return hasTag(block, 'category')
  $ }
  $ function getStartDate(block) {
  $ const m = block.match(/start="(\d{8})/i)
  $ if (!m) return ''
  $ return `${m[1].slice(0, 4)}-${m[1].slice(4, 6)}-${m[1].slice(6, 8)}`
  $ }
  $ function cleanTitleForSearch(title) {
  $ return String(title || '')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .replace(/^\s*(New|Live|Repeat)\s*[:\-]\s*/i, '')
  $ .replace(/\s*\((New|Live|Repeat)\)\s*$/i, '')
  $ .replace(/\s*[-–—]\s*(New|Live|Repeat)\s*$/i, '')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function isMovie(block) {
  $ return hasCategory(block, 'Movie') || hasCategory(block, 'Film')
  $ }
  $ function needsTvmaze(block) {
  $ if (!hasAnyCategory(block)) return true
  $ const desc = getTag(block, 'desc')
  $ if (!desc) return true
  $ const hasPackedDisplayDate = /\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*$/.test(desc)
  $ if (!hasPackedDisplayDate && hasCategory(block, 'Series')) return true
  $ return false
  $ }
  $ function tvmazeShowIsMovieLike(show) {
  $ const type = String(show?.type || '').toLowerCase()
  $ return type === 'movie' || type === 'film'
  $ }
  $ function tvmazeShowIsMiniseries(show) {
  $ const type = String(show?.type || '').toLowerCase()
  $ const name = String(show?.name || '').toLowerCase()
  $ const summary = String(show?.summary || '').toLowerCase()
  $ return (
  $ type.includes('mini') ||
  $ type.includes('limited') ||
  $ name.includes('miniseries') ||
  $ summary.includes('miniseries') ||
  $ summary.includes('limited series')
  $ )
  $ }
  $ function normalize(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .toLowerCase()
  $ .replace(/&/g, 'and')
  $ .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, ' ')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function scoreShow(title, show) {
  $ const a = normalize(title)
  $ const b = normalize(show?.name || '')
  $ if (!a || !b) return 0
  $ if (a === b) return 100
  $ if (a.includes(b) || b.includes(a)) return 80
  $ return 0
  $ }
  $ async function tvmazeSearchShow(title) {
  $ const key = `show:${normalize(title)}`
  $ if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(cache, key)) return cache[key]
  $ const url = `https://api.tvmaze.com/search/shows?q=${encodeURIComponent(title)}`
  $ const result = await fetchJsonWithRetries(url, `search ${title}`)
  $ const candidates = Array.isArray(result) ? result.map(x => x.show).filter(Boolean) : []
  $ let best = null
  $ let bestScore = 0
  $ for (const show of candidates) {
  $ if (tvmazeShowIsMovieLike(show)) continue
  $ const score = scoreShow(title, show)
  $ if (score > bestScore) {
  $ best = show
  $ bestScore = score
  $ }
  $ }
  $ if (bestScore < 80) best = null
  $ cache[key] = best
  $ await sleep(250)
  $ return best
  $ }
  $ async function tvmazeEpisodes(showId) {
  $ const key = `episodes:${showId}`
  $ if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(cache, key)) return cache[key]
  $ const url = `https://api.tvmaze.com/shows/${showId}/episodes`
  $ const episodes = await fetchJsonWithRetries(url, `episodes ${showId}`)
  $ cache[key] = Array.isArray(episodes) ? episodes : []
  $ await sleep(250)
  $ return cache[key]
  $ }
  $ async function fetchJsonWithRetries(url, label) {
  $ const attempts = 4
  $ for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= attempts; attempt++) {
  $ try {
  $ const response = await fetch(url, {
  $ headers: {
  $ 'User-Agent': 'festy-epg-tvpassport-tvmaze-fill-missing-only/1.0'
  $ }
  $ })
  $ if (response.status === 404) return null
  $ if (response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) {
  $ throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`)
  $ }
  $ if (!response.ok) return null
  $ return await response.json()
  $ } catch (err) {
  $ if (attempt === attempts) {
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE FAIL] ${label} | ${err.message}`)
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ const delay = 1000 * attempt + Math.floor(Math.random() * 500)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE RETRY] ${label} | ${err.message} | attempt=${attempt}/${attempts}`)
  $ await sleep(delay)
  $ }
  $ }
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ function findEpisodeForProgramme(episodes, block) {
  $ const scheduleDate = getStartDate(block)
  $ const desc = getTag(block, 'desc')
  $ const codeMatch = desc.match(/\bS(\d+)E(\d+)\b/i)
  $ if (codeMatch) {
  $ const season = Number(codeMatch[1])
  $ const number = Number(codeMatch[2])
  $ const byCode = episodes.find(ep => Number(ep.season) === season && Number(ep.number) === number)
  $ if (byCode) return byCode
  $ }
  $ const packedTitle = getPackedEpisodeTitleFromDesc(desc)
  $ if (packedTitle) {
  $ const packedNorm = normalize(packedTitle)
  $ const byPackedTitle = episodes.find(ep => normalize(ep.name) === packedNorm)
  $ if (byPackedTitle) return byPackedTitle
  $ }
  $ if (scheduleDate) {
  $ const exact = episodes.find(ep => ep.airdate === scheduleDate)
  $ if (exact) return exact
  $ }
  $ return null
  $ }
  $ function getPackedEpisodeTitleFromDesc(desc) {
  $ const cleaned = cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(desc)
  $ .replace(/\s*\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*$/, '')
  $ .trim()
  $ if (!cleaned) return ''
  $ const firstSentence = cleaned.match(/^(.+?)(?:\.\s+|$)/)
  $ const first = firstSentence ? firstSentence[1].trim() : ''
  $ if (!first) return ''
  $ const prefixMatch = first.match(/^(.+?)\s+-\s+(S\d+E\d+|S\d+|E\d+)$/i)
  $ if (prefixMatch) return prefixMatch[1].trim()
  $ if (/^S\d+E\d+$/i.test(first)) return ''
  $ if (first.length > 80) return ''
  $ return first
  $ }
  $ function cleanTvmazeSummary(value) {
  $ return decodeXml(String(value || '')
  $ .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .trim())
  $ }
  $ function formatDateDisplay(date) {
  $ if (!date || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date)) return ''
  $ return `${date.slice(5, 7)}/${date.slice(8, 10)}/${date.slice(0, 4)}`
  $ }
  $ function buildTvmazeEpisodeCode(season, episode) {
  $ const s = Number(season)
  $ const e = Number(episode)
  $ if (!Number.isFinite(s) || !Number.isFinite(e) || s < 1 || e < 1) return ''
  $ return `S${s}E${e}`
  $ }
  $ function descHasEpisodeName(desc, episodeName) {
  $ if (!desc || !episodeName) return false
  $ const descNorm = normalize(desc)
  $ const nameNorm = normalize(episodeName)
  $ return Boolean(nameNorm && descNorm.includes(nameNorm))
  $ }
  $ function descHasEpisodeCode(desc, season, episode) {
  $ const code = buildTvmazeEpisodeCode(season, episode)
  $ if (!desc || !code) return false
  $ return new RegExp(`\\b${code}\\b`, 'i').test(desc)
  $ }
  $ function descHasDisplayDate(desc, airdate) {
  $ const displayDate = formatDateDisplay(airdate)
  $ if (!desc || !displayDate) return false
  $ return desc.includes(`(${displayDate})`) || desc.includes(displayDate)
  $ }
  $ function shouldSuppressTvmazeDisplayDate(block, airdate) {
  $ if (!airdate) return false
  $ const title = getTag(block, 'title')
  $ const desc = getTag(block, 'desc')
  $ const categories = getTags(block, 'category')
  $ const haystack = [title, desc, categories]
  $ .flat()
  $ .filter(Boolean)
  $ .join(' ')
  $ .toLowerCase()
  $ const liveOrCurrent = /\b(live|news|newscast|weather|sports|sporting|game|match|tournament|football|basketball|baseball|hockey|soccer|golf|tennis|racing|wwe|ufc|mma|boxing|pregame|postgame|post-game|pre-game|paid programming|infomercial)\b/.test(haystack)
  $ if (!liveOrCurrent) return false
  $ const scheduleDateText = getStartDate(block)
  $ if (!scheduleDateText || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(scheduleDateText) || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(airdate)) return true
  $ const scheduleDate = new Date(`${scheduleDateText}T00:00:00Z`)
  $ const parsedAirDate = new Date(`${airdate}T00:00:00Z`)
  $ if (Number.isNaN(scheduleDate.getTime()) || Number.isNaN(parsedAirDate.getTime())) return true
  $ // Match the original guide pass: for live/current news, sports, paid programming,
  $ // and similar listings, suppress dates when TVMaze only found the current airing date.
  $ return Math.abs(scheduleDate.getTime() - parsedAirDate.getTime()) / 86400000 <= 45
  $ }
  $ function cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(value) {
  $ return String(value || '')
  $ .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
  $ .replace(/\s+([.,!?;:])/g, '$1')
  $ .replace(/([.,!?;:])([A-Za-z0-9])/g, '$1 $2')
  $ .replace(/\s+Aired\s+(\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4})\.?\s*$/i, ' ($1)')
  $ .replace(/\s+\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*$/g, ' ($2)')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function splitTrailingDisplayDate(desc) {
  $ const cleaned = cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(desc)
  $ const match = cleaned.match(/\s*\((\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{4}|\d{4})\)\s*$/)
  $ if (!match) return { body: cleaned, dateText: '' }
  $ return {
  $ body: cleaned.slice(0, match.index).trim(),
  $ dateText: match[1]
  $ }
  $ }
  $ function parsePackedDescription(desc) {
  $ const split = splitTrailingDisplayDate(desc)
  $ const withoutDate = split.body
  $ const firstSentence = withoutDate.match(/^(.+?)(?:\.\s+|$)/)
  $ const first = firstSentence ? firstSentence[1].trim() : ''
  $ const rest = firstSentence && firstSentence[0].length < withoutDate.length
  $ ? withoutDate.slice(firstSentence[0].length).trim()
  $ : ''
  $ const prefixMatch = first.match(/^(.+?)\s+-\s+(S\d+E\d+|S\d+|E\d+)$/i)
  $ if (prefixMatch) {
  $ return {
  $ episodeTitle: prefixMatch[1].trim(),
  $ episodeCode: prefixMatch[2].toUpperCase(),
  $ body: rest,
  $ dateText: split.dateText,
  $ hadPrefix: true
  $ }
  $ }
  $ const codeOnlyMatch = first.match(/^(S\d+E\d+|S\d+|E\d+)$/i)
  $ if (codeOnlyMatch) {
  $ return {
  $ episodeTitle: '',
  $ episodeCode: codeOnlyMatch[1].toUpperCase(),
  $ body: rest,
  $ dateText: split.dateText,
  $ hadPrefix: true
  $ }
  $ }
  $ return {
  $ episodeTitle: '',
  $ episodeCode: '',
  $ body: withoutDate,
  $ dateText: split.dateText,
  $ hadPrefix: false
  $ }
  $ }
  $ function buildTvmazePackedDescription(existingDesc, show, episode, block) {
  $ if (!episode) return cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(existingDesc)
  $ const existing = cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(existingDesc)
  $ const parsed = parsePackedDescription(existing)
  $ const episodeName = cleanTvmazeSummary(episode?.name || '')
  $ const episodeCode = buildTvmazeEpisodeCode(episode?.season, episode?.number)
  $ const episodeSummary = cleanTvmazeSummary(episode?.summary || '')
  $ const showSummary = cleanTvmazeSummary(show?.summary || '')
  $ const displayDate = formatDateDisplay(episode?.airdate)
  $ let titlePart = parsed.episodeTitle
  $ let codePart = parsed.episodeCode
  $ let bodyPart = parsed.body
  $ let datePart = parsed.dateText
  $ const packedTitle = getPackedEpisodeTitleFromDesc(existing)
  $ if (!titlePart && episodeName && packedTitle && normalize(packedTitle) === normalize(episodeName)) {
  $ titlePart = packedTitle
  $ const firstSentence = bodyPart.match(/^(.+?)(?:\.\s+|$)/)
  $ if (firstSentence) bodyPart = bodyPart.slice(firstSentence[0].length).trim()
  $ }
  $ if (!titlePart && episodeName && !descHasEpisodeName(existing, episodeName)) {
  $ titlePart = episodeName
  $ }
  $ if (!codePart && episodeCode && !descHasEpisodeCode(existing, episode?.season, episode?.number)) {
  $ codePart = episodeCode
  $ }
  $ if (!bodyPart || bodyPart === titlePart || bodyPart === codePart || bodyPart === `${titlePart} - ${codePart}`) {
  $ bodyPart = episodeSummary || showSummary || ''
  $ }
  $ if (!datePart && displayDate && !shouldSuppressTvmazeDisplayDate(block, episode?.airdate) && !descHasDisplayDate(existing, episode?.airdate)) {
  $ datePart = displayDate
  $ }
  $ const parts = []
  $ if (titlePart && codePart) parts.push(`${titlePart} - ${codePart}`)
  $ else if (titlePart) parts.push(titlePart)
  $ else if (codePart) parts.push(codePart)
  $ if (bodyPart) {
  $ const bodyNorm = normalize(bodyPart)
  $ const prefixNorm = normalize(parts[0] || '')
  $ if (!prefixNorm || bodyNorm !== prefixNorm) parts.push(bodyPart)
  $ }
  $ let desc = cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(parts.join('. '))
  $ if (datePart) desc = desc ? `${desc} (${datePart})` : `(${datePart})`
  $ return cleanPackedTvmazeDescription(desc)
  $ }
  $ function replaceTag(block, tag, value) {
  $ const escaped = escapeXml(value)
  $ const tagXml = `  <${tag} lang="en">${escaped}</${tag}>`
  $ if (hasTag(block, tag)) {
  $ return block.replace(new RegExp(`<${tag}\\b[^>]*>[\\s\\S]*?<\\/${tag}>`, 'i'), tagXml.trim())
  $ }
  $ return insertBeforeProgrammeEnd(block, tagXml)
  $ }
  $ function insertBeforeProgrammeEnd(block, tagXml) {
  $ return block.replace(/<\/programme>\s*$/i, `${tagXml}\n</programme>`)
  $ }
  $ function enrichBlockMissingOnly(block, show, episode) {
  $ let updated = block
  $ let changed = false
  $ if (!hasAnyCategory(updated)) {
  $ const category = tvmazeShowIsMiniseries(show) ? 'Miniseries' : 'Series'
  $ updated = insertBeforeProgrammeEnd(updated, `  <category lang="en">${category}</category>`)
  $ changed = true
  $ stats.addedCategories++
  $ }
  $ const existingDesc = getTag(updated, 'desc')
  $ const packedDesc = buildTvmazePackedDescription(existingDesc, show, episode, updated)
  $ if (packedDesc && packedDesc !== existingDesc) {
  $ updated = replaceTag(updated, 'desc', packedDesc)
  $ changed = true
  $ stats.addedDescriptions++
  $ }
  $ if (changed) stats.enriched++
  $ return updated
  $ }
  $ async function main() {
  $ const parts = xml.split(/(<programme\b[\s\S]*?<\/programme>)/gi)
  $ const out = []
  $ for (const part of parts) {
  $ if (!/^<programme\b/i.test(part)) {
  $ out.push(part)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ stats.programmes++
  $ let block = part
  $ const title = cleanTitleForSearch(getTag(block, 'title'))
  $ if (!title) {
  $ out.push(block)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (isMovie(block)) {
  $ stats.skippedMovies++
  $ out.push(block)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (!needsTvmaze(block)) {
  $ out.push(block)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ stats.searchedShows++
  $ const show = await tvmazeSearchShow(title)
  $ if (!show) {
  $ out.push(block)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ if (tvmazeShowIsMovieLike(show)) {
  $ stats.skippedMovies++
  $ out.push(block)
  $ continue
  $ }
  $ stats.matchedShows++
  $ const episodes = await tvmazeEpisodes(show.id)
  $ const episode = findEpisodeForProgramme(episodes, block)
  $ block = enrichBlockMissingOnly(block, show, episode)
  $ out.push(block)
  $ }
  $ fs.writeFileSync(guidePath, out.join(''))
  $ fs.writeFileSync(cachePath, JSON.stringify(cache, null, 2))
  $ console.log('[TVMAZE] missing-only enrichment complete')
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] programmes scanned: ${stats.programmes}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] movies skipped: ${stats.skippedMovies}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] shows searched: ${stats.searchedShows}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] shows matched: ${stats.matchedShows}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] programmes enriched: ${stats.enriched}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] categories added: ${stats.addedCategories}`)
  $ console.log(`[TVMAZE] descriptions added: ${stats.addedDescriptions}`)
  $ }
  $ main().catch(err => {
  $ console.error(err)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ })
  $ EOFJS
  $ CH=$(grep -c '<channel ' guides/TVPassport.xml || true)
  $ PR=$(grep -c '<programme ' guides/TVPassport.xml || true)
  $ DESC=$(grep -c '<desc ' guides/TVPassport.xml || true)
  $ echo "TVPassport channels: $CH"
  $ echo "TVPassport programmes: $PR"
  $ echo "TVPassport descriptions: $DESC"
  $ if [ "$CH" -lt 100 ]; then
  $ echo "Invalid TVPassport output - expected at least 100 channels"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ if [ "$PR" -lt 1 ]; then
  $ echo "Invalid TVPassport output - no programmes"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ if [ "$DESC" -lt 1 ]; then
  $ echo "Invalid TVPassport output - no descriptions"
  $ exit 1
  $ fi
  $ node <<'EOFJS'
  $ const fs = require('fs')
  $ const curatedPath = 'guides/TVPassport.channels.xml'
  $ const guidePath = 'guides/TVPassport.xml'
  $ if (!fs.existsSync(curatedPath)) {
  $ console.error(`Missing curated channel list: ${curatedPath}`)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ if (!fs.existsSync(guidePath)) {
  $ console.error(`Missing generated guide: ${guidePath}`)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ function decodeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
  $ .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
  $ .replace(/&apos;/g, "'")
  $ .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
  $ .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
  $ }
  $ function escapeXml(s) {
  $ return String(s || '')
  $ .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
  $ .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
  $ .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
  $ .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
  $ }
  $ function attr(attrs, name) {
  $ const m = attrs.match(new RegExp(`${name}="([^"]*)"`, 'i'))
  $ return m ? decodeXml(m[1]).trim() : ''
  $ }
  $ function normalize(s) {
  $ return decodeXml(s)
  $ .toLowerCase()
  $ .replace(/&/g, 'and')
  $ .replace(/\+/g, 'plus')
  $ .replace(/\.us$/i, '')
  $ .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, ' ')
  $ .trim()
  $ }
  $ function channelName(block) {
  $ const display = block.match(/<display-name\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/display-name>/i)
  $ if (display) return decodeXml(display[1].replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim())
  $ return decodeXml(block.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim())
  $ }
  $ function iconFromCurated(block, attrs) {
  $ const iconMatch = block.match(/<icon\b[^>]*src="([^"]+)"/i)
  $ if (iconMatch) return decodeXml(iconMatch[1]).trim()
  $ const logoAttr = attr(attrs, 'logo')
  $ if (logoAttr) return logoAttr
  $ return ''
  $ }
  $ const curatedXml = fs.readFileSync(curatedPath, 'utf8')
  $ let guideXml = fs.readFileSync(guidePath, 'utf8')
  $ const logoById = new Map()
  $ const logoByName = new Map()
  $ for (const match of curatedXml.matchAll(/<channel\b([^>]*)>([\s\S]*?)<\/channel>/gi)) {
  $ const attrs = match[1]
  $ const block = match[0]
  $ const id = attr(attrs, 'xmltv_id') || attr(attrs, 'id')
  $ const name = channelName(block)
  $ const logo = iconFromCurated(block, attrs)
  $ if (!logo) continue
  $ if (id) logoById.set(id, logo)
  $ if (id) logoById.set(decodeXml(id).replace(/&amp;/g, '&'), logo)
  $ if (name) logoByName.set(normalize(name), logo)
  $ }
  $ let scanned = 0
  $ let matched = 0
  $ let alreadyCorrect = 0
  $ let added = 0
  $ let updated = 0
  $ let missing = 0
  $ guideXml = guideXml.replace(/<channel\b([^>]*)>([\s\S]*?)<\/channel>/gi, (block, attrs) => {
  $ scanned++
  $ const id = attr(attrs, 'id') || attr(attrs, 'xmltv_id')
  $ const name = channelName(block)
  $ const logo =
  $ logoById.get(id) ||
  $ logoById.get(decodeXml(id).replace(/&amp;/g, '&')) ||
  $ logoByName.get(normalize(name))
  $ if (!logo) {
  $ missing++
  $ return block
  $ }
  $ matched++
  $ const existingIcon = block.match(/<icon\b[^>]*src="([^"]+)"/i)
  $ if (existingIcon) {
  $ const existingLogo = decodeXml(existingIcon[1]).trim()
  $ if (existingLogo === logo) {
  $ alreadyCorrect++
  $ return block
  $ }
  $ updated++
  $ return block.replace(/<icon\b[^>]*src="[^"]*"[^/]*\/>/i, `<icon src="${escapeXml(logo)}"/>`)
  $ }
  $ added++
  $ return block.replace(/(<display-name\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/display-name>)/i, `$1<icon src="${escapeXml(logo)}"/>`)
  $ })
  $ fs.writeFileSync(guidePath, guideXml)
  $ console.log(`Generated TVPassport channels scanned: ${scanned}`)
  $ console.log(`Curated TVPassport logos available by id: ${logoById.size}`)
  $ console.log(`Curated TVPassport logos available by name: ${logoByName.size}`)
  $ console.log(`TVPassport channels matched to curated logos: ${matched}`)
  $ console.log(`TVPassport icons already correct: ${alreadyCorrect}`)
  $ console.log(`TVPassport icons added: ${added}`)
  $ console.log(`TVPassport icons updated: ${updated}`)
  $ console.log(`TVPassport channels still missing curated logo match: ${missing}`)
  $ if (scanned < 100) {
  $ console.error(`Expected at least 100 TVPassport channels, scanned ${scanned}`)
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ if (matched < 100) {
  $ console.error('Refusing to continue: expected at least 100 TVPassport channels to match curated logos')
  $ process.exit(1)
  $ }
  $ EOFJS

  $ echo "No TVPassport guide changes to commit"
